-- clean by zaki

do
	local UI_LIBRARY_URL = "https://pastefy.app/2pOQpGCr/raw"
	local Core = {
		Players = game:GetService("Players"),
		RunService = game:GetService("RunService"),
		Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace"),
		ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"),
		HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService"),
		TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService"),
		CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui"),
		TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService"),
	}
	_G.__SAE_MARK = "a"
	Core.LP = Core.Players.LocalPlayer
	Core._worldRoot = nil

	function Core.World()
		local worldRoot = Core._worldRoot
		if worldRoot and worldRoot.Parent then
			return worldRoot
		end
		Core._worldRoot = nil
		for _, name in ipairs({"World", "__OBJECTS"}) do
			local child = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
			if child and child:FindFirstChild("Areas") then
				Core._worldRoot = child
				return child
			end
		end
		for _, worldRoot2 in ipairs(Core.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if worldRoot2:IsA("Folder") then
				local areas = worldRoot2:FindFirstChild("Areas")
				if areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas") then
					Core._worldRoot = worldRoot2
					return worldRoot2
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	do
		local replicatedStorage = Core.ReplicatedStorage
		local EggState = require(replicatedStorage.Client.EggState)
		local PlotState = require(replicatedStorage.Client.PlotState)
		local Remotes = require(replicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
		local EggRecords = require(replicatedStorage.Shared.Util.EggRecords)
		local AssetItems = require(replicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AssetItems)
		local AssetEarnings = require(replicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AssetEarnings)
		do
			local areaDirectory = pcall(require, replicatedStorage.Data.Areas) and require(replicatedStorage.Data.Areas).Directory or {}
			Core.Assets = require(replicatedStorage.Data.Assets).Directory
			Core.TrailsDir = pcall(require, replicatedStorage.Data.Trails) and require(replicatedStorage.Data.Trails).Directory or {}
			Core.TreadmillsDir = pcall(require, replicatedStorage.Data.Treadmills) and require(replicatedStorage.Data.Treadmills).Directory or {}
			Core.GearsDir = pcall(require, replicatedStorage.Data.Gears) and require(replicatedStorage.Data.Gears).Directory or {}
			Core.TreadmillUtil = (function()
				for _, name in ipairs({"Util", "Utils", "Modules"}) do
					local child = replicatedStorage.Shared:FindFirstChild(name)
					local treadmillUtil = child and child:FindFirstChild("TreadmillUtil")
					if treadmillUtil then
						local ok, info = pcall(require, treadmillUtil)
						if ok and type(info) == "table" and type(info.SpeedPowerToWalkSpeed) == "function" then
							return info
						end
					end
				end
				for _, descendant in ipairs(replicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("ModuleScript") and descendant.Name == "TreadmillUtil" then
						local ok, info = pcall(require, descendant)
						if ok and type(info) == "table" and type(info.SpeedPowerToWalkSpeed) == "function" then
							return info
						end
					end
				end
				return nil
			end)()
			Core.AssetEarnings = AssetEarnings
			do
				local Save = require(replicatedStorage.Shared.Save)
				local readSave = Save.Get or Save.Peek
				if type(readSave) ~= "function" then
					warn("[WisHUB] modul Save tidak punya Get/Peek - data pemain dibaca kosong")
				end
				Core.Save = {
					raw = Save,
					Get = function()
						if type(readSave) == "function" then
							local ok, result = pcall(readSave)
							if ok and type(result) == "table" then
								return result
							end
						end
						return {}
					end,
				}
			end
			do
				local preferences
				for _, name in ipairs({"Preferences", "SettingsCmds"}) do
					local child = replicatedStorage.Shared:FindFirstChild(name)
					if child then
						local ok, result = pcall(require, child)
						if ok and type(result) == "table" then
							preferences = result
							break
						end
					end
				end
				local isPreferenceOn = preferences and (preferences.IsOn or preferences.Allows)
				local setPreference = preferences and (preferences.Set or preferences.Write)
				if not (isPreferenceOn and setPreference) then
					warn("[WisHUB] preferensi pemain tidak ketemu - fitur yang pakai itu dilewati")
				end
				Core.SettingsCmds = isPreferenceOn and setPreference and {
					IsEnabled = function(arg)
						local ok, result = pcall(isPreferenceOn, arg)
						return ok and result or false
					end,
					Set = function(arg, arg2)
						return pcall(setPreference, arg, arg2)
					end,
				} or nil
			end
			Core._snap, Core._snapAt, Core._snapTtl = nil, 0, 0.12

			function Core.readFieldCached()
				local now = os.clock()
				if Core._snap and now - Core._snapAt < Core._snapTtl then
					return Core._snap
				end
				Core._snap, Core._snapAt = EggState.ReadFieldEggs(), now
				return Core._snap
			end

			function Core.dropSnapCache()
				Core._snap = nil
			end

			pcall(function()
				EggState.CarryChanged:Connect(Core.dropSnapCache)
			end)
			Core.carriedUid = nil
			Core.carriedAt = 0
			pcall(function()
				EggState.CarryChanged:Connect(function(record)
					if type(record) ~= "table" then
						return
					end
					if record.IsCarrying == false then
						Core.carriedUid = nil
					elseif record.Uid ~= nil then
						Core.carriedUid = tostring(record.Uid)
						Core.carriedAt = os.clock()
					end
				end)
			end)
			Core.EggCmds = {
				GetAreaEggSnapshot = function()
					return Core.readFieldCached()
				end,
				GetAreaEggRecord = function(uid)
					return EggState.ReadFieldEgg(uid)
				end,
				RequestAreaEggSnapshot = function()
					Core.dropSnapCache()
					return EggState.SyncFieldEggs()
				end,
				GetOwnerRuntimeRecords = function(ownFor)
					local now = os.clock()
					if Core._own and Core._ownFor == ownFor and now - Core._ownAt < 0.5 then
						return Core._own
					end
					Core._own, Core._ownFor, Core._ownAt = EggState.ReadOwnerEggs(ownFor), ownFor, now
					return Core._own
				end,
				RequestCarryAreaEgg = function(uid)
					Core.dropSnapCache()
					local result, lastCarryDenial = EggState.CarryFieldEgg(uid)
					if result ~= true and type(lastCarryDenial) == "string" and lastCarryDenial ~= "" then
						Core.lastCarryDenial = lastCarryDenial
						Core.lastCarryDenialAt = os.clock()
						if lastCarryDenial:lower():find("gameplay area") then
							local now = os.clock()
							if now - (Core.entryDenyAt or 0) > 5 then
								Core.entryDenyRun = 0
							end
							Core.entryDenyAt = now
							Core.entryDenyRun = (Core.entryDenyRun or 0) + 1
							if Core.entryDenyRun >= 3 then
								Core.entryLost = true
								Core.entryDenyRun = 0
							end
						else
							Core.entryDenyRun = 0
						end
					end
					return result, lastCarryDenial
				end,
				RequestEquipTool = function(uid)
					return EggState.WearEggTool(uid)
				end,
				RequestPlaceEgg = function(uid, arg)
					Core._own = nil
					return EggState.PlantEgg(uid, arg)
				end,
				RequestHatchEgg = function(arg)
					Core._own = nil
					return EggState.BeginHatch(arg)
				end,
				RequestDropHeldAreaEgg = function(arg)
					Core.dropSnapCache()
					return EggState.DropFieldEgg(arg)
				end,
				RequestCompleteHatchEgg = function(arg)
					Core._own = nil
					return EggState.FinishHatch(arg)
				end,
				IsLocalEggReady = function(arg)
					return EggState.IsReadyToHatch(arg)
				end,
				AreaEggSnapshotUpdated = EggState.FieldRefreshed,
				AreaEggUpdated = EggState.FieldShifted,
				AreaEggRemoved = EggState.FieldGone,
				AreaEggCarryStateChanged = EggState.CarryChanged,
				AreaEggResetStartCountdown = EggState.ResetCountdown,
				AreaEggRareSpawnsPresented = EggState.RarityRevealed,
			}
			Core.PlotCmds = {
				GetPlotData = function(arg)
					return PlotState.ResolvePlot(arg)
				end,
			}
			do
				local function callRemote(remote, ...)
					if remote == nil then
						error("[shim] remote not mapped (removed feature)", 2)
					end
					if remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
						return remote:InvokeServer(...)
					end
					remote:FireServer(...)
					return true
				end

				Core.Network = {
					Invoke = function(remote, ...)
						return callRemote(remote, ...)
					end,
					Fire = function(remote, ...)
						if remote == nil then
							error("[shim] remote not mapped (removed feature)", 2)
						end
						remote:FireServer(...)
						return true
					end,
					Fired = function(ACTIVE_TREADMILL_EVENT)
						if ACTIVE_TREADMILL_EVENT == nil then
							error("[shim] remote not mapped (removed feature)", 2)
						end
						return ACTIVE_TREADMILL_EVENT.OnClientEvent
					end,
					NET_MAP = {
						Backpack = {EQUIP_BEST = Remotes.Haul.WearBest},
						OfflineAssets = {GET_SUMMARY = Remotes.AwayEarnings.FetchSummary, REQUEST_REDEEM = Remotes.AwayEarnings.AskCollect},
						ActiveAssets = {REQUEST_SELL = Remotes.PenRoster.AskSale, REQUEST_EQUIP = nil},
						AssetInventory = {SET_FAVORITE = Remotes.PetSatchel.WriteFavourite},
						FuseMachine = {
							COMPLETE_REVEAL = Remotes.Fusery.FinishReveal,
							ACKNOWLEDGE_INFO = Remotes.Fusery.ConfirmBriefing,
							INSERT_MOB = Remotes.Fusery.LoadPet,
							START_FUSE = Remotes.Fusery.BeginFuse,
						},
						Treadmills = {
							REQUEST_UNEQUIP = Remotes.Treadmill.AskDoff,
							REQUEST_ACTIVE_RENDER_SNAPSHOT = Remotes.Treadmill.AskRenderSnapshot,
							REQUEST_UPGRADE = Remotes.Treadmill.AskTierRaise,
							ACTIVE_TREADMILL_EVENT = Remotes.Treadmill.AssignedBeltShifted,
						},
						Plots = {REQUEST_BASE_UPGRADE = Remotes.Homestead.AskBaseTierRaise},
						Trails = {REQUEST_PURCHASE = Remotes.Trailwear.AskPurchase, REQUEST_SELECT = Remotes.Trailwear.AskChoose},
						GearShop = {REQUEST_PURCHASE = nil},
						Index = {REQUEST_CLAIM_ALL = Remotes.Codex.AskRedeemAll},
						GroupReward = {CLAIM_REWARD = Remotes.GroupPerk.RedeemPerk},
						Sakura = {
							REQUEST_HIT_TREE = Remotes.Bloomery.AskStrikeTree,
							REQUEST_INSERT_EGG = Remotes.Bloomery.AskLoadEgg,
							REQUEST_DEPOSIT = Remotes.Bloomery.AskMutate,
						},
						Gifting = {GIFT_REQUEST = nil, RESPONSE = nil},
					},
				}
			end
			Core.AssetSer = {
				Deserialize = function(arg)
					return AssetItems.Decode(arg)
				end,
			}
			Core.AssetItemUtil = AssetItems

			local function getAssetCategory(info)
				return info and (info.AssetCategory or info.Category)
			end

			local function getAssetScale(info)
				return info and (info.AssetScale or info.Scale) or 1
			end

			local function getEggDisplayName(arg)
				local assets = Core.Assets and Core.Assets[arg]
				local id = assets and assets.Rarity and assets.Rarity._id
				for _, player in pairs(areaDirectory) do
					for _, map in ipairs(player.DropTable or {}) do
						if map[1] == arg or map[1] == id then
							return (player.DisplayName or "Egg") .. " Egg"
						end
					end
				end
				return "Egg"
			end

			Core.EggItemUtil = {
				GetWeightKg = function(arg)
					return EggRecords.WeightKgForScale(getAssetCategory(arg), getAssetScale(arg))
				end,
				GetSellPrice = function(arg)
					return AssetItems.SalePrice({Category = getAssetCategory(arg), Scale = getAssetScale(arg), Mutations = {}}) * 0.8
				end,
				GetDisplayNameWithWeight = function(arg)
					local value = EggRecords.WeightKgForScale(getAssetCategory(arg), getAssetScale(arg))
					local maxValue = math.max(math.round(value * 100) / 100, 0)
					return string.format("%s (%sKg)", getEggDisplayName(getAssetCategory(arg)), tostring(maxValue))
				end,
			}
		end
		Core.AssetGenerationUtil = {
			ComputeVolumeGeneration = function()
				return 0
			end,
			GetBaseRateMutationOnly = function(info)
				if not info then
					return 0
				end
				local ok, result = pcall(AssetEarnings.LiveRatePerSecond, {
					Category = info.Category or info.AssetCategory,
					Scale = tonumber(info.Scale or info.AssetScale) or 1,
					AssetScale = tonumber(info.AssetScale or info.Scale) or 1,
					Mutations = info.Mutations or {},
					BaseMutation = info.BaseMutation,
				})
				return ok and tonumber(result) or 0
			end,
			GetRate = function(info)
				if not info then
					return 0
				end
				local ok, result = pcall(AssetEarnings.LiveRatePerSecond, {
					Category = info.Category or info.AssetCategory,
					Scale = tonumber(info.Scale or info.AssetScale) or 1,
					AssetScale = tonumber(info.AssetScale or info.Scale) or 1,
					Mutations = info.Mutations or {},
					BaseMutation = info.BaseMutation,
				})
				return ok and tonumber(result) or 0
			end,
		}
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		local raw = rawget(_G, "getgc") or rawget(_G, "get_gc_objects") or type(getgc) == "function" and getgc
		local raw2 = type(debug) == "table" and debug.getconstants or rawget(_G, "getconstants")
		local raw3 = type(debug) == "table" and debug.getstack or rawget(_G, "getstack")
		local raw4 = type(debug) == "table" and debug.setstack or rawget(_G, "setstack")
		local raw5 = rawget(_G, "hookfunction") or rawget(_G, "hookfunc") or type(hookfunction) == "function" and hookfunction or type(hookfunc) == "function" and hookfunc
		local fn = type(islclosure) == "function" and islclosure or function()
			return true
		end
		local raw6 = rawget(_G, "isexecutorclosure") or rawget(_G, "isourclosure") or type(isexecutorclosure) == "function" and isexecutorclosure or function()
			return false
		end
		local fn2 = type(newcclosure) == "function" and newcclosure or function(arg)
			return arg
		end
		if not (raw and raw2 and raw5) then
			local items = {}
			if not raw then
				table.insert(items, "getgc")
			end
			if not raw2 then
				table.insert(items, "debug.getconstants")
			end
			if not raw5 then
				table.insert(items, "hookfunction")
			end
			warn("[WisHUB] BAC suppression inactive - missing: " .. table.concat(items, ", "))
			_G.__SAE_BAC_OK = false
			return
		end
		_G.__SAE_BAC_OK = true

		local function fn3(arg, raw5, callback, map, raw3, raw4)
			local fn
			local ok = pcall(function()
				fn = raw5(arg, callback(function(...)
					if raw3 and raw4 then
						for i = 1, 3 do
							local ok, list = pcall(raw3, i)
							if ok and type(list) == "table" then
								for key, item in pairs(list) do
									if type(item) == "string" and map[item] then
										pcall(raw4, i, key, nil)
									end
								end
								break
							end
						end
					end
					return fn(...)
				end))
			end)
			return ok and fn ~= nil
		end

		local map = {
			["X-16"] = true,
			["Character integrity sampling was intercepted"] = true,
			["Character integrity evaluator returned an invalid completion receipt"] = true,
			["Character integrity movement evaluation failed"] = true,
			["Character integrity execution was interrupted"] = true,
			["Invalid character state mutation scope"] = true,
			["Invalid ground-contact mutation scope"] = true,
		}
		local value = 0
		for _, value2 in pairs(raw(true)) do
			value = value + 1
			if value >= 300 then
				value = 0
				task.wait()
			end
			if type(value2) == "function" and fn(value2) and not raw6(value2) then
				local ok, list = pcall(raw2, value2)
				if ok and type(list) == "table" then
					local flag = false
					for _, item in pairs(list) do
						if type(item) == "string" and map[item] then
							flag = true
							break
						end
					end
					if flag then
						fn3(value2, raw5, fn2, map, raw3, raw4)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		pcall(function()
			local info
			if info and not info.__wishubViolHooked then
				info.__wishubViolHooked = true
				local map = {
					["ClientCharacter: IntegrityViolation"] = true,
					["ClientCharacter: CorrectionStarted"] = true,
					["ClientCharacter: RequestCharacterReset"] = true,
				}
				local fire = info.Fire
				if type(fire) == "function" then
					function info.Fire(arg, ...)
						if type(arg) == "string" and map[arg] then
							return
						end
						return fire(arg, ...)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end)
	local DEFAULT_FPS_CAP = 750
	local activeFpsCap

	local function setCap(arg)
		local number = tonumber(arg)
		if not number then
			return false
		end
		number = math.clamp(math.floor(number), 1, 999)
		local raw = rawget(_G, "setfpscap") or rawget(_G, "set_fps_cap") or type(setfpscap) == "function" and setfpscap
		if not raw then
			return false
		end
		local ok = pcall(raw, number)
		if ok then
			activeFpsCap = number
		end
		return ok
	end

	local function uncap()
		return setCap(999)
	end

	local function getActiveCap()
		return activeFpsCap
	end

	local settingsOverride = {on = false, conns = {}, old = {}}

	local function clearClientRenderedAssets()
		local clientRenderedAssets = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("ClientRenderedAssets")
		if clientRenderedAssets then
			for _, child in ipairs(clientRenderedAssets:GetChildren()) do
				pcall(function()
					child:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	local function clearPlacedEggRenders()
		local placedEggRenders = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlacedEggRenders")
		if not placedEggRenders then
			return
		end
		for _, child in ipairs(placedEggRenders:GetChildren()) do
			pcall(function()
				if not child:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	local function clearPlayerTraps()
		local DEBRIS = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__DEBRIS")
		if not DEBRIS then
			return
		end
		for _, child in ipairs(DEBRIS:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "PlayerTrap" then
				pcall(function()
					child:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	local Hider = {plotConns = {}, eggConns = {}}

	function Hider.folders(arg)
		local results = {}
		for _, child in ipairs(Core.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == arg then
				table.insert(results, child)
			end
		end
		return results
	end

	function Hider.dropPet(instance)
		pcall(function()
			instance:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	function Hider.dropOtherEgg(instance)
		local text = tostring(Core.LP.UserId) .. "_"
		if tostring(instance.Name):sub(1, #text) == text then
			return
		end
		if instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") then
			return
		end
		pcall(function()
			instance:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	function Hider.setOwnerPlot(hideOwnerPlot)
		for _, connection in ipairs(Hider.plotConns) do
			pcall(function()
				connection:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		Hider.plotConns = {}
		if not hideOwnerPlot then
			return
		end
		for _, instance in ipairs(Hider.folders("ClientRenderedAssets")) do
			for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
				Hider.dropPet(child)
			end
			table.insert(Hider.plotConns, instance.ChildAdded:Connect(Hider.dropPet))
		end
		table.insert(Hider.plotConns, Core.Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
			if instance.Name == "ClientRenderedAssets" then
				for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
					Hider.dropPet(child)
				end
				table.insert(Hider.plotConns, instance.ChildAdded:Connect(Hider.dropPet))
			end
		end))
	end

	function Hider.setOtherEggs(hideOtherEggs)
		for _, connection in ipairs(Hider.eggConns) do
			pcall(function()
				connection:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		Hider.eggConns = {}
		if not hideOtherEggs then
			return
		end
		for _, instance in ipairs(Hider.folders("PlacedEggRenders")) do
			for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
				Hider.dropOtherEgg(child)
			end
			table.insert(Hider.eggConns, instance.ChildAdded:Connect(Hider.dropOtherEgg))
		end
		table.insert(Hider.eggConns, Core.Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
			if instance.Name == "PlacedEggRenders" then
				for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
					Hider.dropOtherEgg(child)
				end
				table.insert(Hider.eggConns, instance.ChildAdded:Connect(Hider.dropOtherEgg))
			end
		end))
	end

	local function stopScaryEffects()
		pcall(function()
			local client = Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Client")
			local scaryEffectController = client and client:FindFirstChild("ScaryEffectController")
			if scaryEffectController then
				local module = require(scaryEffectController)
				if module and module.Stop then
					module:Stop()
				end
			end
		end)
		local playerGui = Core.LP and Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
		if playerGui then
			local vignette = playerGui:FindFirstChild("Vignette")
			if vignette then
				pcall(function()
					vignette:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	local function silenceEffects()
		local function stop(instance)
			pcall(function()
				if instance:IsA("Sound") then
					instance.PlayOnRemove = false
					instance:Stop()
				else
					instance.Enabled = false
				end
			end)
		end

		local connection = game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
			if instance:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or instance:IsA("Trail") or instance:IsA("Beam") or instance:IsA("Sparkles") or instance:IsA("Fire") or instance:IsA("Smoke") then
				stop(instance)
			end
		end)
		local connection2 = Core.Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
			if instance.Name == "ClientRenderedAssets" then
				clearClientRenderedAssets()
				instance.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
					pcall(function()
						instance:Destroy()
					end)
				end)
			elseif instance.Name == "PlacedEggRenders" then
				clearPlacedEggRenders()
				instance.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
					pcall(function()
						if not instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") then
							instance:Destroy()
						end
					end)
				end)
			end
		end)
		local connection3
		local clientRenderedAssets = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("ClientRenderedAssets")
		if clientRenderedAssets then
			connection3 = clientRenderedAssets.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
				pcall(function()
					instance:Destroy()
				end)
			end)
		end
		local connection4
		local placedEggRenders = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("PlacedEggRenders")
		if placedEggRenders then
			connection4 = placedEggRenders.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
				pcall(function()
					if not instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt") then
						instance:Destroy()
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
		local connection5
		local runBackEffects = Core.LP and Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and Core.LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("RunBackEffects")
		if runBackEffects then
			pcall(function()
				runBackEffects.Enabled = false
			end)
			connection5 = runBackEffects.Changed:Connect(function(name)
				if name == "Enabled" and runBackEffects.Enabled ~= false then
					pcall(function()
						runBackEffects.Enabled = false
					end)
				end
			end)
		end
		local connection6
		local playerGui = Core.LP and Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
		if playerGui then
			connection6 = playerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
				if instance.Name == "Vignette" then
					pcall(function()
						instance:Destroy()
					end)
				end
			end)
		end
		settingsOverride.conns = {connection, connection2, connection3, connection5, connection6, connection4}
	end

	local function apply()
		settingsOverride.on = true
		setCap(120)
		pcall(function()
			local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
			Lighting.Technology = Enum.Technology.Compatibility
			Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
			Lighting.Brightness = 2
			Lighting.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		end)
		pcall(function()
			for _, descendant in ipairs(Core.Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					pcall(function()
						descendant.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
					end)
					pcall(function()
						descendant.Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 120)
					end)
					pcall(function()
						descendant.CastShadow = false
					end)
				elseif descendant:IsA("Decal") or descendant:IsA("Texture") then
					pcall(function()
						descendant.Texture = ""
					end)
				elseif descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Fire") or descendant:IsA("Smoke") or descendant:IsA("Sparkles") then
					pcall(function()
						descendant.Enabled = false
					end)
				elseif descendant:IsA("Trail") or descendant:IsA("Beam") then
					pcall(function()
						descendant.Enabled = false
					end)
				elseif descendant:IsA("Sound") then
					pcall(function()
						descendant.PlayOnRemove = false
						descendant:Stop()
					end)
				end
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			local playerGui = Core.Players.LocalPlayer and Core.Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
			if playerGui then
				local tbl = {__SAE_Panel = true, __SAE_EggLogs = true, __SAE_PlacePanel = true, WisHUBRuntime = true}
				for _, child in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
					local flag = tbl[child.Name] == true or child:FindFirstChild("DropShadowHolder") ~= nil
					if not flag then
						for _, descendant in ipairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("GuiObject") then
								pcall(function()
									descendant.BackgroundTransparency = 1
								end)
								pcall(function()
									descendant.BorderSizePixel = 0
								end)
							end
							if descendant:IsA("ImageLabel") or descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
								pcall(function()
									descendant.Image = ""
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		if Core.SettingsCmds then
			for _, name in ipairs({"HideOtherPets", "SFX", "Music", "DisableVideos"}) do
				local isEnabled = Core.SettingsCmds.IsEnabled(name)
				settingsOverride.old[name] = isEnabled
				pcall(function()
					Core.SettingsCmds.Set(name, name == "HideOtherPets")
				end)
			end
		end
		clearClientRenderedAssets()
		clearPlacedEggRenders()
		stopScaryEffects()
		silenceEffects()
	end

	local function restore()
		if not settingsOverride.on then
			return
		end
		settingsOverride.on = false
		for _, connection in ipairs(settingsOverride.conns) do
			if connection then
				pcall(function()
					connection:Disconnect()
				end)
			end
		end
		settingsOverride.conns = {}
		if Core.SettingsCmds then
			for key, value in pairs(settingsOverride.old) do
				pcall(function()
					Core.SettingsCmds.Set(key, value)
				end)
			end
		end
		pcall(function()
			local runBackEffects = Core.LP and Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and Core.LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("RunBackEffects")
			if runBackEffects then
				runBackEffects.Enabled = true
			end
		end)
	end

	pcall(function()
		Core.Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
			if instance.Name == "__DEBRIS" then
				instance.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
					if instance.Name == "PlayerTrap" then
						instance:Destroy()
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
		local DEBRIS = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("__DEBRIS")
		if DEBRIS then
			DEBRIS.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
				if instance.Name == "PlayerTrap" then
					instance:Destroy()
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	_G.__SAE_FPS = {apply = apply, restore = restore}

	_G.__SAE_MARK = "b"
	if rawget(_G, "WISHUB_DEBUG") then
		_G.__SAE_ST = SAE_ST

	_G.__SAE_GEN = (_G.__SAE_GEN or 0) + 1
	SAE_ST.gen = _G.__SAE_GEN

	function SAE_ST.stale()
		return _G.__SAE_GEN ~= SAE_ST.gen
	end

	pcall(function()
		local list = {Core.LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")}
		if gethui then
			table.insert(list, gethui())
		end
		pcall(function()
			table.insert(list, Core.CoreGui)
		end)
		for _, item in ipairs(list) do
			if item then
				for _, child in ipairs(item:GetChildren()) do
					local flag = child.Name == "WisHUBRuntime" or child.Name == "__SAE_Panel" or child.Name == "__SAE_EggLogs" or child.Name == "__SAE_PlacePanel" or child:GetAttribute("__WisHubOwned") == true
					if child:IsA("ScreenGui") and flag then
						pcall(function()
							child:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
	_G.__SAE_Gui = nil
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
	pcall(function()
		game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Health, false)
	end)
	do
		local autoReposition = {enabled = true}
		task.spawn(function()
			local humanoid, humanoidRootPart, vector2
			while not SAE_ST.stale() do
				task.wait(20)
				if autoReposition.enabled and not SAE_ST.busy and not SAE_ST.repositioning and not SAE_ST.tpSteal then
					local character = localPlayer.Character
					humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
					humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if humanoid and humanoidRootPart and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
						vector2 = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4, 0, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4)
						pcall(function()
							humanoid:MoveTo(humanoidRootPart.Position + vector2)
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	local RECARRY_MIN_DISTANCE = 61
	local RECARRY_COOLDOWN = 0.1
	local immunityState = {
		installed = false,
		ragdollOrig = {},
		lastEggUid = nil,
		lastRecarry = 0,
		lastScan = 0,
		healthConns = {},
		stateConns = {},
		lastSafePos = nil,
		lostAt = nil,
		origFallenHeight = nil,
		namecallHooked = false,
		manualDropAt = 0,
		touchOff = {},
	}
	local DISABLED_HUMANOID_STATES = {
		Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding,
		Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics,
		Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,
		Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead,
		Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown,
		Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll,
	}
	local RAGDOLL_STATES = {
		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics] = true,
		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = true,
		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = true,
		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding] = true,
		[Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated] = true,
	}

	local function watchHumanoidHealth(humanoid)
		if not humanoid or immunityState.healthConns[humanoid] then
			return
		end
		local items = {}
		immunityState.healthConns[humanoid] = items

		local function fn()
			if not humanoid.Parent then
				for _, connection in ipairs(items) do
					pcall(function()
						connection:Disconnect()
					end)
				end
				immunityState.healthConns[humanoid] = nil
				return
			end
			if not SAE_ST.immunity then
				return
			end
			if humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
				humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
			end
		end

		table.insert(items, humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(fn))
		table.insert(items, Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(fn))
	end

	local function watchHumanoidState(humanoid)
		if not humanoid or immunityState.stateConns[humanoid] then
			return
		end
		immunityState.stateConns[humanoid] = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, arg)
			if not SAE_ST.immunity or not RAGDOLL_STATES[arg] then
				return
			end
			if not humanoid.Parent then
				return
			end
			pcall(function()
				humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
			end)
			pcall(function()
				humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end)
		end)
	end

	local function applyStateImmunity(humanoid, immunity)
		if not humanoid then
			return
		end
		pcall(function()
			for _, value in ipairs(DISABLED_HUMANOID_STATES) do
				humanoid:SetStateEnabled(value, not immunity)
			end
			humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = not immunity
			if immunity then
				humanoid.PlatformStand = false
				humanoid.Sit = false
				if humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
					humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
				end
			end
		end)
		if immunity then
			watchHumanoidHealth(humanoid)
			watchHumanoidState(humanoid)
		end
	end

	local function toggleRagdollConnections(immunity)
		local raw = rawget(_G, "getconnections") or type(getconnections) == "function" and getconnections
		if type(raw) ~= "function" then
			return
		end
		local items = {}
		pcall(function()
			local network = Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Network")
			if not network then
				return
			end
			for _, name in ipairs({"Guards: SpeedHitOffer", "Guards: SpeedHitWarning", "Guards: WakeUp"}) do
				local child = network:FindFirstChild(name)
				if child then
					table.insert(items, child)
				end
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			local Ragdoll = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules.Ragdoll)
			if type(Ragdoll) == "table" and typeof(Ragdoll.ClientRagdollRemote) == "Instance" then
				table.insert(items, Ragdoll.ClientRagdollRemote)
			end
		end)
		for _, remote in ipairs(items) do
			pcall(function()
				for _, instance in ipairs(raw(remote.OnClientEvent)) do
					if immunity then
						if instance.Disable then
							instance:Disable()
						end
					elseif instance.Enable then
						instance:Enable()
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		pcall(function()
			local areas = Core.World()
			areas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("Areas")
			areas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
			if not areas then
				return
			end
			if immunity then
				for _, child in ipairs(areas:GetChildren()) do
					local guard = child:FindFirstChild("Guard")
					for _, part in ipairs(guard and guard:GetDescendants() or {}) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanTouch then
							immunityState.touchOff[part] = true
							part.CanTouch = false
						end
					end
				end
			else
				for instance in pairs(immunityState.touchOff) do
					if instance and instance.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							instance.CanTouch = true
						end)
					end
				end
				immunityState.touchOff = {}
			end
		end)
	end

	local function hasAreaEggRecord(lastEggUid2)
		local areaEggRecord
		pcall(function()
			areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(lastEggUid2)
		end)
		if not areaEggRecord then
			return false
		end
		if areaEggRecord.State == "Claimed" then
			return false
		end
		if areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId and areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId ~= Core.LP.UserId then
			return false
		end
		return true
	end

	local function installImmunity()
		if immunityState.installed then
			return
		end
		immunityState.installed = true
		pcall(function()
			local Ragdoll = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules.Ragdoll)
			if type(Ragdoll) ~= "table" then
				return
			end
			for _, name in ipairs({"ApplyClientRagdoll", "Ragdoll", "TimedRagdoll", "TimedRagdollAsync", "NpcRagdoll"}) do
				local fn = Ragdoll[name]
				if type(fn) == "function" and not immunityState.ragdollOrig[name] then
					immunityState.ragdollOrig[name] = fn
					Ragdoll[name] = function(...)
						if SAE_ST.immunity then
							return
						end
						return fn(...)
					end
				end
			end
			local isRagdolled = Ragdoll.IsRagdolled
			if type(isRagdolled) == "function" and not immunityState.ragdollOrig.IsRagdolled then
				immunityState.ragdollOrig.IsRagdolled = isRagdolled

				function Ragdoll.IsRagdolled(...)
					if SAE_ST.immunity then
						return false
					end
					return isRagdolled(...)
				end
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			local network = Core.Network
			if not network or network.__immHooked then
				return
			end
			network.__immHooked = true
			local fire = network.Fire
			if type(fire) == "function" then
				function network.Fire(name, ...)
					if SAE_ST.immunity then
						local text = tostring(name)
						if (text:find("ForestHit") or text:find("SpeedHit")) and not text:find("Deposit") then
							return
						end
						if text == "ClientCharacter: RequestCharacterReset" or text == "ClientCharacter: CorrectionStarted" then
							return
						end
					end
					return fire(name, ...)
				end
			end
			local invoke = network.Invoke
			if type(invoke) == "function" then
				function network.Invoke(name, info, ...)
					if SAE_ST.immunity and tostring(name):find("RequestAreaEggDrop") then
						if type(info) == "table" and info.Reason and info.Reason ~= "PlayerRequest" then
							return false, "Blocked guard drop"
						end
					end
					return invoke(name, info, ...)
				end
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			local eggCmds = Core.EggCmds
			if not eggCmds or eggCmds.__immHooked then
				return
			end
			eggCmds.__immHooked = true
			local requestDropHeldAreaEgg = eggCmds.RequestDropHeldAreaEgg
			if type(requestDropHeldAreaEgg) == "function" then
				function eggCmds.RequestDropHeldAreaEgg(name, ...)
					if SAE_ST.immunity and name ~= nil and name ~= "PlayerRequest" then
						return false, "Blocked guard drop"
					end
					if name == nil or name == "PlayerRequest" then
						immunityState.manualDropAt = os.clock()
						immunityState.lastEggUid = nil
					end
					return requestDropHeldAreaEgg(name, ...)
				end
			end
		end)
		Core.RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				return
			end
			if not SAE_ST.immunity then
				return
			end
			local character = Core.LP.Character
			if not character then
				return 
			end
			local now = os.clock()
			if now - immunityState.lastScan >= 0.1 then
				immunityState.lastScan = now
				local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if humanoid then
					for _, value in ipairs(DISABLED_HUMANOID_STATES) do
						if humanoid:GetStateEnabled(value) then
							humanoid:SetStateEnabled(value, false)
						end
					end
					if humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath then
						humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
					end
					if humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
						humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
					end
				end
				for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
						if not descendant.Enabled then
							descendant.Enabled = true
						end
					elseif descendant:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or descendant:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
						if descendant.Name:find("Ragdoll") or descendant:GetAttribute("RagdollConstraint") then
							pcall(function()
								descendant:Destroy()
							end)
						end
					elseif descendant:IsA("Attachment") and (descendant.Name:find("Ragdoll") or descendant:GetAttribute("RagdollAttachment")) then
						pcall(function()
							descendant:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end
			end
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not humanoid then
				return
			end
			if humanoid.Sit then
				humanoid.Sit = false
			end
			if humanoid.PlatformStand then
				humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			end
			local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if humanoidRootPart then
				local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				if math.abs(assemblyLinearVelocity.Y) > 6 then
					humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(assemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, assemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
				end
			end
			pcall(function()
				for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					local animationId = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId or ""
					if animationId:find("122257458498464") then
						track:Stop(0)
					end
				end
			end)
			local state = humanoid:GetState()
			if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated then
				pcall(function()
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				end)
				pcall(function()
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end)
			end
		end)
		Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				return
			end
			if os.clock() - (immunityState.lastLook or 0) < 0.1 then
				return
			end
			immunityState.lastLook = os.clock()
			if not SAE_ST.immunity then
				immunityState.lastEggUid = nil
				immunityState.lostAt = nil
				return
			end
			local lastEggUid
			pcall(function()
				local areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
				for _, record in ipairs(areaEggSnapshot and areaEggSnapshot.Records or {}) do
					if record.State == "Carried" and record.CarrierUserId == Core.LP.UserId then
						lastEggUid = record.Uid
						break
					end
				end
			end)
			if lastEggUid then
				immunityState.lastEggUid = lastEggUid
				immunityState.lostAt = nil
				return
			end
			local lastEggUid2 = immunityState.lastEggUid
			if not lastEggUid2 then
				return
			end
			if os.clock() - (immunityState.manualDropAt or 0) < 1.5 then
				return
			end
			if not hasAreaEggRecord(lastEggUid2) then
				immunityState.lastEggUid = nil
				immunityState.lostAt = nil
				return
			end
			local now = os.clock()
			immunityState.lostAt = immunityState.lostAt or now
			if now - immunityState.lastRecarry < RECARRY_COOLDOWN then
				return
			end
			immunityState.lastRecarry = now
			local flag = false
			pcall(function()
				local areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(lastEggUid2)
				local boundsCFrame = areaEggRecord and (areaEggRecord.BottomCFrame or areaEggRecord.BoundsCFrame)
				local instance = SAE_ST.hrp()
				if boundsCFrame and instance then
					flag = (boundsCFrame.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude <= 60
				end
			end)
			if not flag then
				immunityState.lastEggUid = nil
				immunityState.lostAt = nil
				return
			end
			pcall(function()
				Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(lastEggUid2)
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local areaEggSlotsClient = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient")
				local child = areaEggSlotsClient and areaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(tostring(lastEggUid2))
				local carryAreaEgg = child and (child:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt", true) or child:FindFirstChild("CarryAreaEgg", true))
				if carryAreaEgg then
					carryAreaEgg.Enabled = true
					carryAreaEgg.HoldDuration = 0
					carryAreaEgg.RequiresLineOfSight = false
					carryAreaEgg.MaxActivationDistance = 9999
					if fireproximityprompt then
						fireproximityprompt(carryAreaEgg)
					end
				end
			end)
			if SAE_ST.busy or SAE_ST.repositioning then
				return
			end
			if now - (immunityState.lostAt or now) > 4 then
				return
			end
			pcall(function()
				local areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(lastEggUid2)
				local boundsCFrame = areaEggRecord and (areaEggRecord.BottomCFrame or areaEggRecord.BoundsCFrame)
				if not boundsCFrame then
					return
				end
				local character = Core.LP.Character
				local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart then
					return
				end
				local value = boundsCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
				local distance = (humanoidRootPart.Position - value).Magnitude
				if distance > RECARRY_MIN_DISTANCE and distance <= 250 then
					SAE_ST.busy = true
					task.spawn(function()
						pcall(function()
							SAE_ST.flyTo(CFrame.new(value), Config.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed, false)
						end)
						SAE_ST.busy = false
					end)
				end
			end)
		end)
		Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				return
			end
			if not SAE_ST.immunity then
				return
			end
			local character = Core.LP.Character
			local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart then
				return
			end
			local y = humanoidRootPart.Position.Y
			if y < -150 then
				local lastSafePos = immunityState.lastSafePos
				if lastSafePos then
					humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(lastSafePos)
					humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
			elseif y > 0 then
				immunityState.lastSafePos = humanoidRootPart.Position
			end
		end)
		localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(instance)
			local humanoid = instance:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
			if not humanoid then
				return
			end
			humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
				if not SAE_ST.immunity then
					return
				end
				pcall(function()
					humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
					applyStateImmunity(humanoid, true)
				end)
			end)
		end)
		localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(instance)
			immunityState.lastEggUid = nil
			if not SAE_ST.immunity then
				return
			end
			local humanoid = instance:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
			if humanoid then
				applyStateImmunity(humanoid, true)
			end
			pcall(function()
				toggleRagdollConnections(true)
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local Ragdoll = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules.Ragdoll)
				if type(Ragdoll.ClearClientRagdoll) == "function" then
					Ragdoll.ClearClientRagdoll(instance)
				end
			end)
		end)
	end

	local function setImmunity(arg)
		SAE_ST.immunity = arg and true or false
		SAE_ST.immunityWanted = SAE_ST.immunity
		if SAE_ST.immunity then
			installImmunity()
		end
		if not immunityState.installed then
			return
		end
		local character = Core.LP.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		applyStateImmunity(humanoid, SAE_ST.immunity)
		toggleRagdollConnections(SAE_ST.immunity)
		if SAE_ST.immunity then
			pcall(function()
				SAE_ST.armSpoof()
			end)
		end
		pcall(function()
			if SAE_ST.immunity then
				if immunityState.origFallenHeight == nil then
					immunityState.origFallenHeight = Core.Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
				end
				Core.Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
			elseif immunityState.origFallenHeight ~= nil then
				Core.Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = immunityState.origFallenHeight
				immunityState.origFallenHeight = nil
			end
		end)
		if not SAE_ST.immunity then
			immunityState.lastEggUid = nil
		end
	end

	SAE_ST.setImmunity = setImmunity
	SAE_ST.stealGuards = {resetHooked = false, touchOff = {}, healthConn = nil, respawnConn = nil}
	SAE_ST._spoofJumpConn = nil

	function SAE_ST.setupSpoof(instance)
		if not instance or not instance.Parent then
			return
		end
		local parent = instance.Parent
		if parent:GetAttribute("__spoofed") then
			return
		end
		parent:SetAttribute("__spoofed", true)
		local clone = instance:Clone()
		clone.Name = "Humanoid"
		clone.Parent = parent
		instance:Destroy()
		local currentCamera = Core.Workspace.CurrentCamera
		if currentCamera then
			currentCamera.CameraSubject = clone
		end
		local animate = parent:FindFirstChild("Animate")
		if animate then
			local clone2 = animate:Clone()
			animate:Destroy()
			clone2.Parent = parent
		end
		pcall(function()
			clone:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
			clone:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
			clone:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
			clone:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
			clone:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, true)
			clone:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
			clone:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running, true)
			clone.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
			clone.RequiresNeck = false
			if clone.JumpHeight == 0 and clone.JumpPower == 0 then
				clone.JumpPower = 50
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			local playerScripts = Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
			local playerModule = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
			if playerModule then
				local module = require(playerModule)
				local controls = module and module.GetControls and module:GetControls()
				if controls and controls.OnCharacterAdded then
					controls:OnCharacterAdded(parent)
				end
			end
		end)

		local function changeState()
			if not (clone and clone.Parent and clone.Health > 0) then
				return
			end
			pcall(function()
				local primaryPart = parent:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or parent.PrimaryPart
				if primaryPart then
					primaryPart.Anchored = false
				end
				task.spawn(function()
					pcall(function()
						local Remotes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
						if Remotes and Remotes.Treadmill and Remotes.Treadmill.AskDoff then
							Remotes.Treadmill.AskDoff:InvokeServer()
						end
					end)
				end)
				clone.Jump = true
				clone:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			end)
		end

		if SAE_ST._spoofJumpConn then
			pcall(function()
				SAE_ST._spoofJumpConn:Disconnect()
			end)
			SAE_ST._spoofJumpConn = nil
		end
		local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
		SAE_ST._spoofJumpConn = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(changeState)
		UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, _)
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
				changeState()
			end
		end)
		clone.HealthChanged:Connect(function(amount)
			if amount <= 0 then
				clone.Health = 100
				clone:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end
		end)
	end

	SAE_ST.spoofConn = nil

	function SAE_ST.armSpoof()
		local character = Core.LP.Character
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid then
			pcall(SAE_ST.setupSpoof, humanoid)
		end
		if not SAE_ST.spoofConn then
			SAE_ST.spoofConn = Core.LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(instance)
				if SAE_ST.stale() then
					return
				end
				local humanoid = instance:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
				if humanoid then
					pcall(SAE_ST.setupSpoof, humanoid)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	function SAE_ST.stealGuards.touchGuards(arg)
		if arg then
			for _, descendant in ipairs(Core.Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.CanTouch then
					local lowered = descendant.Name:lower()
					local lowered2 = descendant.Parent and descendant.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
					if lowered:find("guard") or lowered2:find("guard") then
						SAE_ST.stealGuards.touchOff[descendant] = true
						descendant.CanTouch = false
					end
				end
			end
		else
			for instance in pairs(SAE_ST.stealGuards.touchOff) do
				if instance and instance.Parent then
					pcall(function()
						instance.CanTouch = true
					end)
				end
			end
			SAE_ST.stealGuards.touchOff = {}
		end
	end

	function SAE_ST.stealGuards.hookReset() end

	function SAE_ST.stealGuards.armHumanoid(humanoid)
		if not humanoid then
			return
		end
		pcall(function()
			humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
			humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
			humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
		end)
		if SAE_ST.stealGuards.healthConn then
			pcall(function()
				SAE_ST.stealGuards.healthConn:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		SAE_ST.stealGuards.healthConn = humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(amount)
			if SAE_ST.autoSteal and amount <= 0 and humanoid.Parent then
				humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
			end
		end)
	end

	function SAE_ST.stealGuards.apply(arg)
		if arg then
			SAE_ST.stealGuards.touchGuards(true)
			SAE_ST.stealGuards.hookReset()
			local character = Core.LP.Character
			SAE_ST.stealGuards.armHumanoid(character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
			if not SAE_ST.stealGuards.respawnConn then
				SAE_ST.stealGuards.respawnConn = Core.LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(instance)
					if not SAE_ST.autoSteal then
						return
					end
					local humanoid = instance:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
					SAE_ST.stealGuards.armHumanoid(humanoid)
					SAE_ST.stealGuards.touchGuards(true)
				end)
			end
		else
			SAE_ST.stealGuards.touchGuards(false)
			if SAE_ST.stealGuards.healthConn then
				pcall(function()
					SAE_ST.stealGuards.healthConn:Disconnect()
				end)
				SAE_ST.stealGuards.healthConn = nil
			end
		end
	end

	local function hasWallStartVisual()
		local wallStartVisual = Core.World() and Core.World().Areas and Core.World().Areas:FindFirstChild("WallStartVisual")
		return wallStartVisual ~= nil
	end

	local function getCharacter()
		return Core.LP.Character
	end

	local function hrp()
		local character = getCharacter()
		return character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end

	SAE_ST.hrp = hrp

	local function isAlive()
		local character = getCharacter()
		if not character then
			return false
		end
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid then
			return false
		end
		if humanoid.Health > 0 then
			return true
		end
		if humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
			return false
		end
		return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") ~= nil
	end

	local function requestStreamAround(position, arg)
		if not position then
			return
		end
		pcall(function()
			if Core.LP.RequestStreamAroundAsync then
				Core.LP:RequestStreamAroundAsync(position, arg or 2)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function raycastGround(position)
		local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
		rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		local filterDescendantsInstances = {getCharacter()}
		local guardAreas = Core.World() and Core.World().Areas and Core.World().Areas.GuardAreas
		if guardAreas then
			for _, child in ipairs(guardAreas:GetChildren()) do
				local nests = child:FindFirstChild("Nests")
				if nests then
					table.insert(filterDescendantsInstances, nests)
				end
				local guard = child:FindFirstChild("Guard")
				if guard then
					table.insert(filterDescendantsInstances, guard)
				end
			end
		end
		local wallStartVisual = Core.World() and Core.World().Areas and Core.World().Areas:FindFirstChild("WallStartVisual")
		if wallStartVisual then
			table.insert(filterDescendantsInstances, wallStartVisual)
		end
		rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterDescendantsInstances
		local rayResult = Core.Workspace:Raycast(position + Vector3.new(0, 60, 0), Vector3.new(0, -600, 0), rayParams)
		return rayResult and rayResult.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0) or position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
	end

	local lookup = {}
	local FPS_SAMPLE_COUNT = 45
	local StepBudget = {CAP = 6.4, MIN = 5, MAX = 9.5, UP = 0.25, DOWN = 0.75, ROLLBACK = 25}

	function StepBudget.budget()
		return math.clamp(SAE_ST.stepBudget or StepBudget.CAP, StepBudget.MIN, StepBudget.MAX)
	end

	function StepBudget.up()
		SAE_ST.stepBudget = math.min(StepBudget.MAX, StepBudget.budget() + StepBudget.UP)
	end

	function StepBudget.down()
		SAE_ST.stepBudget = math.max(StepBudget.MIN, StepBudget.budget() * StepBudget.DOWN)
	end

	local ACCEL_SPEED_FACTOR = 0.22
	local EASE_FLOOR = 0.18
	local ACCEL_DISTANCE_FACTOR = 0.35
	local frameTimes = table.create(FPS_SAMPLE_COUNT, 0.016666666666666666)
	local frameIndex = 1
	local frameTimeSum = FPS_SAMPLE_COUNT / 60
	Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(clamped)
		if SAE_ST.stale() then
			return
		end
		clamped = math.clamp(clamped, 0.0020833333333333333, 0.2)
		frameTimeSum = frameTimeSum - frameTimes[frameIndex] + clamped
		frameTimes[frameIndex] = clamped
		frameIndex = frameIndex % FPS_SAMPLE_COUNT + 1
	end)

	local function getAverageFps()
		return FPS_SAMPLE_COUNT / math.max(frameTimeSum, 1e-06)
	end

	function StepBudget.governedCeiling()
		return math.max(120, getAverageFps() * 6.4)
	end

	function StepBudget.governSpeed(arg)
		if not SAE_ST.fpsGovern then
			return arg
		end
		return math.min(arg, StepBudget.governedCeiling())
	end

	local function resolveTweenSpeed(arg)
		if SAE_ST.tweenMode ~= "Multiplier Speed" then
			return arg
		end
		local character = getCharacter()
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local number = tonumber(humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed) or 0
		if number <= 0 then
			number = 16
		end
		return math.clamp(number * (tonumber(SAE_ST.speedMultiplier) or 1), 16, 2000)
	end

	local WALKSPEED_FALLBACK_FACTOR = 2
	local moveToken = 0

	local function easeOutSine(arg)
		return EASE_FLOOR + (1 - EASE_FLOOR) * math.sin(math.clamp(arg, 0, 1) * (math.pi / 2))
	end

	local function down(part, humanoid, CFrame2, callback, number, arg, arg2)
		if not part or not part.Parent or not isAlive() then
			return false
		end
		if callback and callback() then
			return true
		end
		moveToken = moveToken + 1
		local savedMoveToken = moveToken
		local CFrame3 = part.CFrame
		local distance = (CFrame2.Position - CFrame3.Position).Magnitude
		if distance < 0.1 then
			if isAlive() and part.Parent then
				part.CFrame = CFrame2
				part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				return true
			end
			return false
		end
		local savedNumber = number and number > 0 and number or (humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed or 16) * WALKSPEED_FALLBACK_FACTOR
		local savedNumber2 = number and number >= 490
		if not savedNumber2 then
			savedNumber = StepBudget.governSpeed(savedNumber)
		end
		local minValue = math.min(savedNumber * ACCEL_SPEED_FACTOR, distance * ACCEL_DISTANCE_FACTOR)
		local savedMinValue = arg and minValue or 0
		local savedMinValue2 = arg2 and minValue or 0
		local huge = SAE_ST.fpsGovern and not savedNumber2 and StepBudget.budget() or math.huge
		local minValue2 = 0
		local position
		while minValue2 < distance do
			if moveToken ~= savedMoveToken then
				return false
			end
			if callback and callback() then
				break
			end
			if not isAlive() then
				return false
			end
			local result = Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			if moveToken ~= savedMoveToken then
				return false
			end
			if not part.Parent or not isAlive() then
				return false
			end
			if position and (part.Position - position).Magnitude > StepBudget.ROLLBACK then
				if not SAE_ST.staging then
					StepBudget.down()
				end
				return false
			end
			local savedSavedNumber = savedNumber
			if savedMinValue > 0 and minValue2 < savedMinValue then
				savedSavedNumber = savedNumber * easeOutSine(minValue2 / savedMinValue)
			elseif savedMinValue2 > 0 and distance - minValue2 < savedMinValue2 then
				savedSavedNumber = savedNumber * easeOutSine((distance - minValue2) / savedMinValue2)
			end
			local minValue3 = math.min(result, math.min(0.05, 2 / math.max(getAverageFps(), 1)))
			minValue2 = math.min(distance, minValue2 + math.min(savedSavedNumber * minValue3, huge))
			if not isAlive() or not part.Parent then
				return false
			end
			part.CFrame = CFrame3:Lerp(CFrame2, minValue2 / distance)
			position = part.Position
			part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		end
		if moveToken ~= savedMoveToken or not isAlive() or not part.Parent then
			return false
		end
		if minValue2 >= distance and distance > 60 then
			StepBudget.up()
		end
		part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		return true
	end

	local DEFAULT_SAFE_POSITION = Vector3.new(524.45, 70.28, -365.87)

	local function getDefaultSafePosition()
		return DEFAULT_SAFE_POSITION
	end

	local function getPlotAnchor()
		local plotData = Core.PlotCmds.GetPlotData()
		if plotData then
			for _, name in ipairs({"RespawnPointCFrame", "CenterPoint", "PetArea"}) do
				local instance = plotData[name]
				if type(instance) == "userdata" and instance.Position then
					return raycastGround(instance.Position)
				end
			end
		end
		local instance = hrp()
		return instance and instance.Position or Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
	end

	function Core.rarityRank(rarity)
		if type(rarity) ~= "table" then
			return nil
		end
		return rarity.RarityNumber or rarity.Rank
	end

	local rarityRankByName = {}
	local eggCatalog = {}
	for cat, info in pairs(Core.Assets) do
		local result = type(info) == "table" and Core.rarityRank(info.Rarity)
		if result then
			local id = info.Rarity.DisplayName or info.Rarity._id or "?"
			rarityRankByName[id] = result
			table.insert(eggCatalog, {cat = cat, rnum = result, rname = id})
		end
	end
	table.sort(eggCatalog, function(info, info2)
		if info.rnum == info2.rnum then
			return info.cat < info2.cat
		end
		return info.rnum > info2.rnum
	end)

	local function getRarityInfo(assetCategory)
		local info = Core.Assets[assetCategory]
		if info and info.Rarity then
			return Core.rarityRank(info.Rarity) or 0, info.Rarity.DisplayName or info.Rarity._id or "?", info.Rarity.Color
		end
		return 0, "?", nil
	end

	local function describeAsset(assetCategory)
		local list = Core.Assets[assetCategory]
		if type(list) ~= "table" then
			return nil
		end

		local function fn(text)
			if type(text) == "string" and text ~= "" then
				if text:find("rbxassetid") or text:find("rbxthumb") then
					return text
				end
				if text:match("^%d+$") then
					return "rbxassetid://" .. text
				end
			elseif type(text) == "number" and text > 0 then
				return "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(text)
			end
			return nil
		end

		local list2 = {"Image", "Icon", "Thumbnail", "ImageId", "IconId", "Texture", "Asset", "DisplayImage"}
		for _, value in ipairs(list2) do
			local result = fn(list[value])
			if result then
				return result
			end
		end
		for _, item in pairs(list) do
			if type(item) == "table" then
				for _, value in ipairs(list2) do
					local result = fn(item[value])
					if result then
						return result
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, item in pairs(list) do
			if type(item) == "string" and item:find("rbxassetid") then
				return item
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function logEggSighting(rec)
		local assetCategory = rec and rec.AssetCategory
		if not assetCategory then
			return
		end
		local tbl = SAE_ST.eggLog[assetCategory]
		if not tbl then
			local rarityInfo, rarity, color = getRarityInfo(assetCategory)
			tbl = {
				name = assetCategory,
				rarity = rarity,
				rnum = rarityInfo,
				color = color,
				count = 0,
				last = 0,
				image = describeAsset(assetCategory),
			}
			SAE_ST.eggLog[assetCategory] = tbl
		end
		local info = tbl
		info.count = info.count + 1
		tbl.last = os.clock()
	end

	local function listRarityNames()
		local seen = {}
		local items = {}
		for _, info in ipairs(eggCatalog) do
			if not seen[info.rname] then
				seen[info.rname] = true
				table.insert(items, {info.rname, info.rnum})
			end
		end
		table.sort(items, function(map, map2)
			return map[2] > map2[2]
		end)
		local list = {"Any"}
		for _, item in ipairs(items) do
			table.insert(list, item[1])
		end
		return list
	end

	local function listRarityNamesWithoutAny()
		local results = {}
		for _, rarityName in ipairs(listRarityNames()) do
			if rarityName ~= "Any" then
				table.insert(results, rarityName)
			end
		end
		return results
	end

	local function listGuardAreaNames()

		if guardAreas then

	local function listCategoriesAtOrAboveRarity(amount)
		local results = {}
		for _, info in ipairs(eggCatalog) do
			if info.rnum >= amount then
				table.insert(results, info.cat)
			end
		end
		return results
	end

	local function listContains(sellNames, arg)
		for _, savedSellName in ipairs(sellNames or {}) do
			if savedSellName == arg then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function deserializeAsset(arg)
		local ok, result = pcall(Core.AssetSer.Deserialize, arg)
		if ok and type(result) == "table" then
			return result
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function toSet(stealNames)
		if not stealNames or #stealNames == 0 then
			return nil
		end
		local seen = {}
		for _, savedStealName in ipairs(stealNames) do
			seen[savedStealName] = true
		end
		return seen
	end

	local lastSnapshotRequestAt = 0

	local function getFreshEggSnapshot(arg)
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - lastSnapshotRequestAt >= (arg or 0.75) then
			lastSnapshotRequestAt = now
			pcall(function()
				Core.EggCmds.RequestAreaEggSnapshot()
			end)
		end
		return Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
	end

	pcall(function()
		Core.EggCmds.AreaEggUpdated:Connect(function()
			lastSnapshotRequestAt = os.clock()
		end)
		Core.EggCmds.AreaEggRemoved:Connect(function()
			lastSnapshotRequestAt = os.clock()
		end)
		Core.EggCmds.AreaEggSnapshotUpdated:Connect(function()
			lastSnapshotRequestAt = os.clock()
		end)
	end)
	local eggValueCache = {}
	local eggWeightCache = {}

	local function getEggValue(rec)
		if not rec then
			return 0
		end
		local text = tostring(rec.Uid) .. ":" .. tostring(rec.AssetScale)
		local value = eggValueCache[text]
		if value ~= nil then
			return value
		end
		local number = tonumber(rec.AssetScale) or 1
		local tbl = {
			Category = rec.AssetCategory,
			Scale = number,
			AssetScale = number,
			BaseMutation = rec.BaseMutation,
			Mutations = rec.Mutations or {},
		}
		local value2 = 0
		local assetGenerationUtil = Core.AssetGenerationUtil
		if assetGenerationUtil then
			if assetGenerationUtil.GetBaseRateMutationOnly then
				local ok, result = pcall(assetGenerationUtil.GetBaseRateMutationOnly, tbl)
				if ok and type(result) == "number" and result > 0 then
					value2 = result
				end
			end
			if value2 == 0 and assetGenerationUtil.GetRate then
				local ok, result = pcall(assetGenerationUtil.GetRate, tbl)
				if ok and type(result) == "number" and result > 0 then
					value2 = result
				end
			end
		end
		eggValueCache[text] = value2
		return value2
	end

	local function getEggScore(rec)
		if not rec then
			return 0
		end
		local text = tostring(rec.Uid) .. ":" .. tostring(rec.AssetScale)
		local value = eggWeightCache[text]
		if value ~= nil then
			return value
		end
		local value2 = 0
		if Core.EggItemUtil and Core.EggItemUtil.GetWeightKg then
			local ok, result = pcall(Core.EggItemUtil.GetWeightKg, rec)
			if ok and type(result) == "number" and result > 0 then
				value2 = result
			end
		end
		if value2 == 0 then
			local info
			pcall(function()
				info = Core.Assets[rec.AssetCategory]
			end)
			value2 = (info and tonumber(info.ModelWeight) or 1) * (tonumber(rec.AssetScale) or 1)
		end
		eggWeightCache[text] = value2
		return value2
	end

	local function getOwnedEggValue(record)
		if not record then
			return 0
		end
		local text = tostring(record.Uid or tostring(record)) .. ":" .. tostring(record.AssetScale)
		local value = eggValueCache[text]
		if value ~= nil then
			return value
		end
		local number = tonumber(record.AssetScale) or 1
		local tbl = {
			Category = record.AssetCategory,
			Scale = number,
			AssetScale = number,
			BaseMutation = record.BaseMutation,
			Mutations = record.Mutations or {},
		}
		local value2 = 0
		if Core.AssetEarnings and Core.AssetEarnings.LiveRatePerSecond then
			local ok, result = pcall(Core.AssetEarnings.LiveRatePerSecond, tbl)
			if ok and type(result) == "number" and result > 0 then
				value2 = result
			end
		end
		eggValueCache[text] = value2
		return value2
	end

	local function parseAmount(stealAmount)
		local lowered = tostring(stealAmount or ""):gsub("[%s,$]", ""):lower()
		if lowered == "" then
			return nil
		end
		local match, value = lowered:match("^(-?%d*%.?%d+)([kmbtq]?[aiu]?)$")
		if not match then
			return nil
		end
		local number = tonumber(match)
		if not number then
			return nil
		end
		local value2 = ({k = 1000, m = 1000000, b = 1000000000, t = 1000000000000, qa = 1000000000000000, qi = 1e+18})[value]
		return number * (value2 or 1)
	end

	local function formatNumber(arg)
		local Simple
		pcall(function()
			Simple = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Packages.FormatNumber.Simple)
		end)
		if Simple and Simple.FormatCompact then
			local ok, text = pcall(Simple.FormatCompact, arg, ".#")
			if ok and text then
				return "$" .. tostring(text) .. "/s"
			end
		end
		return string.format("$%d/s", math.floor(arg or 0))
	end

	local function matchesSelection(rec, arg, set, set2)
		local selectBy = SAE_ST.selectBy or "Rarity"
		if selectBy == "Name" then
			return not set or set[rec.AssetCategory] == true
		elseif selectBy == "Mutation" then
			local mutations = rec.Mutations or {}
			local set3 = toSet(SAE_ST.stealMutations)
			if not set3 then
				return #mutations > 0
			end
			for _, savedMutation in ipairs(mutations) do
				if set3[tostring(savedMutation)] then
					return true
				end
			end
			return false
		elseif selectBy == "Value" then
			local amount = parseAmount(SAE_ST.stealAmount)
			return not amount or getEggValue(rec) >= amount
		elseif selectBy == "Weight" then
			local amount = parseAmount(SAE_ST.stealAmount)
			return not amount or getEggScore(rec) >= amount
		end
		return not set2 or set2[arg] == true
	end

	function SAE_ST.TP_FACTORY(callback)
		local settings = {

			pcall(genv.__AbdraHubShutdown)

		local resolveHitDistance

			if instantV1.Status == status then
				return
			end
			instantV1.Status = status
			print("[Instant V1] " .. status)
		end

		local function getEnabled()
			return instantV1.Running and instantV1.Enabled and genv.InstantV1 == instantV1
		end

		function instantV1.Stop(arg)
			arg.Running = false
			arg.Enabled = false
			arg.Status = "Stopped"
			for _, connection in ipairs(items) do
				pcall(function()
					connection:Disconnect()
				end)
			end
			table.clear(items)
			if part then
				part:Destroy()
				part = nil
			end
			if GuardChasePolicy and resolveHitDistance then
				pcall(function()
					GuardChasePolicy.ResolveHitDistance = resolveHitDistance
				end)
			end
			if genv.InstantV1 == arg then
				genv.InstantV1 = nil
			end
		end

		local function stop(eggState)
			local ok, text = pcall(require, eggState)
			if not ok then
				instantV1:Stop()
				error("Instant V1: cannot load " .. tostring(eggState) .. ": " .. tostring(text))
			end
			return text
		end

		if not getAreaEggRecord or not getAreaEggSnapshot or not requestDropHeldAreaEgg or not eggWorld or not eggWorld.AskFieldEggCarry then
			instantV1:Stop()
			error("Instant V1: field egg API is unavailable")

		local cframe = CFrame.new(546.160095, 70.672607, -312.640076, 0.0320008546, -1.19248071e-07, -0.999487817, -1.76635826e-08, 1, -1.19874713e-07, 0.999487817, 2.14906297e-08, 0.0320008546)

		local function getRecords()
			local ok, info = pcall(getAreaEggSnapshot)
			return ok and type(info) == "table" and (info.Records or info) or {}
		end 

		local function fn4(record)
			return type(record) == "table" and type(record.Uid) == "string" and (record.State == info3.States.Slot or record.State == info3.States.Dropped) and typeof(record.BottomCFrame) == "CFrame"
		end

			local info = fn3(uid)
			return info ~= nil and info.State == info3.States.Carried and info.CarrierUserId == localPlayer.UserId
		end

		local function fn6(names, assetCategory)
			return #names == 0 or table.find(names, assetCategory) ~= nil
		end

		local function fn7(info)
			local player = directory[info.AssetCategory]
			local rarity = player and player.Rarity
			local assetCategory = player and (player.DisplayName or player._id) or info.AssetCategory
			local id = rarity and (rarity.DisplayName or rarity._id) or "Unknown"
			return assetCategory, id
		end

		local function fn8(info)
			local result, value = fn7(info)
			if #settings.Names > 0 and not fn6(settings.Names, result) and not fn6(settings.Names, info.AssetCategory) then
				return false
			end
			if not fn6(settings.Rarities, value) or not fn6(settings.Areas, info.AreaId) then
				return false
			end
			if #settings.Mutations > 0 then
				local result2 = fn6(settings.Mutations, info.BaseMutation)
				for _, mutation in ipairs(info.Mutations or {}) do
					result2 = result2 or fn6(settings.Mutations, mutation)
				end
				if not result2 then
					return false
				end
			end
			if settings.MinValuePerSecond > 0 then
				local ok, result2 = pcall(info6.MutationOnlyRatePerSecond, {
					Category = info.AssetCategory,
					Scale = info.AssetScale or 1,
					Mutations = info.Mutations or {},
					BaseMutation = info.BaseMutation,
				})
				if not ok or type(result2) ~= "number" or result2 < settings.MinValuePerSecond then
					return false
				end
			end
			if settings.MinWeightKg > 0 then
				local ok, result2 = pcall(info7.WeightKgForScale, info.AssetCategory, info.AssetScale)
				if not ok or type(result2) ~= "number" or result2 < settings.MinWeightKg then
					return false
				end
			end
			return true
		end

		local function getGuard()
			local OBJECTS = workspace:FindFirstChild("World") or workspace:FindFirstChild("__OBJECTS")
			local areas = OBJECTS and OBJECTS:FindFirstChild("Areas")
			local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
			local forest = guardAreas and guardAreas:FindFirstChild("Forest")
			return forest and forest:FindFirstChild("Guard")
		end

		local function fn9()
			local result, instance = fn2()
			if not instance then
				return nil
			end
			local value
			local value2
			local value3
			for _, record in pairs(getRecords()) do
				if fn4(record) and os.clock() >= (instantV1.Skipped[record.Uid] or 0) and fn8(record) then
					local result2, value4 = fn7(record)
					local value5 = tbl[value4] or 0
					local distance = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude
					if not value or value5 > value2 or value5 == value2 and distance < value3 then
						value, value2, value3 = record, value5, distance
					end
				end
			end
			return value
		end

		local function fn10(uid)
			local result, instance = fn2()
			if not instance then
				return nil
			end
			local guard = getGuard()
			local humanoidRootPart = guard and guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local position = humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Position or instance.Position
			local value
			local value2
			for _, record in pairs(getRecords()) do
				if fn4(record) and record.State == info3.States.Slot and record.AreaId == "Forest" and record.Uid ~= uid and os.clock() >= (instantV1.Skipped[record.Uid] or 0) then
					local result2, value3 = fn7(record)
					local value4 = -(record.BottomCFrame.Position - position).Magnitude - (tbl[value3] or 0) * 100
					if not value or value4 > value2 then
						value, value2 = record, value4
					end
				end
			end
			return value
		end

		local function fn11(instance, info)
			if info.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
				return instance.Size.Y * 0.5 + math.max(info.HipHeight, 0.5)
			end
			return instance.Size.Y * 0.5 + 2
		end

		local function getCframe(info)
			local result, value, value2 = fn2()
			if not fn4(info) or not value or not value2 then
				return nil
			end
			return CFrame.new(info.BottomCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, fn11(value, value2) + 0.15, 0))
		end

		local value
		local value2 = 0
		local flag = false
		local list2 = {
			"LastObservedSample",
			"LastSample",

		local function fn12(arg, part, arg2)
			if type(getgc) ~= "function" then
				return
			end
			if type(value) ~= "table" or rawget(value, "Character") ~= arg or rawget(value, "RootPart") ~= part then
				if flag or os.clock() < value2 then
					return
				end
				value2 = os.clock() + 1
				flag = true
				task.spawn(function()
					local ok, list = pcall(getgc, true)
					local value2
					if ok and type(list) == "table" then
						for _, item in ipairs(list) do
							if type(item) == "table" and rawget(item, "Player") == localPlayer and rawget(item, "Character") == arg and rawget(item, "Humanoid") == arg2 and rawget(item, "RootPart") == part and type(rawget(item, "SampleHistory")) == "table" and type(rawget(item, "Evidence")) == "table" then
								value2 = item
								break
							end
						end
					end
					value = value2
					flag = false
				end)
				return
			end
			local value3 = value
			if not value3 then
				return
			end

			local function fn(preMovementSafeSample)
				if type(preMovementSafeSample) ~= "table" then
					return
				end
				rawset(preMovementSafeSample, "WalkSpeed", 810)
				rawset(preMovementSafeSample, "CFrame", part.CFrame)
				rawset(preMovementSafeSample, "Position", part.Position)
				rawset(preMovementSafeSample, "LinearVelocity", Vector3.zero)
				rawset(preMovementSafeSample, "AngularVelocity", Vector3.zero)
			end

			for _, value4 in ipairs(list2) do
				fn(rawget(value3, value4))
			end
			for _, value4 in pairs(rawget(value3, "SampleHistory") or {}) do
				fn(value4)
			end
			for _, value4 in pairs(rawget(value3, "SafeGroundCheckpoints") or {}) do
				fn(value4)
			end
			local raw = rawget(value3, "Evidence")
			if type(raw) == "table" then
				raw.Speed, raw.Teleport, raw.Flight = 0, 0, 0
			end
			local raw2 = rawget(value3, "ImpulseContext")
			if type(raw2) == "table" then
				local maxValue = math.max(tonumber(raw2.MaxHorizontalSpeed) or 0, 810)
				local maxValue2 = math.max((tonumber(raw2.ExpiresAt) or 0) - (tonumber(raw2.StartedAt) or 0), 0.15)
				raw2.MaxHorizontalSpeed = maxValue
				raw2.OriginPosition = part.Position
				raw2.MaxHorizontalDistance = math.max(tonumber(raw2.MaxHorizontalDistance) or 0, maxValue * maxValue2 + 48)
				fn(raw2.PreMovementSafeSample)
			end
			if rawget(value3, "CorrectionContext") == nil then
				rawset(value3, "FirstSuspiciousAt", nil)
				if rawget(value3, "ThreatLevel") == "Observing" then
					rawset(value3, "ThreatLevel", "Trusted")
				end
			end
		end

		part = Instance.new("Part")
		part.Name = "__InstantV1HoverPad"
		part.Anchored = true

		end

		local function fn13()
			if not part then
				return
			end

			end
		end

		local function fn14(cframe, callback)
			if typeof(cframe) ~= "CFrame" then
				return false
			end
			local result, instance, instance2 = fn2()
			if not instance or not instance2 then
				return false
			end
			local autoRotate = instance2.AutoRotate
			instance2.AutoRotate = false
			local maxValue = math.max(tonumber(settings.GlideSpeed) or 5000, 1)
			local value = os.clock() + math.clamp((cframe.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude / maxValue * 1.6 + 6, 15, 120)
			while getEnabled() and not (callback and callback()) and os.clock() < value do
				result, instance, instance2 = fn2()
				if not instance or not instance2 then
					break
				end
				local vector2 = cframe.Position - instance.Position
				if vector2.Magnitude <= 1 then
					setAttribute(cframe)
					instance2.AutoRotate = autoRotate
					return true
				end
				local result2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				local minValue = math.min(maxValue * math.clamp(result2, 0.004166666666666667, 0.15), vector2.Magnitude)
				local value2 = instance.Position + vector2.Unit * minValue
				setAttribute(CFrame.new(value2, value2 + vector2.Unit))
			end
			if instance2 and instance2.Parent then
				instance2.AutoRotate = autoRotate
			end
			return false
		end

		local function fn15(callback, amount)
			local value = os.clock() + amount
			while getEnabled() and os.clock() < value do
				if callback() then
					return true
				end
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
			return callback()
		end

		local function fn16(uid, arrivalHold, amount, arg)
			local flag = false
			local value = os.clock() + amount
			local value2 = 0
			pcall(function()
				value2 = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStats.Ping:GetValue()
			end)
			local value3 = os.clock() + (arg and 0 or math.max(0.3, value2 * 2.5 / 1000))
			instantV1.ArrivalHold = arrivalHold

			local function fn(arg)
				instantV1.ArrivalHold = nil
				return arg
			end

			while getEnabled() and os.clock() < value do
				if fn5(uid) then
					return fn(true)
				end
				local info = fn3(uid)
				if not fn4(info) then
					return fn(false)
				end
				if arrivalHold then
					setAttribute(arrivalHold)
				end
				if not flag and os.clock() >= value3 then
					flag = true
					value3 = os.clock() + (arg and 0.06 or 0.25)
					task.spawn(function()
						local firstAreaSlotKey
						pcall(function()
							if info4.LooksLikeFirstAreaUid(uid) then
								firstAreaSlotKey = info4.SlotKey(info.AreaId, info.NestId)
							end
						end)
