							tbl.FirstAreaSlotKey = firstAreaSlotKey
						end
						pcall(function()
							eggWorld.AskFieldEggCarry:InvokeServer(tbl)
						end)
						if requestCarryAreaEgg then
							pcall(requestCarryAreaEgg, uid)
						end
						flag = false
					end)
				end
				if arg and type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" then
					pcall(function()
						local areaEggSlotsClient = workspace:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient")
						local child = areaEggSlotsClient and areaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(uid)
						local proximityPrompt = child and (child:FindFirstChild("CarryAreaEgg", true) or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true))
						if proximityPrompt and proximityPrompt.Enabled then
							proximityPrompt.HoldDuration = 0
							proximityPrompt.RequiresLineOfSight = false
							fireproximityprompt(proximityPrompt, 0)
						end
					end)
				end
				task.wait(arg and 0.06 or 0.15)
			end
			return fn(fn5(uid) == true)
		end

		local function invokeServer()
			local treadmill = info2.Treadmill
			if not treadmill or not treadmill.AskDoff then
				return
			end
			local result, part, humanoid = fn2()
			if part and (part.Anchored or humanoid and humanoid.Sit) then
				pcall(function()
					treadmill.AskDoff:InvokeServer()
				end)
				task.wait(0.3)
			end
		end

		local function fireServer(record)
			local collider
			local cframe = getCframe(record)
			if not cframe then
				return false
			end
			instantV1.Priming = true
			fn("Taking Forest primer")
			if not setAttribute(cframe) or not fn16(record.Uid, cframe, 5) then
				instantV1.Priming = false
				return false
			end
			fn("Provoking Forest guard")
			local flag = false
			local value = os.clock() + 1.8
			while getEnabled() and os.clock() < value do
				local number = tonumber(localPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")) or 0
				if not fn5(record.Uid) or number > workspace:GetServerTimeNow() then
					flag = true
					break
				end
				local guard = getGuard()
				local humanoidRootPart = guard and guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local result, instance = fn2()
				if humanoidRootPart and instance then
					local vector2 = humanoidRootPart.Position + humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * 2.5
					local vector3 = Vector3.new(vector2.X, instance.Position.Y, vector2.Z)
					setAttribute(CFrame.lookAt(vector3, Vector3.new(humanoidRootPart.Position.X, vector3.Y, humanoidRootPart.Position.Z)))
					collider = guard:FindFirstChild("Collider") or humanoidRootPart
					if type(firetouchinterest) == "function" then
						pcall(function()
							firetouchinterest(instance, collider, 0)
							firetouchinterest(instance, collider, 1)
						end)
					end
					pcall(function()
						local forestStrike = info2.GuardPatrol and info2.GuardPatrol.ForestStrike
						if forestStrike then
							forestStrike:FireServer({EggUid = record.Uid, GuardCFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame})
						end
					end)
				end
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
			instantV1.Priming = false
			instantV1.Skipped[record.Uid] = os.clock() + 30
			if flag and fn5(record.Uid) then
				fn15(function()
					return not fn5(record.Uid)
				end, 1.5)
			end
			return flag and not fn5(record.Uid)
		end

		local function fn17(arg)
			local info = fn3(arg)
			return info == nil or info.State == info3.States.Claimed
		end

		local function fn18()
			local treadmill = info2.Treadmill
			if not treadmill or not treadmill.AskWearStill or not treadmill.AskDoff then
				return false
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				local localSlot = info8.ResolveLocalSlot()
				local child = info8.ResolveFolder():FindFirstChild(tostring(localSlot))
				local treadmillBottom = child and child:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom")
				local result, value, value2 = fn2()
				if not treadmillBottom or not value or not value2 then
					return nil
				end
				return treadmillBottom.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, treadmillBottom.Size.Y * 0.5 + fn11(value, value2) + 0.05, 0)
			end)
			if not ok or not result or not fn14(result) then
				return false
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
			local flag = false
			for i = 1, 3 do
				local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
					return treadmill.AskWearStill:InvokeServer()
				end)
				if ok2 and result2 == true then
					flag = true
					break
				end
				task.wait(0.3)
			end
			if not flag then
				return false
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
			for i = 1, 3 do
				local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
					return treadmill.AskDoff:InvokeServer()
				end)
				if ok2 and result2 == true then
					return true
				end
				task.wait(0.3)
			end
			return false
		end

		local value3

		local function fn19(uid)
			if not fn5(uid) then
				return false, "Carry lost"
			end
			local roll = math.random(#list)
			if roll == value3 then
				roll = roll % #list + 1
			end
			value3 = roll
			local value = list[roll]
			fn("Gliding egg to Forest relay")
			if not fn14(value, function()
				return not fn5(uid)
			end) then
				return false, "Relay travel failed"
			end
			for i = 1, 3 do
				if not fn5(uid) then
					break
				end
				setAttribute(value)
				task.wait(0.4)
				pcall(requestDropHeldAreaEgg, "PlayerRequest")
				if fn15(function()
					local info = fn3(uid)
					return info and info.State == info3.States.Dropped
				end, 0.85) then
					break
				end
			end
			local info = fn3(uid)
			if not info or info.State ~= info3.States.Dropped then
				return false, "Relay drop failed"
			end
			task.wait(0.85)
			info = fn3(uid)
			if not info or info.State ~= info3.States.Dropped then
				return false, "Relay drop rolled back"
			end
			fn("Reset at safe zone")
			if not fn14(cframe) then
				return false, "Safe reset failed"
			end
			if not fn4(fn3(uid)) then
				return false, "Relay egg returned to nest"
			end
			for i = 1, 2 do
				info = fn3(uid)
				if not fn4(info) then
					return false, "Relay egg unavailable"
				end
				local cframe2 = getCframe(info)
				if not cframe2 then
					return false, "Relay location unavailable"
				end
				fn("Re-grabbing relay egg " .. i .. "/2")
				if fn14(cframe2) and fn16(uid, cframe2, 0.8, true) then
					break
				end
				if not fn14(cframe) then
					return false, "Re-grab reset failed"
				end
				if i == 1 then
					fn("Resetting on treadmill")
					fn18()
					if not fn14(cframe) then
						return false, "Treadmill return failed"
					end
				end
			end
			if not fn5(uid) then
				return false, "Relay re-grab failed"
			end
			fn("Gliding egg home")
			if not fn14(cframe, function()
				return fn17(uid)
			end) and not fn17(uid) then
				return false, "Home travel failed"
			end
			fn("Waiting for deposit")
			if fn15(function()
				return fn17(uid)
			end, 8) then
				return true, "Egg deposited"
			end
			return false, "Deposit not confirmed"
		end

		pcall(function()
			GuardChasePolicy = require(shared2.Modules.GuardAreas.GuardChasePolicy)
			resolveHitDistance = GuardChasePolicy.ResolveHitDistance

			function GuardChasePolicy.ResolveHitDistance(...)
				if getEnabled() then
					return instantV1.Priming and 25 or 1e-05
				end
				return resolveHitDistance(...)
			end
		end)
		table.insert(items, localPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
			value = nil
		end))
		table.insert(items, RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
			if not getEnabled() then
				return
			end
			local result, value, value2 = fn2()
			if value and value2 then
				if instantV1.ArrivalHold then
					setAttribute(instantV1.ArrivalHold)
				end
				pcall(fn12, result, value, value2)
				fn13()
			end
		end))
		table.insert(items, RunService.PostSimulation:Connect(function()
			if getEnabled() then
				fn13()
			end
		end))
		table.insert(items, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not getEnabled() then
				return
			end
			local result, part = fn2()
			if not part then
				return
			end
			local assemblyLinearVelocity = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
			if Vector3.new(assemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, assemblyLinearVelocity.Z).Magnitude > 400 or math.abs(assemblyLinearVelocity.Y) > 400 then
				part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(math.clamp(assemblyLinearVelocity.X, -100, 100), math.clamp(assemblyLinearVelocity.Y, -100, 100), math.clamp(assemblyLinearVelocity.Z, -100, 100))
			end
		end))

		local function changeState(record)
			invokeServer()
			local result, instance = fn2()
			if not instance then
				return false, "Character unavailable"
			end
			if (instance.Position - cframe.Position).Magnitude > 3 then
				fn("Moving to safe zone")
				if not fn14(cframe) then
					return false, "Safe travel failed"
				end
			end
			local result2 = fn10(record.Uid)
			if not result2 then
				return false, "Waiting for Forest primer"
			end
			if not fireServer(result2) then
				return false, "Forest guard bait failed"
			end
			local result3, value, humanoid = fn2()
			if humanoid then
				pcall(function()
					humanoid.PlatformStand = false
					humanoid.Sit = false
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				end)
			end
			if value then
				value.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				value.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			local result4 = fn3(record.Uid)
			if not fn4(result4) then
				return false, "Target egg unavailable"
			end
			local cframe2 = getCframe(result4)
			if not cframe2 then
				return false, "Target location unavailable"
			end
			local value2 = cframe2 + Vector3.new(0, 14, 0)
			fn("Gliding to target egg")
			if not fn14(value2) then
				return false, "Target travel failed"
			end
			local clamped = math.clamp(tonumber(settings.Delay) or 15, 3, 20)
			fn("Hovering above egg for " .. clamped .. "s")
			local value3 = os.clock() + clamped
			while getEnabled() and os.clock() < value3 do
				if not fn4(fn3(record.Uid)) then
					return false, "Target changed"
				end
				setAttribute(value2)
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
			if not getEnabled() then
				return false, "Stopped"
			end
			if not fn14(cframe2) then
				return false, "Target approach failed"
			end
			fn("Securing target egg")
			if not fn16(record.Uid, cframe2, math.max(5, clamped + 3)) then
				return false, "Carry not confirmed"
			end
			return fn19(record.Uid)
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			fn("Auto Steal Instant V1 active")
			while instantV1.Running and genv.InstantV1 == instantV1 do
				if not instantV1.Enabled then
					task.wait(0.25)
				elseif type(info5.IsSealed) == "function" and info5.IsSealed() then
					fn("Waiting for reset wall")
					task.wait(1)
				else
					local record = fn9()
					if not record then
						fn("Waiting for matching egg")
						task.wait(0.5)
					else
						local ok, text, value = pcall(changeState, record)
						instantV1.Priming = false
						if ok then
							fn(value or (text and "Complete" or "Retrying"))
						else
							fn("Error: " .. tostring(text))
						end
						instantV1.Skipped[record.Uid] = os.clock() + (ok and text and 3 or 8)
						task.wait(0.5)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		return instantV1
	end

	function SAE_ST.tpSettings(arg)
		local selectBy = SAE_ST.selectBy or "Rarity"
		local amount = parseAmount(SAE_ST.stealAmount) or 0

		local function fn(stealMutations)
			local results = {}
			local ok, info = pcall(function()
				return require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules.Mutations)
			end)
			for _, savedStealMutation in ipairs(stealMutations or {}) do
				local savedSavedStealMutation
				if ok and type(info) == "table" then
					pcall(function()
						if info.IdSet[savedStealMutation] then
							savedSavedStealMutation = savedStealMutation
						else
							for key in pairs(info.Ids()) do
								if key ~= info.NO_MUTATION and info.LabelOf(key) == savedStealMutation then
									savedSavedStealMutation = key
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end
				table.insert(results, savedSavedStealMutation or savedStealMutation)
			end
			return results
		end

		arg.Delay = math.clamp(tonumber(SAE_ST.tpDelay) or 15, 3, 20)
		arg.Areas = table.clone(SAE_ST.stealAreas or {})
		arg.Rarities = selectBy == "Rarity" and table.clone(SAE_ST.minRarityNames or {}) or {}
		arg.Names = selectBy == "Name" and table.clone(SAE_ST.stealNames or {}) or {}
		arg.Mutations = selectBy == "Mutation" and fn(SAE_ST.stealMutations) or {}
		arg.MinValuePerSecond = selectBy == "Value" and amount or 0
		arg.MinWeightKg = selectBy == "Weight" and amount or 0
	end

	function SAE_ST.tpApply()
		local tp = SAE_ST.TP
		if not (tp and SAE_ST.tpSteal) then
			return
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(SAE_ST.tpSettings, tp.Settings)
		if not ok then
			warn("[Auto Steal] apply settings failed:", result)
		end
	end

	SAE_ST.tpIdleStatus = {["Waiting for matching egg"] = true, ["Waiting for reset wall"] = true, ["Stopped"] = true}

	function SAE_ST.tpHasWork()
		local tp = SAE_ST.TP
		if not (tp and SAE_ST.tpSteal and tp.Running) then
			return false
		end
		return not SAE_ST.tpIdleStatus[tostring(tp.Status)]
	end

	function SAE_ST.tpSync()
		if SAE_ST.tpWorker then
			return
		end
		SAE_ST.tpWorker = true
		task.spawn(function()
			local flag, tp, tp2
			while not SAE_ST.stale() do
				local flag2 = SAE_ST.tpWanted == true
				if flag2 and not SAE_ST.tpSteal then
					flag = false
					pcall(function()
						local character = Core.LP.Character
						flag = character ~= nil and character:GetAttribute("__spoofed") == true
					end)
					if flag then
						pcall(function()
							SAE_ST.notify("Auto Steal: REJOIN needed - your character was changed by an older version of this script.")
						end)
					end
					SAE_ST.tpSteal = true
					local value
					value, tp = pcall(SAE_ST.TP_FACTORY, SAE_ST.tpSettings)
					if value and type(tp) == "table" then
						SAE_ST.TP = tp
					else
						SAE_ST.tpSteal = false
						warn("[Auto Steal] engine failed to start:", tp)
						pcall(function()
							SAE_ST.notify("Auto Steal failed to start: " .. tostring(tp))
						end)
						SAE_ST.tpWanted = false
						flag2 = false
					end
				elseif not flag2 then
					tp2 = SAE_ST.TP
					SAE_ST.TP = nil
					SAE_ST.tpSteal = false
					if tp2 then
						pcall(function()
							tp2:Stop()
						end)
					end
				end
				if SAE_ST.tpWanted == true == flag2 then
					break
				end
			end
			SAE_ST.tpWorker = false
		end)
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while not SAE_ST.stale() do
			task.wait(1)
		end
		local tp = SAE_ST.TP
		SAE_ST.TP = nil
		SAE_ST.tpSteal = false
		if tp then
			pcall(function()
				tp:Stop()
			end)
		end
	end)
	do
		local function safeRequire(tabs)
			if not tabs then
				return nil
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(require, tabs)
			return ok and result or nil
		end

		local riftStateAt = 0
		local riftStateCache

		local function getRiftRemotes()
			local Remotes
			pcall(function()
				Remotes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
			end)
			if Remotes and Remotes.Rift then
				return Remotes.Rift
			end
			return {}
		end

		do
			local function fetchRiftState(arg)
				local now = os.clock()
				if not arg and riftStateCache and now - riftStateAt < 2.5 then
					return riftStateCache
				end
				local riftRemotes = getRiftRemotes()
				if not riftRemotes.AskState then
					return nil
				end
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					return riftRemotes.AskState:InvokeServer()
				end)
				if ok and type(result) == "table" then
					riftStateCache = result
					riftStateAt = now
					return result
				end
				return riftStateCache
			end

			local function matchesRiftBanner(savedRiftBanner, bannerId, savedBannerId)
				local text = string.lower(tostring(savedRiftBanner or "")):gsub("%s+", "")
				local text2 = string.lower(tostring(bannerId or "")):gsub("%s+", "")
				local text3 = string.lower(tostring(savedBannerId or "")):gsub("%s+", "")
				if text == "" or text == "any" then
					return true
				end
				if text == text2 or text == text3 then
					return true
				end
				if (text:find("riftborn") or text:find("verdant")) and (text2:find("verdant") or text3:find("riftborn") or text2:find("riftborn")) then
					return true
				end
				if (text:find("riftbeast") or text:find("umbral")) and (text2:find("umbral") or text3:find("riftbeast") or text2:find("riftbeast")) then
					return true
				end
				if (text:find("shattered") or text:find("radiant")) and (text2:find("radiant") or text3:find("shattered") or text2:find("shattered")) then
					return true
				end
				return false
			end

			local function isRiftBannerAllowed()
				local riftBanners = SAE_ST.riftBanners
				if not riftBanners or #riftBanners == 0 then
					return true
				end
				local riftState = fetchRiftState()
				if not riftState or not riftState.BannerId and not riftState.BannerDisplayName then
					return true
				end
				local bannerId = riftState.BannerId or ""
				local savedBannerId = riftState.BannerDisplayName or bannerId
				for _, savedRiftBanner in ipairs(riftBanners) do
					if matchesRiftBanner(savedRiftBanner, bannerId, savedBannerId) then
						return true
					end
				end
				return false
			end

			local function getRiftDifficulty(arg)
				local RiftRecipes
				pcall(function()
					RiftRecipes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules.RiftRecipes)
				end)
				if RiftRecipes and RiftRecipes.DifficultyOf then
					local ok, result = pcall(RiftRecipes.DifficultyOf, arg)
					if ok and result then
						return result
					end
				end
				return "Medium"
			end

			local function closeRiftGui()
				pcall(function()
					local Tabs = safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.Tabs) or require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.Tabs)
					if Tabs and Tabs.Dismiss then
						Tabs.Dismiss(true)
					end
				end)
				pcall(function()
					local riftTradeIn = Core.LP and Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and Core.LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("RiftTradeIn")
					if riftTradeIn then
						riftTradeIn.Enabled = false
					end
				end)
			end

			local function openRiftGui()
				local ok = pcall(function()
					local Tabs = safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.Tabs) or require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.Tabs)
					if Tabs and Tabs.Show then
						Tabs.Show("RiftTradeIn")
					end
				end)
				local riftTradeIn = Core.LP and Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and Core.LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("RiftTradeIn")
				if riftTradeIn and not riftTradeIn.Enabled then
					riftTradeIn.Enabled = true
				end
			end

			do
				local function setAttribute()
					local riftTradeIn = Core.LP and Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and Core.LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("RiftTradeIn")
					if not riftTradeIn then
						return
					end
					for _, descendant in ipairs(riftTradeIn:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant.Name == "Close" and descendant:IsA("GuiButton") then
							if not descendant:GetAttribute("CloseHooked") then
								descendant:SetAttribute("CloseHooked", true)
								descendant.Activated:Connect(closeRiftGui)
								descendant.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closeRiftGui)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				task.spawn(function()
					task.wait(1)
					setAttribute()
					local playerGui = Core.LP and Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
					if playerGui then
						local riftTradeIn = playerGui:FindFirstChild("RiftTradeIn")
						if riftTradeIn then
							riftTradeIn.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
								if instance.Name == "Close" and instance:IsA("GuiButton") then
									setAttribute()
								end
							end)
						end
						playerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
							if instance.Name == "RiftTradeIn" then
								task.wait(0.1)
								setAttribute()
								instance.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
									if instance.Name == "Close" and instance:IsA("GuiButton") then
										setAttribute()
									end
								end)
							end
						end)
					end
				end)
			end
			local riftSlotCache 
			local riftSlotCacheAt = 0
			local riftSlotCacheKey

			local function computeRiftSlots(requirements, arg)
				local text = table.concat(requirements or {}, "|") .. "|" .. tostring(SAE_ST.riftSacrificeFilter)
				local now = os.clock()
				if not arg and riftSlotCache and now - riftSlotCacheAt < 0.75 and riftSlotCacheKey == text then
					return riftSlotCache.slotUids, riftSlotCache.missingRequirements, riftSlotCache.packedUids
				end
				local require2 = Core.Save or safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save) or safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared:FindFirstChild("Save"))
				local require3 = safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AssetItems)
				local require4 = safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.FuseKernel)
				if not require2 then
					return {}, requirements or {}, {}
				end
				local get = require2.Get and require2.Get()
				if not get then
					return {}, requirements or {}, {}
				end
				local petInventory = get.Inventory or get.PetInventory
				if not petInventory then
					return {}, requirements or {}, {}
				end
				local seen = {}
				for _, equippedAsset in ipairs(get.EquippedAssets or {}) do
					seen[equippedAsset] = true
				end
				local items = {}
				local lookup = {}
				for key, value in pairs(petInventory) do
					local item
					if type(value) == "table" then
						item = value
					elseif require3 and require3.Decode then
						local ok, result = pcall(require3.Decode, value)
						if ok and type(result) == "table" then
							item = result
						end
					end
					if item then
						local UUID = item.Uid or item.Id or item.UUID or key
						local flag = seen[UUID] == true or item.Equipped == true
						local flag2 = item.Locked == true or item.Favorite == true or item.IsFavorite == true
						if not flag and not flag2 then
							local flag3 = true
							if require4 and require4.MayEnterRift then
								local ok, result = pcall(require4.MayEnterRift, UUID, value)
								if not ok or result == false then
									local ok2, result2 = pcall(require4.MayEnterRift, UUID, item)
									if ok2 and result2 == false then
										flag3 = false
									end
								end
							end
							if flag3 then
								local category = item.Category or item.AssetCategory or item.Name or item.ItemData and item.ItemData.Category or ""
								local match = string.lower(tostring(category)):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
								if match and match ~= "" then
									lookup[match] = (lookup[match] or 0) + 1
									local weight = 0
									if require3 and require3.WeightKg then
										local ok, result = pcall(require3.WeightKg, item)
										if ok and type(result) == "number" then
											weight = result
										end
									end
									local mutations = item.Mutations or {}
									local count = type(mutations) == "table" and #mutations or 0
									table.insert(items, {uid = UUID, item = item, cat = match, mutCount = count, weight = weight})
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				table.sort(items, function(info, info2)
					if info.mutCount ~= info2.mutCount then
						return info.mutCount < info2.mutCount
					end
					return info.weight < info2.weight
				end)
				local riftSacrificeFilter = SAE_ST.riftSacrificeFilter or "Non-Mutated Only"
				local slotUids = {}
				local packedUids = {}
				local seen2 = {}
				local missingRequirements = {}
				local lookup2 = {}
				for index, savedRequirement in ipairs(requirements or {}) do
					local match = string.lower(tostring(savedRequirement)):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
					local uid
					for _, info in ipairs(items) do
						if info.cat == match and not seen2[info.uid] then
							local flag = true
							local value = (lookup[info.cat] or 0) - (lookup2[info.cat] or 0)
							if riftSacrificeFilter == "Duplicates Only" and value < 2 then
								flag = false
							elseif riftSacrificeFilter == "Non-Mutated Only" and info.mutCount > 0 then
								flag = false
							end
							if flag then
								uid = info.uid
								lookup2[info.cat] = (lookup2[info.cat] or 0) + 1
								break
							end
						end
					end
					if uid then
						seen2[uid] = true
						slotUids[index] = uid
						table.insert(packedUids, uid)
					else
						slotUids[index] = nil
						table.insert(missingRequirements, savedRequirement)
					end
				end
				riftSlotCache = {slotUids = slotUids, missingRequirements = missingRequirements, packedUids = packedUids}
				riftSlotCacheAt = now
				riftSlotCacheKey = text
				return slotUids, missingRequirements, packedUids
			end

			local riftController
			local riftControllerCheckedAt = 0

			local function getRiftController()
				if riftController then
					local result = debug.getupvalue(riftController, 3)
					if type(result) == "table" then
						return riftController, result
					end
				end
				local now = os.clock()
				if now - riftControllerCheckedAt < 5 then
					return nil, nil
				end
				riftControllerCheckedAt = now
				if not getgc or not debug or not debug.getupvalues then
					return nil, nil
				end
				for _, object in ipairs(getgc(false)) do
					if type(object) == "function" and islclosure and islclosure(object) then
						local info = debug.getinfo(object)
						if info and info.name == "renderSlots" then
							local list = debug.getupvalues(object)
							if #list >= 3 and type(list[3]) == "table" then
								riftController = object
								return riftController, list[3]
							end
						end
					end
				end
				return nil, nil
			end

			local function autoAddMatchingRiftSlots()
				local riftState = fetchRiftState()
				if not riftState or not riftState.Requirements then
					return 0
				end
				local riftController, map = getRiftController()
				if not riftController or not map then
					return 0
				end
				local riftSlots, value = computeRiftSlots(riftState.Requirements)
				local value2 = 0
				local flag = false
				local info = debug.getupvalue(riftController, 1)
				if not info or not info.Requirements then
					pcall(debug.setupvalue, riftController, 1, riftState)
					flag = true
				end
				local require2 = Core.Save or safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save) or safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared:FindFirstChild("Save"))
				local require3 = safeRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AssetItems)
				local get = require2 and require2.Get and require2.Get()
				local petInventory = get and (get.Inventory or get.PetInventory)
				for i = 1, 3 do
					local text = riftState.Requirements[i]
					local savedRiftSlots = riftSlots and riftSlots[i]
					if savedRiftSlots and text and petInventory and petInventory[savedRiftSlots] then
						local value3 = petInventory[savedRiftSlots]
						local instance = type(value3) == "table" and value3 or nil
						if not instance and require3 and require3.Decode then
							local ok, result = pcall(require3.Decode, value3)
							if ok and type(result) == "table" then
								instance = result
							end
						end
						local category = instance and (instance.Category or instance.AssetCategory or instance.Name or instance.ItemData and instance.ItemData.Category) or ""
						local flag2 = string.lower(tostring(category)):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") == string.lower(tostring(text)):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
						if flag2 then
							if not map[i] or map[i].Uid ~= savedRiftSlots or map[i].Category ~= text then
								map[i] = {Uid = savedRiftSlots, Category = text}
								value2 = value2 + 1
								flag = true
							end
						elseif map[i] then
							map[i] = nil
							flag = true
						end
					elseif map[i] then
						local record = map[i]
						local flag2 = false
						if record and record.Uid and petInventory and petInventory[record.Uid] and text then
							local value3 = petInventory[record.Uid]
							local instance = type(value3) == "table" and value3 or nil
							if not instance and require3 and require3.Decode then
								local ok, result = pcall(require3.Decode, value3)
								if ok and type(result) == "table" then
									instance = result
								end
							end
							local category = instance and (instance.Category or instance.AssetCategory or instance.Name or instance.ItemData and instance.ItemData.Category) or ""
							if string.lower(tostring(category)):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") == string.lower(tostring(text)):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") then
								flag2 = true
							end
						end
						if not flag2 then
							map[i] = nil
							flag = true
						end
					end
				end
				if flag then
					pcall(riftController)
				end
				return value2
			end

			local riftTargetsCache
			local riftTargetsAt = 0

			local function getRiftMissingTargets(arg)
				local now = os.clock()
				if not arg and riftTargetsCache and now - riftTargetsAt < 1 then
					return riftTargetsCache
				end
				riftTargetsAt = now
				if not isRiftBannerAllowed() then
					riftTargetsCache = {}
					return riftTargetsCache
				end
				local riftState = fetchRiftState()
				if not riftState or not riftState.Requirements then
					riftTargetsCache = {}
					return riftTargetsCache
				end
				local riftSlots, list = computeRiftSlots(riftState.Requirements)
				if not list or #list == 0 then
					riftTargetsCache = {}
					return riftTargetsCache
				end
				local lookup = {}
				pcall(function()
					local ownerRuntimeRecords = Core.EggCmds and Core.EggCmds.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords(Core.LP.UserId)
					if ownerRuntimeRecords then
						for _, info in pairs(ownerRuntimeRecords) do
							local lowered = string.lower(info.AssetCategory or "")
							lookup[lowered] = (lookup[lowered] or 0) + 1
						end
					end
				end)
				local lookup2 = {}
				for _, item in ipairs(list) do
					local text = type(item) == "table" and (item.Category or item.Name) or tostring(item)
					local lowered = string.lower(text)
					lookup2[lowered] = (lookup2[lowered] or 0) + 1
				end
				local seen = {}
				for key, value in pairs(lookup2) do
					local value2 = lookup[key] or 0
					if value2 < value then
						seen[key] = true
					end
				end
				riftTargetsCache = seen
				return riftTargetsCache
			end

			local function isRiftEggTarget(arg)
				if not arg then
					return false
				end
				local riftMissingTargets = getRiftMissingTargets()
				return riftMissingTargets[string.lower(arg)] == true
			end

			local function isRiftRequiredCategory(arg)
				if not arg then
					return false
				end
				if not isRiftBannerAllowed() then
					return false
				end
				local riftState = fetchRiftState()
				if not riftState or not riftState.Requirements then
					return false
				end
				local lowered = string.lower(arg)
				for _, requirement in ipairs(riftState.Requirements) do
					local text = type(requirement) == "table" and (requirement.Category or requirement.Name) or tostring(requirement)
					if string.lower(text) == lowered then
						return true
					end
				end
				return false
			end

			do
				local function handleRiftDropAction()
					local riftDropAction = SAE_ST.riftDropAction or "Lock Secret & Mythic"
					if riftDropAction == "Keep Everything" then
						return
					end
					local Save
					local AssetItems
					pcall(function()
						Save = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Save)
						AssetItems = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AssetItems)
					end)
					if not Save or not AssetItems then
						return
					end
					local info = Save.Get()
					local inventory = info and info.Inventory
					if not inventory then
						return
					end
					for key, value in pairs(inventory) do
						local ok, info2 = pcall(AssetItems.Decode, value)
						if ok and info2 then
							local category = info2.Category or ""
							local flag = category == "World Eater" or category == "Wendigo"
							if flag and not info2.IsFavorite then
								pcall(function()
									if Core.Network and Core.Network.NET_MAP and Core.Network.NET_MAP.AssetInventory then
										Core.Network.Fire(Core.Network.NET_MAP.AssetInventory.SET_FAVORITE, key, true)
									else
										local Remotes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
										if Remotes and Remotes.PetSatchel and Remotes.PetSatchel.WriteFavourite then
											Remotes.PetSatchel.WriteFavourite:FireServer(key, true)
										end
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				SAE_ST.isRiftBannerAllowed = isRiftBannerAllowed
				SAE_ST.getRiftMissingTargets = getRiftMissingTargets
				SAE_ST.fetchRiftState = fetchRiftState
				SAE_ST.getRiftDifficulty = getRiftDifficulty
				SAE_ST.openRiftGui = openRiftGui
				SAE_ST.closeRiftGui = closeRiftGui
				SAE_ST.autoAddMatchingRiftSlots = autoAddMatchingRiftSlots
				SAE_ST.handleRiftDropAction = handleRiftDropAction
			end
			SAE_ST.isRiftEggTarget = isRiftEggTarget
			SAE_ST.isRiftRequiredCategory = isRiftRequiredCategory
		end
		SAE_ST.getRiftRemotes = getRiftRemotes
	end
	SAE_ST.areaEscapeNeed = {}
	SAE_ST.areaEscapeTold = {}

	function SAE_ST.areaEscapeRequirement(name)
		if name == nil then
			return nil
		end
		local text = tostring(name)
		local value = SAE_ST.areaEscapeNeed[text]
		if value ~= nil then
			return value
		end
		local flag = false
		pcall(function()
			local GuardEscape = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Utils.GuardEscape)
			local instance = Core.World()
			local areas = instance and instance:FindFirstChild("Areas")
			local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
			guardAreas = guardAreas and guardAreas:FindFirstChild(text)
			local child = Core.ReplicatedStorage.Data.Guards.Configs:FindFirstChild(text)
			local module = child and require(child)
			local guard = guardAreas and guardAreas:FindFirstChild("Guard")
			local humanoidRootPart = guard and (guard.PrimaryPart or guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
			local bounds = guardAreas and guardAreas:FindFirstChild("Bounds")
			local closestExitPoint = guardAreas and guardAreas:FindFirstChild("ClosestExitPoint")
			local separationLine = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("SeparationLine")
			if not (type(module) == "table" and humanoidRootPart and bounds and closestExitPoint and separationLine) then
				return
			end
			local value = -separationLine.CFrame.LookVector
			local guardAreas2 = Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
			local exitDistance
			local hitDistance = module.HitDistance
			pcall(function()
				local GuardEscapePrediction = require(guardAreas2.GuardEscapePrediction)
				exitDistance = GuardEscapePrediction.ResolveExitDistance(bounds.CFrame, bounds.Size, closestExitPoint.Position, value)
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local GuardChasePolicy = require(guardAreas2.GuardChasePolicy)
				hitDistance = GuardChasePolicy.ResolveHitDistance(module.HitDistance)
			end)
			if type(exitDistance) ~= "number" then
				return
			end
			local result = GuardEscape.RequiredSpeedPower({
				BaseGuardWalkSpeed = module.WalkSpeed,
				ExitDirection = value,
				ExitDistance = exitDistance,
				FlatRadius = module.FlatRadius,
				GuardStartPosition = humanoidRootPart.Position,
				HitDistance = hitDistance,
				PlayerStartPosition = closestExitPoint.Position,
			})
			if type(result) == "number" then
				flag = result
			end
		end)
		SAE_ST.areaEscapeNeed[text] = flag
		return flag
	end

	SAE_ST._guardScan = {at = 0, byArea = {}}

	function SAE_ST.guardNear(rec)
		if not (rec and rec.AreaId and rec.BottomCFrame) then
			return nil
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		if now - SAE_ST._guardScan.at > 0.5 then
			SAE_ST._guardScan.at = now
			SAE_ST._guardScan.byArea = {}
			pcall(function()
				local instance = Core.World()
				local areas = instance and instance:FindFirstChild("Areas")
				local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
				if not guardAreas then
					return
				end
				for _, child in ipairs(guardAreas:GetChildren()) do
					local guard = child:FindFirstChild("Guard")
					local humanoidRootPart = guard and (guard.PrimaryPart or guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
					if humanoidRootPart then
						SAE_ST._guardScan.byArea[child.Name] = {pos = humanoidRootPart.Position, state = tostring(guard:GetAttribute("GuardState"))}
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		local info = SAE_ST._guardScan.byArea[tostring(rec.AreaId)]
		if not info then
			return nil
		end
		return {
			dist = (info.pos - rec.BottomCFrame.Position).Magnitude,
			state = info.state,
			awake = info.state ~= "Sleeping",
		}
	end

	function SAE_ST.canEscapeArea(name)
		local value = SAE_ST.areaEscapeRequirement(name)
		if value == false or value == nil then
			return true
		end
		local number = 0
		pcall(function()
			number = tonumber(Core.Save.Get().SpeedPower) or 0
		end)
		if number >= value then
			return true
		end
		local text = tostring(name)
		if not SAE_ST.areaEscapeTold[text] then
			SAE_ST.areaEscapeTold[text] = true
			if SAE_ST.notify then
				SAE_ST.notify(("%s skipped - needs %s Speed to outrun its guard, you have %s"):format(text, SAE_ST.fmtNum and SAE_ST.fmtNum(value) or tostring(math.floor(value)), SAE_ST.fmtNum and SAE_ST.fmtNum(number) or tostring(math.floor(number))))
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	SAE_ST.areaFail = {}
	SAE_ST.AREA_FAIL_LIMIT = 4
	SAE_ST.AREA_FAIL_COOLDOWN = 240

	function SAE_ST.areaBlocked(areaId)
		local info = SAE_ST.areaFail[tostring(areaId)]
		if not info then
			return false
		end
		if os.clock() - info.at > SAE_ST.AREA_FAIL_COOLDOWN then
			SAE_ST.areaFail[tostring(areaId)] = nil
			return false
		end
		return info.n >= SAE_ST.AREA_FAIL_LIMIT
	end

	SAE_ST.areaOk = {}

	function SAE_ST.areaProven(areaId)
		return (SAE_ST.areaOk[tostring(areaId)] or 0) > 0
	end

	function SAE_ST.noteAreaResult(areaId, arg)
		if areaId == nil then
			return
		end
		local text = tostring(areaId)
		if arg then
			SAE_ST.areaFail[text] = nil
			SAE_ST.areaOk[text] = (SAE_ST.areaOk[text] or 0) + 1
			return
		end
		local tbl = SAE_ST.areaFail[text] or {n = 0, at = os.clock()}
		local info = tbl
		info.n = info.n + 1
		tbl.at = os.clock()
		SAE_ST.areaFail[text] = tbl
		if tbl.n == SAE_ST.AREA_FAIL_LIMIT and SAE_ST.notify then
			SAE_ST.notify(text .. ": deliveries keep failing here - skipping it for a few minutes")
		end
	end

	function SAE_ST.serverWalkSpeed()
		local value
		pcall(function()
			local treadmillUtil = Core.TreadmillUtil
			local save = Core.Save.Get()
			if treadmillUtil and treadmillUtil.SpeedPowerToWalkSpeed and save then
				value = treadmillUtil.SpeedPowerToWalkSpeed(save.SpeedPower or 0)
			end
		end)
		if type(value) ~= "number" or value ~= value or value < 16 then
			value = 16
		end
		return value
	end

	SAE_ST.STEAL_BAIT = Vector3.new(616, 71, -396)
	SAE_ST.STEAL_WAIT = 6.5
	SAE_ST.STEAL_SPEED = 800

	function SAE_ST.safeZoneCFrame()
		return SAE_ST.customReturnCFrame or CFrame.new(544.4, 70.57, -417)
	end

	function SAE_ST.fastFlyTo(zoneCFrame, _)
		local cframe = typeof(zoneCFrame) == "CFrame" and zoneCFrame or CFrame.new(zoneCFrame)
		local character = getCharacter()
		local instance = hrp()
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not (character and instance) then
			return false
		end

		local function fn()
			return SAE_ST.stale() or not isAlive() or getCharacter() ~= character
		end

		if (cframe.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude < 6 then
			if isAlive() then
				character:PivotTo(cframe)
			end
			return true
		end
		local number = tonumber(SAE_ST.STEAL_SPEED) or 800
		if not down(instance, humanoid, cframe, fn, number, false, false) then
			return false
		end
		if isAlive() and getCharacter() == character then
			character:PivotTo(cframe)
		end
		return true
	end

	function SAE_ST.carryLegTo(cframe, uid)
		local EggState = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)

		local function fn()
			local ok, info = pcall(EggState.ReadFieldEgg, uid)
			if ok and type(info) == "table" then
				return tostring(info.State), info
			end
			return nil, nil
		end

		local function fn2(arg)
			local value = os.clock() + (arg or 3.5)
			while os.clock() < value do
				if SAE_ST.stale() or not isAlive() then
					return false
				end
				local text, info = fn()
				if text == "Carried" then
					return true
				end
				local instance = hrp()
				if instance and info and info.BottomCFrame and (info.BottomCFrame.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
					instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(info.BottomCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
					instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				local flag = false
				pcall(function()
					flag = EggState.CarryFieldEgg(uid) == true
				end)
				if flag then
					return true
				end
				task.wait(0.25)
			end
			return false
		end

		local number = tonumber(SAE_ST.STEAL_SPEED) or 800
		for i = 1, 6 do
			local part = hrp()
			if not (part and isAlive()) then
				return false
			end
			local CFrame2 = part.CFrame
			local distance = (cframe.Position - CFrame2.Position).Magnitude
			if distance < 6 then
				local character = getCharacter()
				if character then
					character:PivotTo(cframe)
				end
				return fn() == "Carried"
			end
			local minValue = 0
			local flag = false
			local value = 0
			while minValue < distance do
				if SAE_ST.stale() or not isAlive() then
					return false
				end
				local result = Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				local part2 = hrp()
				if not part2 then
					return false
				end
				if part2.Anchored then
					part2.Anchored = false
				end
				minValue = math.min(distance, minValue + number * math.min(result, 0.05))
				part2.CFrame = CFrame2:Lerp(cframe, minValue / distance)
				part2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				part2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				value = value + 1
				if value % 3 == 0 and fn() ~= "Carried" then
					flag = true
					break
				end
			end
			if not flag then
				local character = getCharacter()
				if character and isAlive() then
					character:PivotTo(cframe)
				end
				return fn() == "Carried"
			end
			local result = hrp()
			if result then
				result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				result.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			if not fn2(3.5) then
				SAE_ST.carryLegLost = ("lepas di percobaan %d, state=%s"):format(i, tostring(fn()))
				return false
			end
		end
		return fn() == "Carried"
	end

	SAE_ST.fastStat = {calls = 0, flyFail = 0, grabFail = 0, relayFail = 0, bankFail = 0, ok = 0}

	function SAE_ST.fastStealOnce(record)
		if not (record and record.BottomCFrame) then
			return false
		end
		local fastStat = SAE_ST.fastStat
		fastStat.calls = fastStat.calls + 1
		local EggState = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)
		local zoneCFrame = SAE_ST.safeZoneCFrame()
		local cframe = CFrame.new(SAE_ST.STEAL_BAIT)

		local function fn()
			local ok, info = pcall(EggState.ReadFieldEgg, record.Uid)
			if ok and type(info) == "table" then
				return tostring(info.State), info
			end
			return nil, nil
		end

		local function fn2()
			local value = 0
			pcall(function()
				for _ in pairs(Core.Save.Get().EggInventory or {}) do
					value = value + 1
				end
			end)
			return value
		end

		local function fn3()
			if SAE_ST.setImmunity then
				pcall(SAE_ST.setImmunity, false)
			end
		end

		local value = fn2()
		SAE_ST.fastFlyTo(zoneCFrame)
		if not SAE_ST.fastFlyTo(record.BottomCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) then
			local fastStat2 = SAE_ST.fastStat
			fastStat2.flyFail = fastStat2.flyFail + 1
			return false
		end
		local value2 = os.clock() + (tonumber(SAE_ST.STEAL_WAIT) or 6.5)
		while os.clock() < value2 do
			if SAE_ST.stale() or not isAlive() then
				return false
			end
			local result = hrp()
			if result then
				result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				result.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			task.wait(0.1)
		end
		if SAE_ST.setImmunity then
			pcall(SAE_ST.setImmunity, true)
		end
		local flag = false
		local text
		for i = 1, 4 do
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				fn3()
				return false
			end
			local result = hrp()
			if result then
				result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			local flag2 = false
			local value3
			pcall(function()
				local result, value = EggState.CarryFieldEgg(record.Uid, record.NestId)
				flag2, value3 = result == true, value
			end)
			if flag2 then
				flag = true
				break
			end
			text = value3
			if i < 4 then
				task.wait(0.9)
			end
		end
		if not flag then
			fn3()
			local fastStat2 = SAE_ST.fastStat
			fastStat2.grabFail = fastStat2.grabFail + 1
			SAE_ST.grabLastWhy = tostring(text)
			SAE_ST.grabLastArea = tostring(record.AreaId)
			return false
		end
		local result = SAE_ST.carryLegTo(cframe, record.Uid)
		fn3()
		if not result then
			local fastStat2 = SAE_ST.fastStat
			fastStat2.relayFail = fastStat2.relayFail + 1
			SAE_ST.relayLastWhy = SAE_ST.carryLegLost or "lost in transit, state=" .. tostring(fn())
			SAE_ST.noteAreaResult(record.AreaId, false)
			return false
		end
		pcall(function()
			if SAE_ST.ITS and SAE_ST.ITS.dropIsOurs then
				SAE_ST.ITS.dropIsOurs()
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			return EggState.DropFieldEgg("PlayerRequest")
		end)
		if SAE_ST.setImmunity then
			pcall(SAE_ST.setImmunity, true)
		end
		SAE_ST.fastFlyTo(zoneCFrame)
		SAE_ST.fastFlyTo(cframe)
		local value3 = os.clock() + (tonumber(SAE_ST.STEAL_WAIT) or 6.5)
		while os.clock() < value3 do
			if SAE_ST.stale() or not isAlive() then
				fn3()
				return false
			end
			local result2 = hrp()
			if result2 then
				result2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				result2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			task.wait(0.1)
		end
		local flag2 = false
		local text2
		for i = 1, 16 do
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				fn3()
				return false
			end
			pcall(function()
				local result, info = fn()
				local instance = hrp()
				if instance and info and info.BottomCFrame and (info.BottomCFrame.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
					instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(info.BottomCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
					instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local result, value = EggState.CarryFieldEgg(record.Uid, record.NestId)
				flag2, text2 = result == true, value
			end)
			if flag2 then
				break
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
		if not flag2 then
			fn3()
			local fastStat2 = SAE_ST.fastStat
			fastStat2.relayFail = fastStat2.relayFail + 1
			SAE_ST.relayLastWhy = tostring(text2)
			SAE_ST.noteAreaResult(record.AreaId, false)
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST.carryLegTo(zoneCFrame, record.Uid)
		fn3()
		local value4 = os.clock() + 8
		while os.clock() < value4 do
			if fn2() > value then
				local fastStat2 = SAE_ST.fastStat
				fastStat2.ok = fastStat2.ok + 1
				SAE_ST.noteAreaResult(record.AreaId, true)
				return true
			end
			local result2 = hrp()
			if result2 then
				result2.CFrame = zoneCFrame
				result2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
		local fastStat2 = SAE_ST.fastStat
		fastStat2.bankFail = fastStat2.bankFail + 1
		SAE_ST.noteAreaResult(record.AreaId, false)
		return false
	end

	local function sort()
		local freshEggSnapshot = getFreshEggSnapshot()
		local set = toSet(SAE_ST.stealNames)
		local set2 = toSet(SAE_ST.minRarityNames)
		local set3 = toSet(SAE_ST.stealAreas)
		local results = {}
		local now = os.clock()
		local collectCooldown = SAE_ST.collectCooldown or {}
		for key, value in pairs(collectCooldown) do
			if now - value > 10 then
				collectCooldown[key] = nil
			end
		end
		local isRiftBannerAllowed = SAE_ST.autoRift and SAE_ST.riftMode == "Full Auto (Steal & Trade)" and SAE_ST.isRiftBannerAllowed()
		local riftMissingTargets = isRiftBannerAllowed and SAE_ST.getRiftMissingTargets() or nil
		for _, rec in ipairs(freshEggSnapshot.Records) do
			local flag = rec.State ~= "Claimed" and rec.State ~= "Carried" and not rec.CarrierUserId
			if flag and set3 and not set3[tostring(rec.AreaId)] then
				flag = false
			end
			if flag and SAE_ST.areaBlocked(rec.AreaId) then
				flag = false
			end
			if flag then
				local guardNear = SAE_ST.guardNear and SAE_ST.guardNear(rec)
				if guardNear and guardNear.awake and guardNear.dist < 90 then
					flag = false
				end
			end
			if flag and SAE_ST.bait and SAE_ST.bait.skip and SAE_ST.bait.skip(rec.AreaId) then
				flag = false
			end
			if flag and rec.BottomCFrame and not tostring(rec.Uid):find("FirstAreaEgg") then
				if not collectCooldown[rec.Uid] then
					local rarityInfo, value = getRarityInfo(rec.AssetCategory)
					local lowered = string.lower(rec.AssetCategory or "")
					local savedRiftMissingTargets = riftMissingTargets and riftMissingTargets[lowered] == true or false
					if savedRiftMissingTargets or SAE_ST.autoSteal and matchesSelection(rec, value, set, set2) then
						table.insert(results, {rec = rec, rnum = rarityInfo, mut = #(rec.Mutations or {}), rift = savedRiftMissingTargets})
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local selectBy = SAE_ST.selectBy
		if selectBy == "Value" then
			table.sort(results, function(info, info2)
				return getEggValue(info.rec) > getEggValue(info2.rec)
			end)
		elseif selectBy == "Weight" then
			table.sort(results, function(info, info2)
				return getEggScore(info.rec) > getEggScore(info2.rec)
			end)
		elseif selectBy == "Mutation" then
			table.sort(results, function(info, info2)
				if info.mut == info2.mut then
					return info.rnum > info2.rnum
				end
				return info.mut > info2.mut
			end)
		elseif SAE_ST.valueTargeting then
			table.sort(results, function(info, info2)
				local value = info.rnum * 1000 + info.mut
				local value2 = info2.rnum * 1000 + info2.mut
				if value == value2 then
					return info.mut > info2.mut
				end
				return value > value2
			end)
		else
			local zero = hrp() and hrp().Position or Vector3.zero
			for _, info in ipairs(results) do
				info.dist = info.rec.BottomCFrame and (info.rec.BottomCFrame.Position - zero).Magnitude or math.huge
			end
			local flag = false
			for _, info in ipairs(results) do
				info.proven = SAE_ST.areaProven(info.rec.AreaId) and 1 or 0
				if info.proven == 1 then
					flag = true
				end
			end
			table.sort(results, function(info, info2)
				if info.rnum == info2.rnum then
					return info.dist < info2.dist
				end
				return info.rnum > info2.rnum
			end)
		end
		return results
	end

	do
		local DETOUR_OFFSETS = {20, -20, 35, -35, 50, -50, 70, -70, 100, -100, 140, -140, 190, -190}
		local wallRayParams

		local function getWallRayParams()
			if wallRayParams then
				return wallRayParams
			end
			local instance = Core.World()
			if not instance then
				return nil
			end
			local filterDescendantsInstances = {}
			for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name ~= "Areas" then
					table.insert(filterDescendantsInstances, child)
				end
			end
			if #filterDescendantsInstances == 0 then
				return nil
			end
			local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
			rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
			rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterDescendantsInstances
			rayParams.IgnoreWater = true
			wallRayParams = rayParams
			return rayParams
		end

		local function segmentHitsWall(vector2, vector3)
			local wallRayParams = getWallRayParams()
			if not wallRayParams then
				return nil
			end
			local vector4 = vector3 - vector2
			if vector4.Magnitude < 0.05 then
				return nil
			end
			return Core.Workspace:Raycast(vector2, vector4, wallRayParams)
		end

		local function isPathClear(list)
			for i = 1, #list - 1 do
				if segmentHitsWall(list[i], list[i + 1]) then
					return false
				end
			end
			return true
		end

		local function findDetourAroundWall(position, position2)
			if not segmentHitsWall(position, position2) then
				return nil
			end
			local vector2 = Vector3.new(position.X, position.Y, position2.Z)
			if isPathClear({position, vector2, position2}) then
				return {CFrame.new(vector2)}
			end
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(position2.X, position.Y, position.Z)
			if isPathClear({position, vector3, position2}) then
				return {CFrame.new(vector3)}
			end
			local vector4 = Vector3.new(position2.X - position.X, 0, position2.Z - position.Z)
			if vector4.Magnitude < 0.1 then
				return nil
			end
			local vector5 = Vector3.new(-vector4.Unit.Z, 0, vector4.Unit.X)
			local vector6 = Vector3.new(position2.X, position.Y, position2.Z)
			for _, value in ipairs(DETOUR_OFFSETS) do
				local value2 = position + vector5 * value
				local value3 = vector6 + vector5 * value
				if isPathClear({position, value2, value3, position2}) then
					return {CFrame.new(value2), CFrame.new(value3)}
				end
			end
			return nil
		end

		function SAE_ST.getAdaptiveSpeed(number)
			if not SAE_ST.fpsGovern then
				return number
			end
			local result = Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			local value = 1 / math.max(result, 0.001)
			local clamped = math.clamp(value / 60, 0.35, 1.25)
			return math.max(16, number * clamped)
		end

		function SAE_ST.realWalkMoveTo(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, arg, callback)
			local character = getCharacter()
			local instance = hrp()
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not (character and instance and humanoid) then
				return false
			end
			for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("BasePart") then
					child.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(1, 0.5, 0, 1, 100)
					if child.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and child.Name ~= "LowerTorso" then
						child.CanCollide = false
					end
				end
			end
			humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
			humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
			local savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame = typeof(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame) == "CFrame" and savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Position or savedMyPlotSafeCFrame
			local number = tonumber(SAE_ST.realWalkSpeed) or tonumber(arg) or 250
			number = math.clamp(number, 16, 1500)
			number = SAE_ST.getAdaptiveSpeed(number)
			local detourAroundWall = findDetourAroundWall(instance.Position, savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame)
			local items = {}
			if detourAroundWall then
				for _, instance2 in ipairs(detourAroundWall) do
					table.insert(items, instance2.Position)
				end
			end
			table.insert(items, savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame)
			local distance = (savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame - instance.Position).Magnitude
			local value = os.clock() + math.max(6, distance / 10)
			local position = instance.Position
			local now = os.clock()
			local count = #items
			for index, vector2 in ipairs(items) do
				local flag = index == count
				local value2 = flag and 3.2 or 5.5
				while isAlive() and (Vector3.new(instance.Position.X, 0, instance.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(vector2.X, 0, vector2.Z)).Magnitude > value2 do
					if callback and callback() then
						instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						return false
					end
					if not SAE_ST.autoSteal and not (myCarriedEgg ~= nil and myCarriedEgg()) then
						instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						return false
					end
					if os.clock() >= value then
						break
					end
					local distance2 = (Vector3.new(instance.Position.X, 0, instance.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame.X, 0, savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Z)).Magnitude
					local savedNumber = number
					if flag or distance2 < 80 then
						if distance2 <= 8 then
							savedNumber = 22
						elseif distance2 <= 24 then
							savedNumber = 38
						elseif distance2 <= 80 then
							savedNumber = math.clamp(distance2 * 2.8, 55, number)
						end
					end
					humanoid.WalkSpeed = savedNumber
					humanoid:MoveTo(vector2)
					if instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y > 5 then
						instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
					end
					if (instance.Position - position).Magnitude > 1.2 then
						position = instance.Position
						now = os.clock()
					elseif os.clock() - now >= 0.4 then
						humanoid.Jump = true
						now = os.clock()
					end
					Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end
			end
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = SAE_ST.serverWalkSpeed and SAE_ST.serverWalkSpeed() or 16
			humanoid:MoveTo(instance.Position)
			instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			return (instance.Position - savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame).Magnitude <= 15
		end
	end

	function SAE_ST.flyMoveTo(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, arg, callback, arg2, _)
		local character = getCharacter()
		local instance = hrp()
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not (character and instance and humanoid) then
			return false
		end
		local savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame = typeof(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame) == "CFrame" and savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Position or savedMyPlotSafeCFrame
		local distance = (savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame - instance.Position).Magnitude
		if distance < 6 then
			character:PivotTo(typeof(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame) == "CFrame" and savedMyPlotSafeCFrame or CFrame.new(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame))
			return true
		end
		local number = tonumber(arg) or tonumber(SAE_ST.flySpeed) or tonumber(SAE_ST.tweenSpeed) or 450
		number = SAE_ST.getAdaptiveSpeed(number)
		local clamped = math.clamp(tonumber(SAE_ST.flyHeight) or 90, 20, 300)
		local value = math.max(instance.Position.Y, savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Y) + clamped
		local cframe = CFrame.new(instance.Position.X, value, instance.Position.Z)
		local cframe2 = CFrame.new(savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame.X, value, savedSavedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Z)

		local function fn()
			if not SAE_ST.autoSteal and not arg2 and not (myCarriedEgg ~= nil and myCarriedEgg()) then
				return true
			end
			if callback and callback() then
				return true
			end
			return false
		end

		if math.abs(instance.Position.Y - value) > 4 then
			if not down(instance, humanoid, cframe, fn, number * 0.9, true, false) then
				return false
			end
		end
		if not down(instance, humanoid, cframe2, fn, number, false, false) then
			return false
		end
		local cframe3 = typeof(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame) == "CFrame" and savedMyPlotSafeCFrame or CFrame.new(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame)
		if not down(instance, humanoid, cframe3, fn, number, false, true) then
			return false
		end
		if isAlive() and getCharacter() == character then
			character:PivotTo(cframe3)
		end
		return true
	end

	local function tweenToCFrame(cframe, floored, arg, arg2)
		local character = getCharacter()
		if not character then
			return false
		end
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
			humanoid.Sit = false
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
		local position = cframe.Position
		if (position - instance.Position).Magnitude < 6 then
			character:PivotTo(cframe)
			return true
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			requestStreamAround(position, 3)
		end)

		local function fn()
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				return true
			end
			if not isAlive() or getCharacter() ~= character then
				return true
			end
			if arg and (instance.Position - position).Magnitude <= 12 then
				return true
			end
			return false
		end

		if SAE_ST.movementMode == "Real WalkSpeed" then
			return SAE_ST.realWalkMoveTo(cframe, floored, fn)
		elseif SAE_ST.movementMode == "Fly" or SAE_ST.movementMode == "Semi Teleport" then
			return SAE_ST.flyMoveTo(cframe, floored, fn, arg, arg2)
		end
		local tweenSpeed = resolveTweenSpeed(tonumber(floored) or nil)
		tweenSpeed = SAE_ST.getAdaptiveSpeed and SAE_ST.getAdaptiveSpeed(tweenSpeed) or tweenSpeed
		if not down(instance, humanoid, cframe, fn, tweenSpeed, true, not arg2) then
			return false
		end
		if isAlive() and getCharacter() == character then
			character:PivotTo(cframe)
		end
		return true
	end

	local function flyTo(cframe, tweenSpeed)
		local cframe2 = typeof(cframe) == "CFrame" and cframe or CFrame.new(cframe)
		return tweenToCFrame(cframe2, tweenSpeed, false)
	end

	SAE_ST.flyTo = flyTo

	local function glideTo(arg, arg2)
		local cframe = typeof(arg) == "CFrame" and arg or CFrame.new(arg)
		return tweenToCFrame(cframe, arg2, true)
	end

	local function travelTo(treadmillPart, tweenSpeed)
		local cframe = typeof(treadmillPart) == "CFrame" and treadmillPart or CFrame.new(treadmillPart)
		local character = getCharacter()
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return tweenToCFrame(cframe, tweenSpeed, false)
		end
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local position = instance.Position
		local position2 = cframe.Position
		if (Vector3.new(position2.X, 0, position2.Z) - Vector3.new(position.X, 0, position.Z)).Magnitude < 25 then
			return tweenToCFrame(cframe, tweenSpeed, false)
		end
		pcall(function()
			requestStreamAround(position2, 2)
		end)
		local path = game:GetService("PathfindingService"):CreatePath({AgentRadius = 5, AgentHeight = 6, AgentCanJump = false, WaypointSpacing = 16})
		local ok = pcall(function()
			path:ComputeAsync(position, position2)
		end)
		if not ok or path.Status ~= Enum.PathStatus.Success then
			return tweenToCFrame(cframe, tweenSpeed, false)
		end
		local waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
		if #waypoints < 2 then
			return tweenToCFrame(cframe, tweenSpeed, false)
		end
		local value = position2.Y + 3
		local overlapParams = OverlapParams.new()
		overlapParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
		local instance2 = Core.World()
		overlapParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = instance2 and instance2:FindFirstChild("Build") and {instance2.Build} or {}
		local vector2 = Vector3.new(6, 3, 6)

		local function fn(x, z, x2, z2)
			local vector3 = Vector3.new(x, value, z)
			local vector4 = Vector3.new(x2 - x, 0, z2 - z)
			local magnitude = vector4.Magnitude
			local maxValue = math.max(1, math.floor(magnitude / 6))
			for i = 0, maxValue do
				local value2 = vector3 + vector4 * (i / maxValue)
				for _, part in ipairs(Core.Workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(CFrame.new(value2), vector2, overlapParams)) do
					if part.CanCollide then
						return true
					end
				end
			end
			return false
		end

		local function fn2()
			return not isAlive() or getCharacter() ~= character or not instance.Parent
		end

		local x = position.X
		local z = position.Z
		local flag = true

		local function fn3(x2, z2)
			if fn2() then
				return false
			end
			down(instance, humanoid, CFrame.new(x2, value, z2), fn2, tweenSpeed, flag, false)
			flag = false
			x, z = x2, z2
			return true
		end

		for i = 2, #waypoints do
			if fn2() then
				return false
			end
			local x2 = waypoints[i].Position.X
			local z2 = waypoints[i].Position.Z
			if not fn(x, z, x2, z) and not fn(x2, z, x2, z2) then
				if not fn3(x2, z) then
					return false
				end
				if not fn3(x2, z2) then
					return false
				end
			elseif not fn(x, z, x, z2) and not fn(x, z2, x2, z2) then
				if not fn3(x, z2) then
					return false
				end
				if not fn3(x2, z2) then
					return false
				end
			elseif not fn3(x2, z2) then
				return false
			end
		end
		return tweenToCFrame(cframe, tweenSpeed, false)
	end

	do
		local carryTrack
		local carryTrackCharacter
		local lastRootPosition

		local function stopCarryAnimation()
			if carryTrack then
				pcall(function()
					carryTrack:Stop(0.15)
				end)
			end
			carryTrack, carryTrackCharacter = nil, nil
		end

		local function playCarryAnimation(character)
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
			if not animator then
				return
			end
			if carryTrack and carryTrackCharacter == character and carryTrack.IsPlaying then
				return
			end
			stopCarryAnimation()
			local animationId = "rbxassetid://913376220"
			local animate = character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
			local walk = animate and (animate:FindFirstChild("run") or animate:FindFirstChild("walk"))
			local animation = walk and walk:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation")
			if animation and animation.AnimationId ~= "" then
				animationId = animation.AnimationId
			end
			local animation2 = Instance.new("Animation")
			animation2.AnimationId = animationId
			local ok, instance = pcall(function()
				return animator:LoadAnimation(animation2)
			end)
			if ok and instance then
				instance.Looped = true
				pcall(function()
					instance.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
				end)
				pcall(function()
					instance:Play(0.1)
				end)
				carryTrack, carryTrackCharacter = instance, character
			end
		end

		local CARRY_ANIM_BASE_SPEED = 24
		Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(arg)
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				return
			end
			if SAE_ST.moveStyle ~= "Walk" then
				if carryTrack then
					stopCarryAnimation()
				end
				lastRootPosition = nil
				return
			end
			local character = getCharacter()
			local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart then
				stopCarryAnimation()
				lastRootPosition = nil
				return
			end
			local position = humanoidRootPart.Position
			local distance = lastRootPosition and (position - lastRootPosition).Magnitude or 0
			lastRootPosition = position
			if distance > 0.15 then
				playCarryAnimation(character)
				if carryTrack then
					local value = distance / math.max(arg, 0.004166666666666667)
					local clamped = math.clamp(value / CARRY_ANIM_BASE_SPEED, 0.9, 1.6)
					pcall(function()
						carryTrack:AdjustSpeed(clamped)
					end)
				end
			else
				stopCarryAnimation()
			end
		end)
	end

	local function dropHeldEgg()
		pcall(function()
			Core.EggCmds.RequestDropHeldAreaEgg("PlayerRequest")
		end)
	end

	end)
	pcall(function()

	end)
	local SPEED_PROFILES = {

		},

	local activeProfileName = "Default"
	lookup = {}
	do
		local function getGroundOffset()
			local character = getCharacter()
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local hipHeight = humanoid and humanoid.HipHeight and humanoid.HipHeight > 0 and humanoid.HipHeight or 2
			local size = humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Size and humanoidRootPart.Size.Y > 0 and humanoidRootPart.Size.Y / 2 or 1
			return hipHeight + size
		end

		SAE_ST.customReturnCFrame = CFrame.new(544.40094, 70.5743103, -417, 0.0320008546, -1.19248071e-07, -0.999487817, -1.76635826e-08, 1, -1.19874713e-07, 0.999487817, 2.14906297e-08, 0.0320008546)

		function getMyPlotSafeCFrame()
			if SAE_ST.customReturnCFrame then
				return SAE_ST.customReturnCFrame
			end
			local plot
			pcall(function()
				local PlotState = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.PlotState)
				plot = PlotState.ResolvePlot(Core.LP)
			end)
			local groundOffset = getGroundOffset()
			return CFrame.new(544.40094, 70.5743103, -417)
		end
	end
	local myPlotSafeCFrame = getMyPlotSafeCFrame()

	local function applyProfile(activeProfileName2)
		local list = SPEED_PROFILES[activeProfileName2]
		if not list then
			return
		end
		activeProfileName = activeProfileName2
		for key, item in pairs(list) do
			lookup[key] = item
		end
		myPlotSafeCFrame = getMyPlotSafeCFrame()
	end

	applyProfile("Default")

	local function setBaseTweenSpeed(arg)
		local clamped = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(arg) or 250), 50, 5000)
		SAE_ST.tweenSpeed = clamped
		SPEED_PROFILES.Default.GlideSpeed = clamped
		SPEED_PROFILES.Default.ReturnSpeed = clamped
		SPEED_PROFILES.Default.ChunkSize = clamped
		SPEED_PROFILES.Default.TouchingSpeed = math.max(120, math.floor(clamped * 0.6))
		SPEED_PROFILES.Default.Name = string.format("Default Safe (%d Speed)", clamped)
		local savedClamped = clamped
		SPEED_PROFILES.Turbo.GlideSpeed = savedClamped
		SPEED_PROFILES.Turbo.ReturnSpeed = savedClamped
		SPEED_PROFILES.Turbo.ChunkSize = savedClamped
		SPEED_PROFILES.Turbo.TouchingSpeed = math.max(120, math.floor(savedClamped * 0.53))
		SPEED_PROFILES.Turbo.Name = string.format("Turbo High-Speed (%d Speed)", savedClamped)
		applyProfile(activeProfileName)
	end

	_G.SetCapFPS = {
