	local APPROACH_STANDOFF = 30

	local function approachTarget(arg, callback)
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		local savedMyPlotSafeCFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame

		local function fn()
			if not isAlive() then
				return true
			end
			if callback and callback() then
				return true
			end
			return false
		end

		if SAE_ST.movementMode == "Real WalkSpeed" then
			return SAE_ST.realWalkMoveTo(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, arg, fn)
		elseif SAE_ST.movementMode == "Fly" then
			return SAE_ST.flyMoveTo(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, arg, fn, true, false)
		end
		local position = instance.Position
		local position2 = savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Position
		local value = position2.X - position.X
		local value2 = position2.Z - position.Z
		local root = math.sqrt(value * value + value2 * value2)
		if root > APPROACH_STANDOFF then
			local value3 = value / root
			local value4 = value2 / root
			local cframe = CFrame.new(position2.X - value3 * APPROACH_STANDOFF, position2.Y, position2.Z - value4 * APPROACH_STANDOFF)
			local humanoid = getCharacter() and getCharacter():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid then
				local result = down(instance, humanoid, cframe, fn, arg, false, true)
				if not result or fn() then
					local result2 = hrp()
					if result2 then
						result2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						result2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end
					return false
				end
			else
				local result = tweenToCFrame(cframe, arg, false, true)
				if not result or fn() then
					local result2 = hrp()
					if result2 then
						result2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						result2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end
					return false
				end
			end
			if fn() then
				return false
			end
		end
		local humanoid = getCharacter() and getCharacter():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid then
			local result = down(instance, humanoid, savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, fn, arg, false, true)
			if not result or fn() then
				local result2 = hrp()
				if result2 then
					result2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					result2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				return false
			end
			return result
		else
			return tweenToCFrame(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, arg, true)
		end
	end

	local function isNearPlot()
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		return (instance.Position - myPlotSafeCFrame.Position).Magnitude <= 15
	end

	local function returnHome(arg, myCarriedEgg2)
		myPlotSafeCFrame = getMyPlotSafeCFrame()
		if (SAE_ST.movementMode == "Teleport" or SAE_ST.movementMode == "Semi Teleport") and myPlotSafeCFrame then
			local character = getCharacter()
			local result = hrp()
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if character and result then
				character:PivotTo(myPlotSafeCFrame)
				result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				result.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				if humanoid then
					humanoid.PlatformStand = false
					humanoid.Sit = false
					pcall(function()
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
					end)
				end
			end
			return true
		end
		local reGrab = SAE_ST.ReGrab
		local value = 6
		local value2 = 0
		while value2 < value do
			value2 = value2 + 1
			if isNearPlot() then
				return true
			end
			local flag = false
			local value3 = 0
			local now = os.clock()

			local function fn()
				if not isAlive() then
					return true
				end
				if not myCarriedEgg2 then
					return false
				end
				if os.clock() - now < 0.35 then
					return false
				end
				if reGrab.getMyCarriedEgg() ~= myCarriedEgg2 then
					value3 = value3 + 1
					if value3 >= 4 then
						flag = true
						return true
					end
				else
					value3 = 0
				end
				return false
			end

			local result = approachTarget(arg, fn)
			if flag or myCarriedEgg2 and reGrab.getMyCarriedEgg() ~= myCarriedEgg2 then
				local result2 = hrp()
				if result2 then
					result2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					result2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				reGrab.recoverPlayerState()
				task.wait(0.08)
				local flag2 = reGrab.execute(myCarriedEgg2, 3.5)
				if not flag2 then
					local myCarriedEgg3 = reGrab.getMyCarriedEgg()
					if myCarriedEgg3 then
						myCarriedEgg2 = myCarriedEgg3
						flag2 = true
					end
				end
				if not flag2 then
					return false
				end
				local result3 = hrp()
				if result3 then
					result3.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					result3.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
			elseif result or isNearPlot() then
				return true
			end
		end
		return isNearPlot()
	end

	SAE_ST.returnHome = returnHome
	do
		local function tryRequire(arg)
			if not arg then
				return nil
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(require, arg)
			return ok and result or nil
		end

		local require2 = tryRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared:FindFirstChild("Remotes"))
		local require3 = tryRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Client") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client:FindFirstChild("EggState"))
		local require4 = tryRequire(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared:FindFirstChild("Modules") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Modules:FindFirstChild("RagdollJoints"))
		do
			local askFieldEggCarry2 = require2 and require2.EggWorld and require2.EggWorld.AskFieldEggCarry
			if not askFieldEggCarry2 then
				local networking = Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Packages:FindFirstChild("Networking")
				askFieldEggCarry2 = networking and (networking:FindFirstChild("RF/EggWorld/AskFieldEggCarry") or networking:FindFirstChild("Eggs: RequestAreaEggCarry"))
			end 
		end
		local writeLimpness = require2 and require2.Limpness and require2.Limpness.WriteLimpness
		if not writeLimpness then
			local networking = Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Packages:FindFirstChild("Networking")
			writeLimpness = networking and networking:FindFirstChild("RE/Limpness/WriteLimpness")
		end
		local reGrab = {}

		function reGrab.recoverPlayerState()
			pcall(function()
				local character = getCharacter()
				if require4 and require4.Release and character then
					require4.Release(character)
				end
				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if humanoid then
					humanoid.PlatformStand = false
					humanoid.Sit = false
					humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp, true)
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				end
				if writeLimpness then
					if writeLimpness:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
						writeLimpness:FireServer(false)
					elseif writeLimpness:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
						writeLimpness:InvokeServer(false)
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		function reGrab.getMyCarriedEgg()
			if Core.carriedUid then
				return Core.carriedUid
			end
			local areaEggSnapshot
			if Core.EggCmds and Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot then
				pcall(function()
					areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
				end)
			end
			if (not areaEggSnapshot or not areaEggSnapshot.Records) and require3 and require3.ReadFieldEggs then
				pcall(function()
					areaEggSnapshot = require3.ReadFieldEggs()
				end)
			end
			if areaEggSnapshot and areaEggSnapshot.Records then
				local userId = Core.LP and Core.LP.UserId
				for _, record in ipairs(areaEggSnapshot.Records) do
					if record.State == "Carried" and record.CarrierUserId == userId then
						return record.Uid
					end
				end
			end
			return nil
		end

		function reGrab.getEggDropPosition(name)
			local pivot
			if Core.EggCmds and Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord then
				pcall(function()
					local areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(name)
					if areaEggRecord then
						pivot = areaEggRecord.BottomCFrame or areaEggRecord.BoundsCFrame
					end
				end)
			end
			if not pivot and require3 and require3.ReadFieldEgg then
				pcall(function()
					local fieldEgg = require3.ReadFieldEgg(name)
					if fieldEgg then
						pivot = fieldEgg.BottomCFrame or fieldEgg.BoundsCFrame
					end
				end)
			end
			if not pivot then
				local areaEggSlotsClient = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient")
				local child = areaEggSlotsClient and areaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(tostring(name))
				if child then
					pivot = child:GetPivot()
				end
			end
			if pivot then
				return typeof(pivot) == "CFrame" and pivot.Position or pivot
			end
			return nil
		end

		function reGrab.execute(uid, amount)
			local humanoid
			if not uid then
				return false
			end
			if reGrab.getMyCarriedEgg() == uid then
				return true
			end
			amount = amount or 3.5
			local now = os.clock()
			reGrab.recoverPlayerState()
			local instance = hrp()
			if instance then
				instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end

			local function fn(arg)
				local result = hrp()
				if not result then
					return
				end
				result.Anchored = false
				result.CFrame = CFrame.new(arg)
				result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				result.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end

			local eggDropPosition = reGrab.getEggDropPosition(uid)
			if not eggDropPosition and instance then
				eggDropPosition = instance.Position
			end
			if not eggDropPosition then
				return false
			end
			fn(eggDropPosition + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0))
			local flag = false
			local flag2 = false
			local now2 = 0
			local now3 = os.clock()
			local maxValue = now + amount
			while os.clock() < maxValue do
				if reGrab.getMyCarriedEgg() == uid then
					break
				end
				if os.clock() - now3 >= 0.5 then
					now3 = os.clock()
					local eggDropPosition2 = reGrab.getEggDropPosition(uid)
					if eggDropPosition2 and (eggDropPosition2 - eggDropPosition).Magnitude > 6 then
						eggDropPosition = eggDropPosition2
						flag = false
						reGrab.recoverPlayerState()
						fn(eggDropPosition + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0))
					end
				end
				local result = hrp()
				if result and not flag then
					result.Anchored = true
					flag = true
				end
				if os.clock() - now2 >= 0.1 then
					now2 = os.clock()
					local value
					local text
					if Core.EggCmds and Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg then
						value, text = Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(uid)
					elseif require3 and require3.CarryFieldEgg then
						value, text = require3.CarryFieldEgg(uid)
					end
					if value == true then
						break
					end
					local lowered = type(text) == "string" and text:lower() or ""
					if lowered:find("already carrying") then
						break
					elseif lowered:find("knocked down") then
						if not flag2 then
							flag2 = true
							maxValue = math.max(maxValue, os.clock() + 4)
						end
						reGrab.recoverPlayerState()
						local part = hrp()
						if part and part.Anchored then
							part.Anchored = false
							flag = false
						end
						humanoid = getCharacter() and getCharacter():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if humanoid then
							pcall(function()
								humanoid.PlatformStand = false
								humanoid.Sit = false
								humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
							end)
						end
						task.wait(0.35)
					end
				end
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
			local part = hrp()
			if part and part.Anchored then
				part.Anchored = false
			end
			return reGrab.getMyCarriedEgg() == uid
		end

		function reGrab.createCarryWatcher(uid, callback)
			local value = 0
			local now = os.clock()
			return function()
				if os.clock() - now < 0.5 then
					return false
				end
				if reGrab.getMyCarriedEgg() ~= uid then
					value = value + 1
					if value >= 8 then
						value = 0
						if callback then
							return callback(uid)
						else
							return reGrab.execute(uid, 3.5)
						end
					end
				else
					value = 0
				end
				return false
			end
		end

		SAE_ST.ReGrab = reGrab
	end

	local function getMyCarriedEgg()
		return SAE_ST.ReGrab.getMyCarriedEgg()
	end

	local function recoverDroppedEgg(arg, amount)
		return SAE_ST.ReGrab.execute(arg, amount and amount * 1.5 or 3.5)
	end

	SAE_ST.recoverDroppedEgg = recoverDroppedEgg
	local HOME_RADIUS = 60

	local function moveTo(uid)
		local instance, areaEggRecord
		local instance2 = hrp()
		if not instance2 then
			return false
		end
		local character = getCharacter()
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		myPlotSafeCFrame = getMyPlotSafeCFrame()
		if SAE_ST.movementMode ~= "Teleport" and SAE_ST.movementMode ~= "Semi Teleport" and (instance2.Position - myPlotSafeCFrame.Position).Magnitude > HOME_RADIUS then
			return false
		end
		if SAE_ST.movementMode == "Real WalkSpeed" and humanoid then
			humanoid:MoveTo(myPlotSafeCFrame.Position)
		else
			instance2.CFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
			pcall(function()
				instance2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
		end
		local flag = false
		local connection
		pcall(function()
			connection = Core.EggCmds.AreaEggCarryStateChanged:Connect(function(info)
				if info and info.IsCarrying == false then
					flag = true
				end
			end)
		end)
		local value = os.clock() + 2
		repeat
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			if flag then
				break
			end
			instance = hrp()
			if instance and (instance.Position - myPlotSafeCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
				instance.CFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
				pcall(function()
					instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end)
			end
			if not getMyCarriedEgg() then
				areaEggRecord = nil
				pcall(function()
					areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(uid)
				end)
				if areaEggRecord and (areaEggRecord.State == "Claimed" or areaEggRecord.State == "Slot") then
					break
				end
			end
		until os.clock() >= value
		if connection then
			pcall(function()
				connection:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		return getMyCarriedEgg() == nil
	end

	local function unequipTools()
		local myCarriedEgg2 = getMyCarriedEgg()
		if not myCarriedEgg2 then
			return false
		end
		returnHome(lookup.ReturnSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed or 300, myCarriedEgg2)
		if not getMyCarriedEgg() or getMyCarriedEgg() ~= myCarriedEgg2 then
			if not SAE_ST.ReGrab.execute(myCarriedEgg2, 3.5) then
				return false
			end
			returnHome(lookup.ReturnSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed or 300, myCarriedEgg2)
			if not getMyCarriedEgg() or getMyCarriedEgg() ~= myCarriedEgg2 then
				return false
			end
		end
		pcall(function()
			local character = getCharacter()
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid then
				humanoid:UnequipTools()
			end
		end)
		return moveTo(myCarriedEgg2)
	end

	SAE_ST._entryDone = false

	function SAE_ST.gameplayEntryMissing()
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		local fieldCached = Core.readFieldCached()
		local records = fieldCached and fieldCached.Records
		if not records then
			return false
		end
		local record
		for _, record2 in ipairs(records) do
			if record2.State == "Slot" and record2.BottomCFrame and not tostring(record2.Uid):find("FirstAreaEgg") and (record2.BottomCFrame.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude > 60 then
				record = record2
				break
			end
		end
		if not record then
			return false
		end
		local result, text = Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(record.Uid)
		return type(text) == "string" and text:lower():find("gameplay area") ~= nil
	end

	function SAE_ST._walkFlatTo(amount, z2, arg)
		local controls
		pcall(function()
			local PlayerModule = require(Core.LP.PlayerScripts.PlayerModule)
			controls = PlayerModule:GetControls()
			controls:Disable()
		end)
		local value = os.clock() + (arg or 30)
		local position
		local now = os.clock()
		while os.clock() < value do
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				break
			end
			local instance = hrp()
			local character = getCharacter()
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not (instance and humanoid) then
				break
			end
			if instance.Anchored then
				instance.Anchored = false
			end
			local value2 = amount - instance.Position.X
			local value3 = z2 - instance.Position.Z
			local vector2 = Vector3.new(value2, 0, value3)
			if vector2.Magnitude <= 5 then
				break
			end
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = vector2.Magnitude < 14 and 10 or 70
			humanoid:Move(vector2.Unit, false)
			if position and (instance.Position - position).Magnitude < 1 then
				if os.clock() - now > 1 then
					humanoid.Jump = true
					now = os.clock()
				end
				if os.clock() - now > 6 then
					break
				end
			else
				position, now = instance.Position, os.clock()
			end
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		pcall(function()
			local character = getCharacter()
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid then
				humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
				humanoid.WalkSpeed = SAE_ST.serverWalkSpeed and SAE_ST.serverWalkSpeed() or 16
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			if controls then
				controls:Enable()
			end
		end)
	end

	function SAE_ST.registerGameplayEntry()
		if SAE_ST._entryDone then
			return true
		end
		if SAE_ST._entryBusy then
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST._entryBusy = true
		local busy = SAE_ST.busy
		local repositioning = SAE_ST.repositioning
		SAE_ST.busy, SAE_ST.repositioning = true, true
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return SAE_ST._registerGameplayEntryInner()
		end)
		SAE_ST.busy, SAE_ST.repositioning = busy, repositioning
		SAE_ST._entryBusy = false
		return ok and result or false
	end

	function SAE_ST._registerGameplayEntryInner()
		if not SAE_ST.gameplayEntryMissing() then
			SAE_ST._entryDone = true
			return true
		end
		local x
		local z
		pcall(function()
			local areas = Core.World() and Core.World():FindFirstChild("Areas")
			local separationLine = areas and (areas:FindFirstChild("GameplayZ") or areas:FindFirstChild("SeparationLine"))
			if separationLine then
				x = separationLine.Position.X
				z = separationLine.Position.Z
			end
		end)
		if not x then
			if SAE_ST.notify then
				SAE_ST.notify("Gameplay gate not found - cannot register entry")
			end
			return false
		end
		local instance = hrp()
		local z2 = z or instance and instance.Position.Z or -400
		SAE_ST._walkFlatTo(instance and instance.Position.X or x, z2, 30)
		task.wait(0.3)
		SAE_ST._walkFlatTo(x - 40, z2, 40)
		task.wait(0.4)
		SAE_ST._walkFlatTo(x + 140, z2, 45)
		task.wait(1)
		SAE_ST._entryDone = not SAE_ST.gameplayEntryMissing()
		if SAE_ST.notify then
			SAE_ST.notify(SAE_ST._entryDone and "Entered gameplay area - stealing enabled" or "Still outside the gameplay area - walk out of spawn once")
		end
		return SAE_ST._entryDone
	end

	Core.LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		SAE_ST._entryDone = false
	end)

	local function ensureGameplayEntry()
		if not SAE_ST.autoSteal then
			return
		end
		pcall(SAE_ST.registerGameplayEntry)
		local value = os.clock() + 3
		while SAE_ST.busy and os.clock() < value do
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
		if getMyCarriedEgg() then
			return (pcall(unequipTools))
		end
		if isNearPlot() then
			return
		end
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return
		end
		local distance = (instance.Position - myPlotSafeCFrame.Position).Magnitude
		local huge = math.huge
		for _, info in ipairs(sort()) do
			local bottomCFrame = info.rec and info.rec.BottomCFrame
			if bottomCFrame then
				local distance2 = (instance.Position - bottomCFrame.Position).Magnitude
				if distance2 < huge then
					huge = distance2
				end
			end
		end
		if huge < distance then
			return
		end
		return (pcall(function()
			approachTarget(lookup.ReturnSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed)
		end))
	end

	local info

	local function enterArea(areaId)
		if not areaId or areaId == "" then
			return
		end
		pcall(function()
			if info then
				if type(info.EnterArea) == "function" then
					pcall(info.EnterArea, areaId)
				end
				if type(info.RequestEnterArea) == "function" then
					pcall(info.RequestEnterArea, areaId)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local wallSealedLatch = false
	local wallPhaseFlip = false
	local WALL_GRACE_SECONDS = 0.15
	local AreaEggResetWall
	pcall(function()
		AreaEggResetWall = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.AreaEggResetWall)
	end)

	local function isResetWallSealedByModule()
		if AreaEggResetWall and AreaEggResetWall.IsSealed then
			local ok, result = pcall(AreaEggResetWall.IsSealed)
			if ok and typeof(result) == "boolean" then
				return result
			end
		end
		local instance = Core.World()
		local areas = instance and instance:FindFirstChild("Areas")
		local wallStartCollision = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("WallStartCollision")
		return wallStartCollision and wallStartCollision:IsA("BasePart") and wallStartCollision.CanCollide == true or false
	end

	local function computeWallSealed()
		local flag = false
		if wallSealedLatch then
			flag = true
		end
		if not flag and isResetWallSealedByModule() then
			flag = true
		end
		if not flag and CycleCmds and CycleCmds.IsNight then
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return CycleCmds.IsNight(Core.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
			end)
			if ok and typeof(result) == "boolean" and result then
				flag = true
			end
		end
		if flag then
			SAE_ST.lastWallTime = os.clock()
			wallPhaseFlip = true
		elseif wallPhaseFlip then
			wallPhaseFlip = false
			lastSnapshotRequestAt = 0
			pcall(function()
				Core.EggCmds.RequestAreaEggSnapshot()
			end)
		end
		return flag
	end

	local function isWallSealed()
		return computeWallSealed()
	end

	local function isResetWallSealed()
		return isResetWallSealedByModule()
	end

	local function isWallActive()
		if wallSealedLatch or isResetWallSealed() then
			return true
		end
		return os.clock() - (SAE_ST.lastWallTime or 0) < WALL_GRACE_SECONDS
	end

	local function isPhaseEnding()
		if not CycleCmds or not CycleCmds.GetPhaseTimeLeft then
			return false
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return CycleCmds.GetPhaseTimeLeft(Core.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
		end)
		return ok and typeof(result) == "number" and result < 5
	end

	pcall(function()
		if Core.EggCmds.AreaEggResetStartCountdown then
			Core.EggCmds.AreaEggResetStartCountdown:Connect(function()
				wallSealedLatch = true
			end)
		end
		if Core.EggCmds.AreaEggRareSpawnsPresented then
			Core.EggCmds.AreaEggRareSpawnsPresented:Connect(function()
				wallSealedLatch = false
			end)
		end
		if Core.EggCmds.AreaEggSnapshotUpdated then
			Core.EggCmds.AreaEggSnapshotUpdated:Connect(function()
				wallSealedLatch = false
			end)
		end
	end)

	local function enablePrompt(carryAreaEgg)
		if not carryAreaEgg or not carryAreaEgg:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
			return
		end
		carryAreaEgg.Enabled = true
		carryAreaEgg.HoldDuration = 0
		carryAreaEgg.RequiresLineOfSight = false
		carryAreaEgg.MaxActivationDistance = 9999
		carryAreaEgg.ClickablePrompt = true
	end

	local function getNumber(info)
		if not info then
			return 0
		end
		if info.BoundsSize then
			return (info.BoundsSize.X or 1) * (info.BoundsSize.Y or 1) * (info.BoundsSize.Z or 1)
		end
		if info.Volume then
			return tonumber(info.Volume) or 0
		end
		return 0
	end

	local function estimateAssetWeight(info)
		local number = 0
		local category = info.AssetCategory or info.Category
		local scale = info.AssetScale or info.Scale
		if category and scale and Core.Assets and Core.Assets[category] then
			pcall(function()
				number = Core.AssetEarnings.CatalogRatePerSecond({Category = category, Scale = scale, Mutations = {}})
			end)
		end
		if (not number or number == 0) and info.GenerationRate then
			number = tonumber(info.GenerationRate) or 0
		end
		return number
	end

	local function findEggSlotModel(record)
		if not record or not record.Uid then
			return nil
		end
		local areaEggSlotsClient = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient")
		if areaEggSlotsClient and #areaEggSlotsClient:GetChildren() > 24 then
			areaEggSlotsClient:ClearAllChildren()
		end
		if not areaEggSlotsClient then
			areaEggSlotsClient = Instance.new("Folder")
			areaEggSlotsClient.Name = "AreaEggSlotsClient"
			areaEggSlotsClient.Parent = Core.Workspace
		end
		local child = areaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(tostring(record.Uid))
		if child then
			local carryAreaEgg = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt", true) or child:FindFirstChild("CarryAreaEgg", true)
			if not carryAreaEgg then
				carryAreaEgg = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
				carryAreaEgg.Name = "CarryAreaEgg"
				carryAreaEgg.ActionText = "Steal"
				carryAreaEgg.ObjectText = "Egg"
				carryAreaEgg.Parent = child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") or child
			end
			enablePrompt(carryAreaEgg)
			return child
		end
		local model
		if EggRenderer and EggRenderer.RenderVisual then
			pcall(function()
				model = EggRenderer.RenderVisual({
					OwnerUserId = 0,
					ScaleAlpha = 1,
					UID = record.Uid,
					ModelName = tostring(record.Uid),
					Record = {
						AssetCategory = record.AssetCategory or record.Category or "Bronto",
						AssetScale = record.AssetScale or 1,
						AssetEyeColor = record.AssetEyeColor or 0,
						AssetColorSeed = record.AssetColorSeed or 0,
						AssetColorIndex = record.AssetColorIndex or 0,
						Mutations = record.Mutations and table.clone(record.Mutations) or {},
						BaseMutation = record.BaseMutation,
					},
				}, areaEggSlotsClient, false).Model
				if model and record.BottomCFrame then
					model:PivotTo(record.BottomCFrame)
				end
			end)
		end
		if model then
			local carryAreaEgg = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt", true) or model:FindFirstChild("CarryAreaEgg", true)
			if not carryAreaEgg then
				carryAreaEgg = Instance.new("ProximityPrompt")
				carryAreaEgg.Name = "CarryAreaEgg"
				carryAreaEgg.ActionText = "Steal"
				carryAreaEgg.ObjectText = "Egg"
				carryAreaEgg.Parent = model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") or model
			end
			enablePrompt(carryAreaEgg)
		end
		return model
	end

	do
		local lastSlotScanAt = 0

		local function refreshEggSlots(arg)
			local now = os.clock()
			if not arg and now - lastSlotScanAt < 2.5 then
				return
			end
			lastSlotScanAt = now
			local areaEggSnapshot
			pcall(function()
				areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
			end)
			if not areaEggSnapshot or not areaEggSnapshot.Records then
				return
			end
			for _, info in ipairs(areaEggSnapshot.Records) do
				if info.State == "Slot" and info.BottomCFrame then
					pcall(function()
						findEggSlotModel(info)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	local swingBatAtCarrier
	SAE_ST.bait = {
		needs = {},
		misses = {},
		use = true,
		told = {},
		maxWait = 45,
		stats = {tries = 0, noDecoy = 0, flyFail = 0, decoyFail = 0, noGuard = 0, targetFail = 0, ok = 0},
		dragged = 0,
		warnedDrag = false,
	}

	function SAE_ST.bait.guardState(arg)
		local guard
		pcall(function()
			guard = Core.World().Areas.GuardAreas[arg].Guard
		end)
		return guard and tostring(guard:GetAttribute("GuardState")) or nil
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.goTo(myPlotSafeCFrame, clamped, arg)
		local part = hrp()
		if not part then
			return false
		end
		if part.Anchored then
			part.Anchored = false
		end
		local position = part.Position
		local savedMyPlotSafeCFrame = typeof(myPlotSafeCFrame) == "CFrame" and myPlotSafeCFrame.Position or myPlotSafeCFrame
		local distance = (savedMyPlotSafeCFrame - position).Magnitude
		if distance < 4 then
			local character = getCharacter()
			if character and typeof(myPlotSafeCFrame) == "CFrame" then
				character:PivotTo(myPlotSafeCFrame)
			end
			return true
		end
		local clamped2 = math.clamp(tonumber(clamped) or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed or 300, 60, 5000)
		local minValue = 0
		local now = 0
		local now2
		while minValue < distance do
			if SAE_ST.stale() or not isAlive() then
				return false
			end
			if SAE_ST.carryUid and os.clock() - now >= 0.25 then
				now = os.clock()
				if Core.carriedUid == nil then
					now2 = now2 or os.clock()
					if os.clock() - now2 > 1.5 then
						SAE_ST.carryLost = true
						return false
					end
				elseif tostring(Core.carriedUid) ~= tostring(SAE_ST.carryUid) then
					SAE_ST.carryLost = true
					return false
				else
					now2 = nil
				end
			end
			local result = Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			part = hrp()
			if not part then
				return false
			end
			minValue = math.min(distance, minValue + clamped2 * result)
			part.CFrame = CFrame.new(position:Lerp(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, minValue / distance))
			part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		end
		local character = getCharacter()
		if character and typeof(myPlotSafeCFrame) == "CFrame" then
			character:PivotTo(myPlotSafeCFrame)
		end
		task.wait(0.2)
		part = hrp()
		if part and (part.Position - savedMyPlotSafeCFrame).Magnitude > 15 then
			if not arg then
				task.wait(2)
				if SAE_ST.bait.goTo(myPlotSafeCFrame, 150, true) then
					return true
				end
			end
			local bait = SAE_ST.bait
			bait.dragged = bait.dragged + 1
			if SAE_ST.bait.dragged >= 3 and not SAE_ST.bait.warnedDrag then
				SAE_ST.bait.warnedDrag = true
				if SAE_ST.notify then
					SAE_ST.notify("Server is pulling the character back - hop server or rejoin to keep stealing")
				end
			end
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST.bait.dragged = 0
		return true
	end

	SAE_ST.bait.voidAt, SAE_ST.bait.voidArea = 0, nil
	pcall(function()
		local Remotes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
		Remotes.Alerts.Raise.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
			local text = ""
			for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
				local selected = select(i, ...)
				if type(selected) == "table" then
					local text2 = selected.Text or selected.Message or selected.message or selected.text
					if type(text2) == "string" and text2 ~= "" then
						text = text2
						break
					end
				elseif type(selected) == "string" and selected:find(" ", 1, true) then
					text = selected
				end
			end
			if text:find("returned to its nest", 1, true) or text:find("Delivery failed", 1, true) then
				SAE_ST.bait.voidAt = os.clock()
				pcall(function()
					SAE_ST.noteAreaResult(SAE_ST.bait.lastArea, false)
				end)
				local lastArea = SAE_ST.bait.lastArea
				if lastArea then
					SAE_ST.bait.misses[lastArea] = 2
					if not SAE_ST.bait.needs[lastArea] then
						SAE_ST.bait.needs[lastArea] = os.clock()
						if not SAE_ST.bait.told[lastArea] then
							SAE_ST.bait.told[lastArea] = true
							if SAE_ST.notify then
								if SAE_ST.bait.use then
									SAE_ST.notify(lastArea .. ": guard voids deliveries here - using decoy runs")
								else
									SAE_ST.notify(lastArea .. ": guard voids deliveries here - skipping it (turn on Guard Decoy Runs to farm it anyway)")
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end)

	function SAE_ST.bait.invCount()
		local value = 0
		pcall(function()
			for _ in pairs(Core.Save.Get().EggInventory or {}) do
				value = value + 1
			end
		end)
		return value
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.pick(arg, arg2)
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return nil
		end
		local areaEggSnapshot
		pcall(function()
			areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
		end)
		local value
		local value2
		for _, record in ipairs(areaEggSnapshot and areaEggSnapshot.Records or {}) do
			if record.AreaId == arg and record.Uid ~= arg2 and record.BottomCFrame and record.State ~= "Claimed" and record.State ~= "Carried" and not record.CarrierUserId and not tostring(record.Uid):find("FirstAreaEgg") then
				local distance = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude
				if not value2 or distance < value2 then
					value, value2 = record, distance
				end
			end
		end
		return value
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.take(decoy, arg)
		local value = decoy.BottomCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
		local value2 = os.clock() + (arg or 5)
		local now = 0
		while os.clock() < value2 do
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				return false
			end
			local character = getCharacter()
			if not character then
				return false
			end
			if os.clock() - now >= 0.05 then
				character:PivotTo(value)
				local result = hrp()
				if result then
					result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
			end
			if os.clock() - now >= 0.1 then
				now = os.clock()
				pcall(function()
					Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(decoy.Uid)
				end)
				if getMyCarriedEgg() == decoy.Uid then
					return true
				end
			end
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		return false
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.strike(eggUid, areaId, arg)
		local text
		local humanoidRootPart
		pcall(function()
			local guard = Core.World().Areas.GuardAreas[areaId].Guard
			humanoidRootPart = guard.PrimaryPart or guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		end)
		local now = os.clock()
		while os.clock() - now < (arg or 4) do
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				return false, 0
			end
			pcall(function()
				local Remotes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
				Remotes.GuardPatrol.ForestStrike:FireServer({
					EggUid = eggUid,
					GuardCFrame = humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.CFrame or hrp() and hrp().CFrame or CFrame.new(),
				})
			end)
			task.wait(0.08)
			if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= eggUid then
				local value = os.clock() - now
				text = nil
				pcall(function()
					text = tostring(Core.World().Areas.GuardAreas[areaId].Guard:GetAttribute("GuardState"))
				end)
				local flag = text == "RetrievingEgg" or text == "Chasing" or text == "ReturningHome"
				return flag, value
			end
		end
		return false, os.clock() - now
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.pickDecoy(record)
		if not (record and record.AreaId and record.BottomCFrame) then
			return nil
		end
		local fieldCached = Core.readFieldCached()
		local value
		local value2
		for _, record2 in ipairs(fieldCached and fieldCached.Records or {}) do
			if record2.State == "Slot" and not record2.CarrierUserId and tostring(record2.AreaId) == tostring(record.AreaId) and tostring(record2.Uid) ~= tostring(record.Uid) and record2.BottomCFrame and not tostring(record2.Uid):find("FirstAreaEgg") then
				local distance = (record2.BottomCFrame.Position - record.BottomCFrame.Position).Magnitude
				if not value2 or distance < value2 then
					value, value2 = record2, distance
				end
			end
		end
		return value, value2
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.primeWithDecoy(arg)
		local decoy = SAE_ST.bait.pickDecoy(arg)
		if not decoy then
			return false, "tidak ada telur lain di area ini"
		end
		local stats = SAE_ST.bait.stats
		if SAE_ST.immunityWanted == nil then
			SAE_ST.immunityWanted = SAE_ST.immunity and true or false
		end
		SAE_ST.immunity = false

		local function fn()
			SAE_ST.immunity = SAE_ST.immunityWanted and true or false
		end

		if not SAE_ST.bait.take(decoy, 5) then
			local savedStats = stats
			savedStats.decoyFail = savedStats.decoyFail + 1
			fn()
			return false, "umpan gagal diambil"
		end
		task.wait(0.4)
		local result = SAE_ST.bait.strike(decoy.Uid, decoy.AreaId, 4)
		if not result then
			local savedStats = stats
			savedStats.noGuard = savedStats.noGuard + 1
			fn()
			return false, "guard tidak merebut umpan"
		end
		local now = os.clock()
		while os.clock() - now < 6 do
			pcall(function()
				SAE_ST.ReGrab.recoverPlayerState()
			end)
			if SAE_ST.bait.take(arg, 2.5) then
				local savedStats = stats
				savedStats.ok = savedStats.ok + 1
				fn()
				return true
			end
			task.wait(0.3)
		end
		local savedStats = stats
		savedStats.targetFail = savedStats.targetFail + 1
		fn()
		return false, "target gagal diambil sesudah umpan"
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.prime(record)
		if not record or not record.AreaId or not record.BottomCFrame then
			return false
		end
		local stats = SAE_ST.bait.stats
		local savedStats = stats
		savedStats.tries = savedStats.tries + 1
		local result = SAE_ST.bait.primeWithDecoy(record)
		if result then
			return true
		end
		if SAE_ST.immunityWanted == nil then
			SAE_ST.immunityWanted = SAE_ST.immunity and true or false
		end
		SAE_ST.immunity = false

		local function fn()
			SAE_ST.immunity = SAE_ST.immunityWanted and true or false
		end

		for i = 1, 3 do
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				fn()
				return false
			end
			if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= record.Uid then
				task.wait(0.6)
				if not SAE_ST.bait.take(record, 5) then
					local savedStats2 = stats
					savedStats2.decoyFail = savedStats2.decoyFail + 1
					fn()
					return false
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
			local result2 = SAE_ST.bait.strike(record.Uid, record.AreaId, 4)
			if result2 then
				if SAE_ST.bait.take(record, 4) then
					local savedStats2 = stats
					savedStats2.ok = savedStats2.ok + 1
					fn()
					return true
				end
				local savedStats2 = stats
				savedStats2.targetFail = savedStats2.targetFail + 1
				fn()
				return false
			end
		end
		local savedStats2 = stats
		savedStats2.noGuard = savedStats2.noGuard + 1
		fn()
		return false
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.deliver(carryUid, clamped)
		if not carryUid then
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST.carryUid, SAE_ST.carryLost = carryUid, false
		local result = SAE_ST.bait.goTo(myPlotSafeCFrame, math.max(tonumber(clamped) or 0, tonumber(SAE_ST.tweenSpeed) or 0, 500))
		SAE_ST.carryUid = nil
		if not result and SAE_ST.carryLost then
			return false
		end
		local value = os.clock() + 6
		local now = 0
		while os.clock() < value do
			if os.clock() - now >= 0.2 then
				now = os.clock()
				if not getMyCarriedEgg() then
					break
				end
			end
			local instance = hrp()
			if instance and (instance.Position - myPlotSafeCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 8 then
				instance.CFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
				instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		if getMyCarriedEgg() then
			pcall(function()
				moveTo(carryUid)
			end)
		end
		return getMyCarriedEgg() == nil
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.run(record)
		if not record or not record.BottomCFrame or not record.AreaId then
			return false
		end
		if SAE_ST.stale() then
			return false
		end
		local clamped = math.clamp(math.floor(math.max(lookup.GlideSpeed or 0, SAE_ST.tweenSpeed or 0, 450)), 120, 500)
		local value = record.BottomCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
		if getMyCarriedEgg() then
			return unequipTools()
		end
		if not SAE_ST.bait.goTo(value, clamped) then
			return false
		end
		task.wait(0.5)
		if not SAE_ST.bait.take(record, 5) then
			SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
			return false
		end
		local result = SAE_ST.bait.prime(record)
		if not result or getMyCarriedEgg() ~= record.Uid then
			SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST.bait.lastArea = record.AreaId
		local result2 = SAE_ST.bait.invCount()
		if not SAE_ST.bait.deliver(record.Uid, clamped) then
			pcall(function()
				unequipTools()
			end)
		end
		return SAE_ST.bait.judge(record.AreaId, result2)
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.judge(areaId, amount)
		if not areaId or not amount then
			return false
		end
		local value = os.clock() + 2
		repeat
			if SAE_ST.bait.invCount() > amount then
				SAE_ST.bait.misses[areaId] = nil
				SAE_ST.bait.needs[areaId] = nil
				return true
			end
			task.wait(0.15)
		until os.clock() >= value
		local value2 = (SAE_ST.bait.misses[areaId] or 0) + 1
		SAE_ST.bait.misses[areaId] = value2
		if value2 >= 2 and not SAE_ST.bait.needs[areaId] then
			SAE_ST.bait.needs[areaId] = os.clock()
			if not SAE_ST.bait.told[areaId] then
				SAE_ST.bait.told[areaId] = true
				if SAE_ST.notify then
					if SAE_ST.bait.use then
						SAE_ST.notify(areaId .. ": guarded area - handing the egg to the guard first so the delivery lands")
					else
						SAE_ST.notify(areaId .. ": guarded area - skipping it (turn on Guard Handoff to farm it)")
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.skip(areaId)
		if SAE_ST.bait.use then
			return false
		end
		local value = SAE_ST.bait.needs[areaId]
		if not value then
			return false
		end
		if os.clock() - value > 600 then
			SAE_ST.bait.needs[areaId] = nil
			SAE_ST.bait.misses[areaId] = nil
			return false
		end
		return true
	end

	function SAE_ST.bait.wanted(arg)
		if not SAE_ST.bait.use then
			return false
		end
		local value = SAE_ST.bait.needs[arg]
		if not value then
			return false
		end
		if os.clock() - value > 600 then
			SAE_ST.bait.needs[arg] = nil
			SAE_ST.bait.misses[arg] = nil
			return false
		end
		return true
	end

	function SAE_ST.removeTeleportClone()
		pcall(function()
			for _, child in ipairs(Core.Workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if child.Name:find("TeleportDecoy") or child.Name == "SZ_VisualGhost" then
					pcall(function()
						child:Destroy()
					end)
				end
			end
		end)
		local character = getCharacter()
		if character then
			for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
				end
				if descendant:IsA("Trail") then
					descendant.Enabled = true
				end
			end
		end
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid then
			humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			humanoid.Sit = false
			pcall(function()
				humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end)
		end
		local currentCamera = Core.Workspace.CurrentCamera
		if currentCamera and humanoid then
			currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			pcall(function()
				currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
			end)
		end
	end

	if getgenv then
		getgenv().ZK_TeleportCleanup = SAE_ST.removeTeleportClone
	end
	do
		local function setStateEnabled()
			local character = getCharacter()
			if not character then
				return
			end
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
					if not descendant.Enabled then
						descendant.Enabled = true
					end
				elseif descendant:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or descendant:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
					if descendant.Name:find("Ragdoll") or descendant:GetAttribute("RagdollConstraint") then
						pcall(function()
							descendant:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				elseif descendant:IsA("Attachment") and (descendant.Name:find("Ragdoll") or descendant:GetAttribute("RagdollAttachment")) then
					pcall(function()
						descendant:Destroy()
					end)
				end
			end
			if humanoid then
				humanoid.PlatformStand = false
				humanoid.Sit = false
				pcall(function()
					humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
					humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
					humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
					humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding, false)
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
					task.wait(0.02)
					humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end)
			end
			if humanoidRootPart then
				humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
		end

		local blacklistedUids = {}
		pcall(function()
			local EggState = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)
			if EggState and EggState.CarryChanged then
				EggState.CarryChanged:Connect(function(record)
					if record and record.CarrierUserId == Core.LP.UserId and record.Uid then
						blacklistedUids[tostring(record.Uid)] = true
					elseif record and record.CarrierUserId ~= Core.LP.UserId and record.Uid then
						blacklistedUids[tostring(record.Uid)] = nil
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		local function isUidBlacklisted(uid)
			return blacklistedUids[tostring(uid)] == true
		end

		local function getGuardPosition()
			local forest = Core.World() and Core.World():FindFirstChild("Areas") and Core.World().Areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas") and Core.World().Areas.GuardAreas:FindFirstChild("Forest")
			if forest then
				local guard = forest:FindFirstChild("Guard")
				local primaryPart = guard and (guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or guard:FindFirstChild("Collider") or guard:IsA("Model") and guard.PrimaryPart)
				if guard and primaryPart then
					return guard, primaryPart
				end
				local forestGuardAuthored = forest:FindFirstChild("ForestGuardAuthored")
				local primaryPart2 = forestGuardAuthored and (forestGuardAuthored:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or forestGuardAuthored:IsA("Model") and forestGuardAuthored.PrimaryPart)
				if forestGuardAuthored and primaryPart2 then
					return forestGuardAuthored, primaryPart2
				end
			end
			local areaGuards = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("AreaGuards")
			if areaGuards then
				for _, child in ipairs(areaGuards:GetChildren()) do
					local humanoidRootPart = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Position.X < 850 then
						return child, humanoidRootPart
					end
				end
			end
			return nil, nil
		end

		local function findSnapshotEgg(uid)
			local areaEggSnapshot
			pcall(function()
				areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
			end)
			if not areaEggSnapshot or not areaEggSnapshot.Records then
				pcall(function()
					local EggState = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)
					areaEggSnapshot = EggState.ReadFieldEggs()
				end)
			end
			local records = areaEggSnapshot and areaEggSnapshot.Records or {}
			local value
			for _, record in ipairs(records) do
				if (record.State == "Slot" or record.State == "Dropped") and record.Uid ~= uid and (not record.CarrierUserId or record.CarrierUserId == Core.LP.UserId) then
					local position = record.BottomCFrame and record.BottomCFrame.Position or record.BoundsCFrame and record.BoundsCFrame.Position
					local savedPosition = record.AreaId == "Forest" or tostring(record.Uid):find("Forest", 1, true) or tostring(record.Uid):find("^FirstArea") or position and position.X < 680
					if savedPosition then
						local baitDrops = SAE_ST._baitDrops and SAE_ST._baitDrops[tostring(record.Uid)]
						if not baitDrops or os.clock() > baitDrops then
							if record.State == "Slot" then
								value = record
								break
							elseif not value then
								value = record
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			if not value then
				for _, record in ipairs(records) do
					if (record.State == "Slot" or record.State == "Dropped") and record.Uid ~= uid and (not record.CarrierUserId or record.CarrierUserId == Core.LP.UserId) then
						local position = record.BottomCFrame and record.BottomCFrame.Position or record.BoundsCFrame and record.BoundsCFrame.Position
						local savedPosition = record.AreaId == "Forest" or tostring(record.Uid):find("Forest", 1, true) or tostring(record.Uid):find("^FirstArea") or position and position.X < 680
						if savedPosition then
							if record.State == "Slot" then
								value = record
								break
							elseif not value then
								value = record
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			return value
		end

		function SAE_ST.runTeleportSteal(record)
			if not record or not record.Uid then
				return false
			end
			if isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive() then
				return false
			end
			while not isAlive() do
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
			local character = getCharacter()
			local instance = hrp()
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not character or not instance or not humanoid then
				return false
			end
			SAE_ST._isStealing = true
			local myPlotSafeCFrame = getMyPlotSafeCFrame()
			local position = record.BottomCFrame and record.BottomCFrame.Position or record.BoundsCFrame and record.BoundsCFrame.Position
			if not position then
				SAE_ST._isStealing = false
				return false
			end
			local vector2 = Vector3.new(position.X, position.Y + 2.5, position.Z)
			enterArea(record.AreaId)
			local eggSlotModel = findEggSlotModel(record)
			local areaSlotKey
			pcall(function()
				local AreaEggSlotIdentity = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AreaEggSlotIdentity)
				if AreaEggSlotIdentity and record.AreaId and record.NestId then
					areaSlotKey = AreaEggSlotIdentity.SlotKey(record.AreaId, record.NestId)
				end
			end)
			if not areaSlotKey and record.AreaId and record.NestId then
				areaSlotKey = tostring(record.AreaId) .. ":" .. tostring(record.NestId)
			end
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(requestStreamAround) == "function" then
					requestStreamAround(position, 2)
				elseif Core.LP and typeof(Core.LP.RequestStreamAroundAsync) == "function" then
					Core.LP:RequestStreamAroundAsync(position, 2)
				end
			end)
			if getMyCarriedEgg() then
				moveTo(getMyCarriedEgg())
				task.wait(0.08)
			end
			local currentCamera = Core.Workspace.CurrentCamera
			local cameraType = currentCamera and currentCamera.CameraType
			local CFrame2 = currentCamera and currentCamera.CFrame
			local CFrame3 = instance.CFrame
			local clone

			local function fn()
				pcall(function()
					if clone then
						clone:Destroy()
						clone = nil
					end
					local character = getCharacter()
					if character then
						for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
								descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
							end
							if descendant:IsA("Trail") then
								descendant.Enabled = true
							end
						end
					end
					if currentCamera then
						currentCamera.CameraType = cameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable and cameraType or Enum.CameraType.Custom
						local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if humanoid then
							currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
						end
						if CFrame2 then
							currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame2
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			pcall(function()
				if character and CFrame3 then
					character.Archivable = true
					clone = character:Clone()
					clone.Name = "SZ_VisualGhost"
					for _, descendant in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") or descendant:IsA("Sound") then

					clone.Parent = Core.Workspace

							descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1

							descendant.Enabled = false
							pcall(function()
								descendant:Clear()
							end)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			if currentCamera and CFrame2 then
				currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
				currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame2
			end

				SAE_ST._isStealing = false

			for _, connection in ipairs(items) do

			SAE_ST._waitingGuardHit = false
			if flag2 then

				SAE_ST._baitDrops[tostring(savedSnapshotEgg.Uid)] = os.clock() + 0.4

			else
				setStateEnabled()
				instance = hrp() or instance
				if instance and instance.Parent then
					instance.CFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
					instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				fn()
				SAE_ST._isStealing = false
				return false

			task.wait(0.04)
			enterArea(record.AreaId)
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(requestStreamAround) == "function" then
					requestStreamAround(vector2, 2)
				elseif Core.LP and typeof(Core.LP.RequestStreamAroundAsync) == "function" then
					Core.LP:RequestStreamAroundAsync(vector2, 2)
				end
			end)
			eggSlotModel = eggSlotModel or findEggSlotModel(record)
			instance = hrp() or instance
			if instance and instance.Parent then
				instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(vector2)
				instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			local number = tonumber(SAE_ST.semiTeleportHoldSettle) or 2.5
			local now2 = os.clock()
			while os.clock() - now2 < number do
				instance = hrp() or instance
				if instance and instance.Parent then
					instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(vector2)
					instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
			local uid = record.Uid
			local flag3 = false
			blacklistedUids[tostring(uid)] = nil
			local now3 = os.clock()
			while not flag3 and os.clock() - now3 < 2.5 do
				instance = hrp() or instance
				if instance and instance.Parent then
					if instance.Position.Y < 65 then
						instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(vector2)
					end
					instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				pcall(function()
					local EggState = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.EggState)
					if EggState and EggState.CarryFieldEgg then
						if areaSlotKey then
							EggState.CarryFieldEgg(uid, areaSlotKey)
						else
							EggState.CarryFieldEgg(uid)
						end
					end
				end)
				task.spawn(function()
					pcall(function()
						local Remotes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
						if Remotes and Remotes.EggWorld and Remotes.EggWorld.AskFieldEggCarry then
							Remotes.EggWorld.AskFieldEggCarry:InvokeServer({Uid = uid, AreaSlotKey = areaSlotKey, FirstAreaSlotKey = areaSlotKey})
						end
					end)
				end)
				pcall(function()
					Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(uid)
				end)
				local carryAreaEgg = eggSlotModel and (eggSlotModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt", true) or eggSlotModel:FindFirstChild("CarryAreaEgg", true))
				if not carryAreaEgg then
					local areaEggSlotsClient = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("AreaEggSlotsClient")
					local child = areaEggSlotsClient and (areaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(tostring(uid)) or areaSlotKey and areaEggSlotsClient:FindFirstChild(areaSlotKey))
					carryAreaEgg = child and (child:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt", true) or child:FindFirstChild("CarryAreaEgg", true))
				end
				if carryAreaEgg and fireproximityprompt and carryAreaEgg.Enabled then
					carryAreaEgg.HoldDuration = 0
					pcall(function()
						fireproximityprompt(carryAreaEgg, 0)
					end)
				end
				if isUidBlacklisted(uid) or getMyCarriedEgg() == uid then
					flag3 = true
					break
				end
				task.wait(0.04)
			end
			if not flag3 and not (isUidBlacklisted(uid) or getMyCarriedEgg() == uid) then
				if SAE_ST.ReGrab.execute(uid, 2.5) then
					flag3 = true
				else
					setStateEnabled()
					instance = hrp() or instance
					if instance and instance.Parent then
						instance.CFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
						instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end
					fn()
					SAE_ST._isStealing = false
					return false
				end
			end
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(requestStreamAround) == "function" then
					requestStreamAround(myPlotSafeCFrame.Position, 2)
				elseif Core.LP and typeof(Core.LP.RequestStreamAroundAsync) == "function" then
					Core.LP:RequestStreamAroundAsync(myPlotSafeCFrame.Position, 2)
				end
			end)
			local connection
			pcall(function()
				connection = Core.RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
					local character = getCharacter()
					if character then
						for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
								descendant.CanCollide = false
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end)
			local instance2 = hrp()
			local savedVector2 = instance2 and instance2.Position or vector2
			local value2 = math.max(savedVector2.Y, myPlotSafeCFrame.Position.Y) + 65
			local cframe = CFrame.new(savedVector2.X, value2, savedVector2.Z)
			local cframe2 = CFrame.new(myPlotSafeCFrame.Position.X, value2, myPlotSafeCFrame.Position.Z)
			local humanoid2 = getCharacter() and getCharacter():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			local function getFloored()
				local averageFps = type(getAverageFps) == "function" and getAverageFps() or 60
				local value = 500
				local number = tonumber(SAE_ST.semiTeleportSpeed) or 800
				local maxValue = math.max(value, number)
				local clamped = math.clamp((averageFps - 25) / 30, 0, 1)
				return math.floor(value + (maxValue - value) * clamped + 0.5)
			end

			local carryWatcher = SAE_ST.ReGrab.createCarryWatcher(uid, function()
				return true
			end)

			local function fn2()
				if not isAlive() then
					return true
				end
				if carryWatcher and carryWatcher() then
					return true
				end
				return false
			end

			local floored = getFloored()
			if instance2 and humanoid2 then
				down(instance2, humanoid2, cframe, fn2, floored, true, false)
				if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= uid then
					SAE_ST.ReGrab.execute(uid, 3.5)
					instance2 = hrp()
					if instance2 then
						local position2 = instance2.Position
						cframe = CFrame.new(position2.X, math.max(position2.Y, myPlotSafeCFrame.Position.Y) + 65, position2.Z)
					end
				end
				floored = getFloored()
				down(instance2, humanoid2, cframe2, fn2, floored, false, false)
				if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= uid then
					SAE_ST.ReGrab.execute(uid, 3.5)
					instance2 = hrp()
				end
				floored = getFloored()
				down(instance2, humanoid2, myPlotSafeCFrame, fn2, floored, false, true)
			else
				tweenToCFrame(myPlotSafeCFrame, floored, true)
			end
			if connection then
				pcall(function()
					connection:Disconnect()
				end)
			end
			instance = hrp() or instance

	local function runSteal(record, arg)
		local areaEggRecord, flag, flag2, areaEggRecord2
		if SAE_ST.movementMode == "Teleport" or SAE_ST.movementMode == "Semi Teleport" then
			return SAE_ST.runTeleportSteal(record)
		end
		if SAE_ST.stale() then
			return false
		end
		if isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive() then
			return false
		end
		while not isAlive() do
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
		if getMyCarriedEgg() then
			local result = unequipTools()
			applyProfile("Default")
			return result
		end
		local character = getCharacter()
		if not character then
			return false
		end
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local result = SAE_ST.prepareTreadmillBoost(instance, humanoid)
		if not result then
			task.wait(0.1)
			result = SAE_ST.prepareTreadmillBoost(instance, humanoid)
		end
		if not result then
			applyProfile("Default")
			return false
		end
		if isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive() then
			applyProfile("Default")
			return false
		end
		instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		local instance2 = record.BottomCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
		local instance3 = record.BottomCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
		requestStreamAround(instance2.Position, 2)
		local eggSlotModel = findEggSlotModel(record)
		local carryAreaEgg
		pcall(function()
			carryAreaEgg = eggSlotModel and (eggSlotModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt", true) or eggSlotModel:FindFirstChild("CarryAreaEgg", true))
			if carryAreaEgg then
				carryAreaEgg.Enabled = true
				carryAreaEgg.HoldDuration = 0
				carryAreaEgg.RequiresLineOfSight = false
				carryAreaEgg.MaxActivationDistance = 9999
				carryAreaEgg.ClickablePrompt = true
			end
		end)
		if not tweenToCFrame(instance2, arg or lookup.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed, false) then
			SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
			applyProfile("Default")
			return false
		end
		enterArea(record.AreaId)
		pcall(function()
			Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(record.Uid)
		end)
		if carryAreaEgg then
			pcall(fireproximityprompt, carryAreaEgg)
		end
		instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if SAE_ST.movementMode ~= "Real WalkSpeed" then
			character:PivotTo(instance3)
		end
		local flag3 = false
		local now = 0
		local now2 = 0
		local now3 = 0
		local value = 0.03333333333333333
		instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		character:PivotTo(instance3)
		local savedAreaEggRecord2 = false
		local value2 = os.clock() + (SAE_ST.grabHold or 0.5)
		repeat
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				return false
			end
			if getCharacter() ~= character or not isAlive() then
				return false
			end
			if isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive() then
				return false
			end
			if not (SAE_ST.autoSteal or SAE_ST.mpeSteal) then
				return false
			end
			if os.clock() - now2 >= 0.5 then
				areaEggRecord = nil
				pcall(function()
					areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(record.Uid)
				end)
				if not areaEggRecord or areaEggRecord.State == "Claimed" then
					SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
					return false
				end
				if areaEggRecord.State == "Carried" and areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId ~= Core.LP.UserId then
					if SAE_ST.autoSlap and os.clock() - (SAE_ST.lastSlapAt or 0) >= 6 then
						SAE_ST.lastSlapAt = os.clock()
						flag = false
						pcall(function()
							flag = swingBatAtCarrier(record.Uid, areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId)
						end)
						if flag and getMyCarriedEgg() == record.Uid then
							flag2 = false
							pcall(function()
								flag2 = unequipTools()
							end)
							if flag2 then
								return true
							end
						end
					end
					SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
					return false
				end
				if areaEggRecord and areaEggRecord.State == "Carried" and areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId == Core.LP.UserId then
					savedAreaEggRecord2 = true
					break
				end
				now2 = os.clock()
			end
			if os.clock() - now3 >= value then
				now3 = os.clock()
				pcall(function()
					Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(record.Uid)
				end)
			end
			if os.clock() - now >= 0.3 then
				requestStreamAround(instance.Position, 1)
				requestStreamAround(instance3.Position, 1)
				now = os.clock()
			end
			if (instance.Position - instance3.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
				instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				character:PivotTo(instance3)
			end
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			areaEggRecord2 = nil
			pcall(function()
				areaEggRecord2 = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(record.Uid)
			end)
			savedAreaEggRecord2 = areaEggRecord2 and areaEggRecord2.State == "Carried" or false
		until savedAreaEggRecord2 or os.clock() >= value2
		if not savedAreaEggRecord2 then
			if SAE_ST.ReGrab.execute(record.Uid, 2.5) then
				savedAreaEggRecord2 = true
			else
				if askFieldEggCarry then
					pcall(function()
						local result, value = askFieldEggCarry:InvokeServer({Uid = record.Uid})
						if result and value == true then
							local value2 = os.clock() + 0.5
							repeat
								Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
								savedAreaEggRecord2 = getMyCarriedEgg() == record.Uid
							until savedAreaEggRecord2 or os.clock() >= value2
						end
					end)
				end
				if not savedAreaEggRecord2 then
					if getMyCarriedEgg() then
						savedAreaEggRecord2 = true
					else
						SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
						return false
					end
				end
			end
		end
		flag3 = true
		if flag3 then
			local savedMyPlotSafeCFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
			if lookup.ReturnToBase and savedMyPlotSafeCFrame then
				returnHome(lookup.ReturnSpeed or 300, record.Uid)
			end
			if not getMyCarriedEgg() or getMyCarriedEgg() ~= record.Uid then
				if SAE_ST.ReGrab.execute(record.Uid, 3.5) then
					returnHome(lookup.ReturnSpeed or 300, record.Uid)
				end
				if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= record.Uid then
					flag3 = false
				end
			end
			pcall(function()
				local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if humanoid then
					humanoid:UnequipTools()
				end
			end)
			if flag3 then
				moveTo(record.Uid)
			end
		end
		applyProfile("Default")
		return flag3
	end

	SAE_ST.ITS = {
		primed = false,
		primedAt = 0,
		lastPrimeTry = 0,
		hopStep = 55,
		landTolerance = 8,
		windowSeconds = 4,
		takesPerWindow = 2,
		takes = 0,
		refusals = 0,
		lastPrimeFail = 0,
		coolUntil = 0,
		coolSeconds = 25,
	}
	SAE_ST.ITS.decoy = {model = nil, conn = nil, idle = nil, run = nil, walkSpeed = 16}

	function SAE_ST.ITS.decoyOff()
		local decoy = SAE_ST.ITS.decoy
		if decoy.conn then
			pcall(function()
				decoy.conn:Disconnect()
			end)
			decoy.conn = nil
		end
		if decoy.idle then
			pcall(function()
				decoy.idle:Stop(0.1)
			end)
			decoy.idle = nil
		end
		if decoy.run then
			pcall(function()
				decoy.run:Stop(0.1)
			end)
			decoy.run = nil
		end
		local character = getCharacter()
		if character then
			local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed == 0 then
				humanoid.WalkSpeed = decoy.walkSpeed or 16
			end
			for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
				elseif descendant:IsA("Decal") then
					descendant.Transparency = 0
				elseif descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Beam") or descendant:IsA("Trail") then
					descendant.Enabled = true
				end
			end
		end
		if decoy.model then
			pcall(function()
				decoy.model:Destroy()
			end)
			decoy.model = nil
		end
		local currentCamera = Core.Workspace.CurrentCamera
		if currentCamera then
			currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if humanoid then
				pcall(function()
					currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.decoyOn(myPlotSafeCFrame2)
		if not SAE_ST.instantSteal then
			return
		end
		local decoy = SAE_ST.ITS.decoy
		local character = getCharacter()
		local result = hrp()
		if not character or not result then
			return
		end
		local savedMyPlotSafeCFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame2 or myPlotSafeCFrame
		if not decoy.model or not decoy.model.Parent then
			pcall(function()
				character.Archivable = true
				local clone = character:Clone()
				clone.Name = character.Name .. "_Standin"
				for _, descendant in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						descendant.CanCollide = false
						descendant.Anchored = false 
					elseif descendant:IsA("Script") or descendant:IsA("LocalScript") then
						descendant:Destroy()
					end
				end
				local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local humanoid2 = clone:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local primaryPart = clone:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or clone.PrimaryPart
				if humanoid2 then
					if humanoid then
						humanoid2.HipHeight = humanoid.HipHeight
					end
					humanoid2.WalkSpeed = 16
					humanoid2.AutoRotate = false
					local animator = humanoid2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", humanoid2)
					local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
					animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://507766388"
					decoy.idle = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
					decoy.idle.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
					decoy.idle.Looped = true
					decoy.idle:Play()
					local animation2 = Instance.new("Animation")
					animation2.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://913376220"
					decoy.run = animator:LoadAnimation(animation2)
					decoy.run.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement
					decoy.run.Looped = true
				end
				if primaryPart then
					local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
					rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
					rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {clone, character}
					local rayResult = Core.Workspace:Raycast(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0), Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rayParams)
					local y = rayResult and rayResult.Position.Y + (humanoid2 and humanoid2.HipHeight or 2) + primaryPart.Size.Y / 2 or savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Position.Y
					primaryPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Position.X, y, savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Position.Z) * savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Rotation
				end
				clone.Parent = Core.Workspace
				decoy.model = clone
			end)
		end
		if decoy.conn then
			return
		end
		decoy.conn = Core.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if SAE_ST.stale() then
				SAE_ST.ITS.decoyOff()
				return
			end
			if not SAE_ST.instantSteal then
				SAE_ST.ITS.decoyOff()
				return
			end
			local model = decoy.model
			if not model or not model.Parent then
				return
			end
			local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local primaryPart = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model.PrimaryPart
			local currentCamera = Core.Workspace.CurrentCamera
			if currentCamera and humanoid then
				if currentCamera.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then
					currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
				end
				if currentCamera.CameraSubject ~= humanoid then
					currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
				end
			end
			local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
			local value = ((UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Down)) and 1 or 0) - ((UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Up)) and 1 or 0)
			local value2 = ((UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Right)) and 1 or 0) - ((UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Left)) and 1 or 0)
			if humanoid and currentCamera and (value2 ~= 0 or value ~= 0) then
				local lookVector = currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector
				local rightVector = currentCamera.CFrame.RightVector
				local vector2 = Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)
				local vector3 = Vector3.new(rightVector.X, 0, rightVector.Z)
				if vector2.Magnitude > 0.01 and vector3.Magnitude > 0.01 then
					local unit = vector3.Unit * value2 + vector2.Unit * -value
					if unit.Magnitude > 0.01 then
						unit = unit.Unit
						if decoy.idle and decoy.idle.IsPlaying then
							decoy.idle:Stop(0.15)
						end
						if decoy.run and not decoy.run.IsPlaying then
							decoy.run:Play(0.15)
						end
						humanoid:Move(unit, false)
						if primaryPart then
							primaryPart.CFrame = primaryPart.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(primaryPart.Position, primaryPart.Position + unit), 0.3)
						end
					end
				end
			elseif humanoid then
				if decoy.run and decoy.run.IsPlaying then
					decoy.run:Stop(0.15)
				end
				if decoy.idle and not decoy.idle.IsPlaying then
					decoy.idle:Play(0.15)
				end
				humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
			end
			local character = getCharacter()
			if character and character.Parent then
				local humanoid2 = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if humanoid2 and humanoid2.WalkSpeed > 0 and not SAE_ST.ITS.walking then
					decoy.walkSpeed = humanoid2.WalkSpeed
					humanoid2.WalkSpeed = 0
				elseif humanoid2 and SAE_ST.ITS.walking and humanoid2.WalkSpeed == 0 then
					humanoid2.WalkSpeed = decoy.walkSpeed or 16
				end
				for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
					elseif descendant:IsA("Decal") then
						descendant.Transparency = 1
					elseif descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Beam") or descendant:IsA("Trail") then
						descendant.Enabled = false
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.settle(CFrame2, arg)
		local character = getCharacter()
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		for i = 1, arg or 3 do
			local result = hrp()
			if not result then
				return
			end
			result.CFrame = CFrame2
			result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			result.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
				local state = humanoid:GetState()
				if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed then
					pcall(function()
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
					end)
				end
			end
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.place(myPlotSafeCFrame, arg)
		local cframe = typeof(myPlotSafeCFrame) == "CFrame" and myPlotSafeCFrame or CFrame.new(myPlotSafeCFrame)
		for i = 1, arg or 3 do
			local result = hrp()
			if not result then
				return false
			end
			result.CFrame = cframe
			result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			result.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			local instance = hrp()
			if instance and (instance.Position - cframe.Position).Magnitude <= SAE_ST.ITS.landTolerance then
				SAE_ST.ITS.settle(cframe, 2)
				return true
			end
			if i < (arg or 3) then
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.step(amount, arg)
		local value = os.clock() + (arg or 8)
		while os.clock() < value do
			local instance = hrp()
			if not instance then
				return false
			end
			local vector2 = amount - instance.Position
			if vector2.Magnitude < 6 then
				return true
			end
			instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(instance.Position + vector2.Unit * math.min(SAE_ST.ITS.hopStep, vector2.Magnitude))
			instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			for j = 1, 3 do
				Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
			task.wait(0.12)
		end
		return false
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.walk(position, arg)
		local character = getCharacter()
		local instance = hrp()
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not instance or not humanoid then
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST.ITS.walking = true
		if humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 0 then
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = SAE_ST.ITS.decoy.walkSpeed or 16
		end

		local function fn(arg)
			SAE_ST.ITS.walking = false
			return arg
		end

		local path = game:GetService("PathfindingService"):CreatePath({AgentRadius = 3, AgentHeight = 6, AgentCanJump = true})
		local ok = pcall(function()
			path:ComputeAsync(instance.Position, position)
		end)
		if not ok or path.Status ~= Enum.PathStatus.Success then
			return fn(false)
		end
		local waypoints = path:GetWaypoints()
		local value = os.clock() + (arg or 45)
		for i = 2, #waypoints do
			local instance2 = waypoints[i]
			humanoid = getCharacter() and getCharacter():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not humanoid then
				return fn(false)
			end
			if instance2.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump then
				humanoid.Jump = true
			end
			local flag = false
			while os.clock() < value do
				local instance3 = hrp()
				if not instance3 then
					return fn(false)
				end
				local vector2 = Vector3.new(instance3.Position.X - instance2.Position.X, 0, instance3.Position.Z - instance2.Position.Z)
				if vector2.Magnitude < 6 then
					flag = true
					break
				end
				humanoid:MoveTo(instance2.Position)
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
			if not flag then
				return fn(false)
			end
		end
		return fn(true)
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.grab(record, arg)
		local areaEggRecord, areaEggRecord2
		local instance = record.BottomCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
		local value = os.clock() + (arg or 2)
		local now = os.clock()
		local now2 = 0
		local now3 = 0
		local flag = false
		local connection
		pcall(function()
			connection = Core.EggCmds.AreaEggCarryStateChanged:Connect(function(info)
				if info and info.IsCarrying == true then
					flag = true
				end
			end)
		end)

		local function fn(arg)
			if connection then
				pcall(function()
					connection:Disconnect()
				end)
			end
			return arg
		end

		while os.clock() < value do
			local character = getCharacter()
			if not character then
				return fn(false)
			end
			local instance2 = hrp()
			if instance2 and (instance2.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude > 3 then
				character:PivotTo(instance)
				instance2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			local flag2 = os.clock() - now < 0.15
			if flag2 or os.clock() - now2 >= 0.08 then
				now2 = os.clock()
				pcall(function()
					Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(record.Uid)
				end)
			end
			if flag then
				areaEggRecord = nil
				pcall(function()
					areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(record.Uid)
				end)
				if areaEggRecord and areaEggRecord.State == "Carried" and areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId == Core.LP.UserId then
					return fn(true)
				end
				if getMyCarriedEgg() == record.Uid then
					return fn(true)
				end
				flag = false
			end
			if os.clock() - now3 >= 0.15 then
				now3 = os.clock()
				areaEggRecord2 = nil
				pcall(function()
					areaEggRecord2 = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(record.Uid)
				end)
				if areaEggRecord2 and areaEggRecord2.State == "Carried" then
					if areaEggRecord2.CarrierUserId == Core.LP.UserId then
						return fn(true)
					end
					return fn(false)
				end
				if not areaEggRecord2 or areaEggRecord2.State == "Claimed" then
					return fn(false)
				end
			end
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		return fn(false)
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.heldIsJunk(myCarriedEgg2)
		if not myCarriedEgg2 then
			return false
		end
		if SAE_ST.ITS.fuelUid and myCarriedEgg2 == SAE_ST.ITS.fuelUid then
			return true
		end
		local areaEggRecord
		pcall(function()
			areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(myCarriedEgg2)
		end)
		if not areaEggRecord then
			return true
		end
		return not SAE_ST.ITS.wanted(areaEggRecord)
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.wanted(info)
		if not info then
			return false
		end
		local rarityInfo, value = getRarityInfo(info.AssetCategory)
		local ok, result = pcall(matchesSelection, info, value, toSet(SAE_ST.stealNames), toSet(SAE_ST.minRarityNames))
		return ok and result and true or false
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.nearest(arg)
		local instance = hrp()
		local zero = instance and instance.Position or Vector3.zero
		local value
		local value2
		local value3
		local value4
		local ok, info = pcall(function()
			return Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
		end)
		if not ok or not info then
			return nil
		end
		for _, record in ipairs(info.Records or {}) do
			local flag = record.State ~= "Claimed" and record.State ~= "Carried" and not record.CarrierUserId
			if flag and record.BottomCFrame and not tostring(record.Uid):find("FirstAreaEgg") then
				local distance = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - zero).Magnitude
				if distance >= (arg or 0) then
					if not value4 or distance < value4 then
						value3, value4 = record, distance
					end
					if not SAE_ST.ITS.wanted(record) and (not value2 or distance < value2) then
						value, value2 = record, distance
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if value then
			return value, value2
		end
		return value3, value4
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.guardRoot(arg)
		local instance = Core.World()
		local areas = instance and instance:FindFirstChild("Areas")
		local guardAreas = areas and areas:FindFirstChild("GuardAreas")
		if not guardAreas then
			return nil
		end

		local function getPrimaryPart(instance)
			local guard = instance and instance:FindFirstChild("Guard")
			if not guard then
				return nil
			end
			if guard:IsA("BasePart") then
				return guard
			end
			return guard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or guard.PrimaryPart
		end

		if arg then
			return getPrimaryPart(guardAreas:FindFirstChild(arg))
		end
		local instance2 = hrp()
		local zero = instance2 and instance2.Position or Vector3.zero
		local value
		local value2
		for _, child in ipairs(guardAreas:GetChildren()) do
			local primaryPart = getPrimaryPart(child)
			if primaryPart then
				local distance = (primaryPart.Position - zero).Magnitude
				if not value2 or distance < value2 then
					value, value2 = primaryPart, distance
				end
			end
		end
		return value
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.dropIsOurs()
		pcall(function()
			immunityState.manualDropAt = os.clock()
			immunityState.lastEggUid = nil
			immunityState.lostAt = nil
		end)
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.release(eggUid, arg)
		if not eggUid then
			return false
		end
		local part = SAE_ST.ITS.guardRoot("Forest") or SAE_ST.ITS.guardRoot(nil)
		local value = os.clock() + (arg or 3)
		while os.clock() < value do
			SAE_ST.ITS.dropIsOurs()
			pcall(function()
				Core.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Shared", 1)
				local Remotes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
				Remotes.GuardPatrol.ForestStrike:FireServer({EggUid = eggUid, GuardCFrame = part and part.CFrame or hrp() and hrp().CFrame or CFrame.new()})
			end)
			task.wait(0.08)
			if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= eggUid then
				for j = 1, 12 do
					SAE_ST.ITS.dropIsOurs()
					Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end
				return true
			end
		end
