		return getMyCarriedEgg() ~= eggUid
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.dump(myCarriedEgg2, arg)
		if not myCarriedEgg2 then
			return true
		end
		local value = os.clock() + (arg or 2)
		while os.clock() < value do
			SAE_ST.ITS.dropIsOurs()
			pcall(function()
				Core.EggCmds.RequestDropHeldAreaEgg("PlayerRequest")
			end)
			task.wait(0.12)
			if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= myCarriedEgg2 then
				for j = 1, 12 do
					SAE_ST.ITS.dropIsOurs()
					Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end
				return true
			end
		end
		return getMyCarriedEgg() ~= myCarriedEgg2
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.reset()
		local character = getCharacter()
		local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
			return false
		end
		humanoid.Health = 0
		local instance
		local connection
		connection = Core.LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(arg)
			instance = arg
		end)
		local value = os.clock() + 12
		while os.clock() < value and not instance do
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
		if connection then
			pcall(function()
				connection:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		instance:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 8)
		task.wait(2.2)
		SAE_ST.ITS.refusals = 0
		return true
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.prime()
		if os.clock() < (SAE_ST.ITS.coolUntil or 0) then
			return false
		end
		if os.clock() - (SAE_ST.ITS.lastPrimeFail or 0) < 1.5 then
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST.ITS.lastPrimeTry = os.clock()
		SAE_ST.ITS.primed = false
		SAE_ST.ITS.takes = 0
		local myCarriedEgg2 = getMyCarriedEgg()
		if not myCarriedEgg2 then
			pcall(function()
				Core.EggCmds.RequestAreaEggSnapshot()
			end)
			local record
			for i = 1, 3 do
				record = SAE_ST.ITS.nearest(0)
				if record then
					break
				end
				task.wait(0.3)
			end
			if not record then
				return false
			end
			local value = record.BottomCFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
			pcall(function()
				SAE_ST.flyTo(value, math.max(lookup.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed, 400))
			end)
			if not SAE_ST.ITS.grab(record, 1.5) then
				SAE_ST.ITS.walk(record.BottomCFrame.Position, 45)
				local instance = hrp()
				if instance and (instance.Position - record.BottomCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 12 then
					if not SAE_ST.ITS.step(value, 12) then
						return false
					end
				end
			end
			SAE_ST.ITS.fuelUid = record.Uid
			if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= record.Uid and not SAE_ST.ITS.grab(record, 2) then
				SAE_ST.ITS.refusals = SAE_ST.ITS.refusals + 1
				SAE_ST.ITS.lastPrimeFail = os.clock()
				if SAE_ST.ITS.refusals >= 3 then
					SAE_ST.ITS.coolUntil = os.clock() + SAE_ST.ITS.coolSeconds
					SAE_ST.ITS.refusals = 0
					SAE_ST.ITS.locked = true
					if SAE_ST.instantHop and SAE_ST.serverHop then
						if SAE_ST.notify then
							SAE_ST.notify("Instant steal: switching server to keep going")
						end
						task.spawn(function()
							pcall(SAE_ST.serverHop)
						end)
					elseif SAE_ST.notify then
						SAE_ST.notify("Instant steal: this server stopped accepting pickups - rejoin or hop")
					end
				end
				return false
			end
			myCarriedEgg2 = record.Uid
		end
		if SAE_ST.ITS.release(myCarriedEgg2, 3) then
			SAE_ST.ITS.primed = true
			SAE_ST.ITS.primedAt = os.clock()
			SAE_ST.ITS.takes = 0
			SAE_ST.ITS.refusals = 0
			SAE_ST.ITS.lastPrimeFail = 0
			SAE_ST.ITS.fuelUid = nil
			return true
		end
		SAE_ST.ITS.lastPrimeFail = os.clock()
		return false
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.ready()
		if not SAE_ST.ITS.primed then
			return false
		end
		if os.clock() - (SAE_ST.ITS.primedAt or 0) > SAE_ST.ITS.windowSeconds then
			SAE_ST.ITS.primed = false
			return false
		end
		if SAE_ST.ITS.takes >= SAE_ST.ITS.takesPerWindow then
			SAE_ST.ITS.primed = false
			return false
		end
		return true
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.bank(_, arg)
		local flag = false
		local connection
		pcall(function()
			connection = Core.EggCmds.AreaEggCarryStateChanged:Connect(function(info)
				if info and info.IsCarrying == false then
					flag = true
				end
			end)
		end)
		local value = os.clock() + (arg or 2.5)
		local now = 0
		while os.clock() < value do
			local instance = hrp()
			if instance and (instance.Position - myPlotSafeCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 6 then
				instance.CFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
				instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			if flag then
				break
			end
			if os.clock() - now >= 0.15 then
				now = os.clock()
				if getMyCarriedEgg() == nil then
					break
				end
			end
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		end
		if connection then
			pcall(function()
				connection:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
		return getMyCarriedEgg() == nil
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.stealOne(record)
		if not record or not record.BottomCFrame then
			return false
		end
		local value = record.BottomCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
		local areaEggRecord
		pcall(function()
			areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(record.Uid)
		end)
		if areaEggRecord and (areaEggRecord.State == "Claimed" or areaEggRecord.State == "Carried" or areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId) then
			SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
			return false
		end
		pcall(function()
			local Remotes = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes)
			if record.AreaId then
				Remotes.ZoneProbe.AnchorForZone:FireServer(record.AreaId)
			end
		end)
		if not SAE_ST.ITS.ready() then
			SAE_ST.ITS.primed = false
			return false
		end
		if not SAE_ST.ITS.place(value, 2) then
			SAE_ST.ITS.primed = false
			return false
		end
		if not SAE_ST.ITS.grab(record, 4) then
			SAE_ST.ITS.place(myPlotSafeCFrame, 2)
			local flag = false
			local value2 = os.clock() + 1.2
			local now = 0
			while os.clock() < value2 do
				if os.clock() - now >= 0.1 then
					now = os.clock()
					pcall(function()
						Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(record.Uid)
					end)
				end
				Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				local instance = hrp()
				if instance and (instance.Position - myPlotSafeCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 6 then
					instance.CFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
					instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				if getMyCarriedEgg() == record.Uid then
					flag = true
					break
				end
			end
			if not flag then
				SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
				SAE_ST.ITS.primed = false
				return false
			end
		end
		SAE_ST.ITS.takes = SAE_ST.ITS.takes + 1
		local myCarriedEgg2 = getMyCarriedEgg()
		if myCarriedEgg2 ~= record.Uid then
			if myCarriedEgg2 and SAE_ST.ITS.heldIsJunk(myCarriedEgg2) then
				if not SAE_ST.ITS.dump(myCarriedEgg2, 2) then
					SAE_ST.ITS.release(myCarriedEgg2, 2)
				end
				SAE_ST.ITS.fuelUid = nil
			end
			return false
		end
		if not SAE_ST.ITS.place(myPlotSafeCFrame, 2) then
			SAE_ST.ITS.primed = false
			local flag = false
			if SAE_ST.ITS.prime() then
				flag = SAE_ST.ITS.place(myPlotSafeCFrame, 3)
			end
			if not flag then
				pcall(function()
					returnHome(lookup.ReturnSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed or 300)
				end)
			end
		end
		SAE_ST.bait.lastArea = record.AreaId
		if SAE_ST.bait.wanted(record.AreaId) then
			pcall(SAE_ST.bait.prime, record)
			if getMyCarriedEgg() ~= record.Uid then
				SAE_ST.collectCooldown[record.Uid] = os.clock()
				return false
			end
		end
		local result = SAE_ST.bait.invCount()
		local result2 = SAE_ST.ITS.bank(record.Uid, 2.5)
		if not result2 then
			SAE_ST.ITS.place(myPlotSafeCFrame, 2)
			result2 = SAE_ST.ITS.bank(record.Uid, 2)
		end
		if not result2 then
			pcall(function()
				result2 = moveTo(record.Uid)
			end)
		end
		if not result2 then
			pcall(function()
				result2 = unequipTools()
			end)
		end
		if result2 then
			result2 = SAE_ST.bait.judge(record.AreaId, result)
		end
		if result2 then
			pcall(function()
				Core.EggCmds.RequestAreaEggSnapshot()
			end)
		end
		return result2
	end

	function SAE_ST.ITS.storageFull()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local value = 0
		for _ in pairs(save and save.EggInventory or {}) do
			value = value + 1
		end
		return value >= 115, value
	end

	function SAE_ST.runInstantSteal(arg)
		if isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive() then
			return false
		end
		while not isAlive() do
			task.wait(0.4)
		end
		local result, value = SAE_ST.ITS.storageFull()
		if result then
			if SAE_ST.notify then
				SAE_ST.notify(("Egg storage full (%d/115) - hatch or place eggs to keep stealing"):format(value))
			end
			task.wait(2)
			return false
		end
		if SAE_ST.instantView then
			pcall(function()
				SAE_ST.ITS.decoyOn(myPlotSafeCFrame)
			end)
		end
		local myCarriedEgg2 = getMyCarriedEgg()
		if myCarriedEgg2 then
			if SAE_ST.ITS.heldIsJunk(myCarriedEgg2) then
				if not SAE_ST.ITS.dump(myCarriedEgg2, 2) then
					SAE_ST.ITS.release(myCarriedEgg2, 2)
				end
				SAE_ST.ITS.fuelUid = nil
				return false
			end
			return pcall(unequipTools) and true or false
		end
		if not SAE_ST.ITS.ready() then
			if not SAE_ST.ITS.prime() then
				task.wait(os.clock() < (SAE_ST.ITS.coolUntil or 0) and 1 or 0.35)
				return false
			end
		end
		return SAE_ST.ITS.stealOne(arg)
	end

	local function getCFrame()
		local ok, info = pcall(Core.PlotCmds.GetPlotData)
		if ok and info then
			local petArea = info.PetArea
			if typeof(petArea) == "Instance" and petArea:IsA("BasePart") then
				return petArea.CFrame, petArea.Size
			end
			if typeof(petArea) == "CFrame" then
				return petArea, nil
			end
			if typeof(petArea) == "Vector3" then
				return CFrame.new(petArea), nil
			end
			local centerPoint = info.CenterPoint
			if typeof(centerPoint) == "Instance" and centerPoint:IsA("BasePart") then
				return centerPoint.CFrame, centerPoint.Size
			end
			if typeof(centerPoint) == "CFrame" then
				return centerPoint, nil
			end
			if typeof(centerPoint) == "Vector3" then
				return CFrame.new(centerPoint), nil
			end
		end
		return CFrame.new(getPlotAnchor()), nil
	end

	local PLOT_CAPACITY = 30
	local MAX_PLACE_FAILURES = 6

	local function countOwnedEggs()
		local value = 0
		local value2 = 0
		pcall(function()
			for _, info in pairs(Core.EggCmds.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords(Core.LP.UserId) or {}) do
				if info.Placement then
					value = value + 1
				else
					value2 = value2 + 1
				end
			end
		end)
		return value, math.max(0, PLOT_CAPACITY - value), value2
	end

	local function placeEggs(placeSelectedOnly, placeNames, placeRarities, getAutoSteal, arg)
		SAE_ST.placing = true
		local value = 0
		local set = placeSelectedOnly and toSet(placeNames) or nil
		local savedPlaceRarities = placeRarities and #placeRarities > 0 and placeRarities or nil
		local set2 = SAE_ST.placeMutations and #SAE_ST.placeMutations > 0 and toSet(SAE_ST.placeMutations) or nil
		local flag = false
		local plotFullAt = 0
		pcall(function()
			local plotData = Core.PlotCmds.GetPlotData()
			if not plotData then
				return
			end
			local result, value2 = countOwnedEggs()
			if value2 <= 0 then
				flag = true
				plotFullAt = result
				return
			end
			if SAE_ST.plotFull and result == SAE_ST.plotFullAt then
				flag = true
				plotFullAt = result
				return
			end
			local petArea = plotData.PetArea
			local centerPoint = plotData.CenterPoint
			local CFrame2
			if typeof(centerPoint) == "Instance" and centerPoint:IsA("BasePart") then
				CFrame2 = centerPoint.CFrame
			elseif typeof(centerPoint) == "CFrame" then
				CFrame2 = centerPoint
			elseif typeof(centerPoint) == "Vector3" then
				CFrame2 = CFrame.new(centerPoint)
			end
			if not CFrame2 and typeof(petArea) == "Instance" and petArea:IsA("BasePart") then
				CFrame2 = petArea.CFrame
			end
			if not CFrame2 then
				return
			end
			local savedPetArea
			if typeof(petArea) == "Instance" and petArea:IsA("BasePart") then
				savedPetArea = petArea
			elseif typeof(petArea) == "Vector3" then
				savedPetArea = {Position = petArea, Size = Vector3.zero}
			else
				return
			end
			local value3 = savedPetArea.Position.Y + savedPetArea.Size.Y / 2 + 0.6
			local value4 = savedPetArea.Position.X - savedPetArea.Size.X / 2 + 4
			local value5 = savedPetArea.Position.X + savedPetArea.Size.X / 2 - 4
			local value6 = savedPetArea.Position.Z - savedPetArea.Size.Z / 2 + 4
			local value7 = savedPetArea.Position.Z + savedPetArea.Size.Z / 2 - 4
			if value5 <= value4 or value7 <= value6 then
				return
			end
			local ownerRuntimeRecords = Core.EggCmds.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords(Core.LP.UserId)
			if not ownerRuntimeRecords then
				return
			end
			local value8 = 6
			local maxValue = math.max(2, math.floor((value5 - value4) / value8))
			local maxValue2 = math.max(2, math.floor((value7 - value6) / value8))
			local items = {}
			for i = 0, maxValue2 - 1 do
				for j = 0, maxValue - 1 do
					local value9 = value4 + (value5 - value4) * (j / math.max(maxValue - 1, 1))
					local value10 = value6 + (value7 - value6) * (i / math.max(maxValue2 - 1, 1))
					local value11 = CFrame.new(value9, value3, value10) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(math.random(-8, 8)), 0)
					table.insert(items, CFrame2:ToObjectSpace(value11))
				end
			end
			if #items == 0 then
				return
			end
			local vector2 = Vector3.new(savedPetArea.Position.X, 0, savedPetArea.Position.Z)
			table.sort(items, function(amount, amount2)
				local instance = CFrame2 * amount
				local instance2 = CFrame2 * amount2
				return (Vector3.new(instance.Position.X, 0, instance.Position.Z) - vector2).Magnitude < (Vector3.new(instance2.Position.X, 0, instance2.Position.Z) - vector2).Magnitude
			end)

			local function getLocalCFrame(placement)
				if typeof(placement) == "CFrame" then
					return placement
				end
				if type(placement) == "table" then
					if typeof(placement.LocalCFrame) == "CFrame" then
						return placement.LocalCFrame
					end
					if typeof(placement.CFrame) == "CFrame" then
						return placement.CFrame
					end
				end
				return nil
			end

			local seen = {}
			for _, info in pairs(ownerRuntimeRecords) do
				if info.Placement then
					local localCFrame = getLocalCFrame(info.Placement)
					if localCFrame then
						local instance = CFrame2 * localCFrame
						seen[string.format("%.0f_%.0f", instance.Position.X, instance.Position.Z)] = true
					end
				end
			end
			plotFullAt = 0
			for _, info in pairs(ownerRuntimeRecords) do
				if info.Placement then
					plotFullAt = plotFullAt + 1
				end
			end
			if SAE_ST.plotFull then
				SAE_ST.plotFull = false
			end
			local result2 = hrp()
			if result2 then
				travelTo(savedPetArea.Position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), SAE_ST.tweenSpeed)
				task.wait(0.2)
			end
			local items2 = {}
			for uid, info in pairs(ownerRuntimeRecords) do
				if info.Placement == nil and SAE_ST.placeEnabled[uid] ~= false then
					local assetCategory = info.AssetCategory
					local savedAssetCategory = not set or assetCategory and set[assetCategory] == true
					local contains = true
					if savedPlaceRarities and assetCategory then
						local rarityInfo, value9 = getRarityInfo(assetCategory)
						contains = listContains(savedPlaceRarities, value9)
					end
					if arg and assetCategory then
						local rarityInfo, value9 = getRarityInfo(assetCategory)
						if value9 ~= arg then
							contains = false
						end
					end
					local flag2 = true
					if set2 then
						flag2 = false
						for _, mutation in ipairs(info.Mutations or {}) do
							if set2[tostring(mutation)] then
								flag2 = true
								break
							end
						end
					end
					local flag3 = true
					local val = 0
					if SAE_ST.placeValue ~= "" then
						val = getOwnedEggValue(info)
						local amount = parseAmount(SAE_ST.placeValue)
						if amount then
							flag3 = val >= amount
						end
					end
					if savedAssetCategory and contains and flag2 and flag3 then
						table.insert(items2, {uid = uid, cat = assetCategory, val = val})
					end
				end
			end
			if SAE_ST.placeValue ~= "" then
				table.sort(items2, function(info, info2)
					return info.val > info2.val
				end)
			end
			while #items2 > value2 do
				table.remove(items2)
			end
			local value9 = 0
			local value10 = 0
			local value11 = 0
			for _, info in ipairs(items2) do
				if getAutoSteal and getAutoSteal() then
					break
				end
				if value >= value2 then
					flag = true
					break
				end
				local value12
				for j = 1, #items do
					value9 = value9 % #items + 1
					local instance = CFrame2 * items[value9]
					if not seen[string.format("%.0f_%.0f", instance.Position.X, instance.Position.Z)] then
						value12 = items[value9]
						break
					end
				end
				if not value12 then
					flag = true
					break
				end
				local instance = CFrame2 * value12
				pcall(function()
					Core.EggCmds.RequestEquipTool(info.uid)
				end)
				local ok, result3, value13 = pcall(function()
					return Core.EggCmds.RequestPlaceEgg(info.uid, value12)
				end)
				seen[string.format("%.0f_%.0f", instance.Position.X, instance.Position.Z)] = true
				if ok and result3 then
					value = value + 1
					value11 = 0
					value10 = 0
				else
					local text = tostring(value13 or "")
					value10 = value10 + 1
					if text:find("closer") then
						value11 = value11 + 1
						if value11 >= MAX_PLACE_FAILURES then
							flag = true
							break
						end
					else
						value11 = 0
						if value10 >= 25 then
							flag = true
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		SAE_ST.placing = false
		if flag then
			SAE_ST.plotFull = true
			SAE_ST.plotFullAt = plotFullAt
		end
		return value, flag
	end

	local function hatchEggs(getAutoSteal)
		SAE_ST.hatching = true
		local value = 0
		pcall(function()
			for key in pairs(Core.EggCmds.GetOwnerRuntimeRecords(Core.LP.UserId)) do
				if getAutoSteal and getAutoSteal() then
					break
				end
				if Core.EggCmds.IsLocalEggReady(key) then
					if Core.EggCmds.RequestHatchEgg(key) then
						value = value + 1
						pcall(function()
							Core.EggCmds.RequestCompleteHatchEgg(key)
						end)
						SAE_ST.plotFull = false
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		SAE_ST.hatching = false
		return value
	end

	local function equipBest()
		pcall(function()
			Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Backpack.EQUIP_BEST)
		end)
	end

	local function claimOfflineEarnings()
		local ok, info = pcall(function()
			return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.OfflineAssets.GET_SUMMARY)
		end)
		if ok and info and (info.ClaimableAmount or 0) > 0 then
			pcall(function()
				Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.OfflineAssets.REQUEST_REDEEM, {Kind = "Claim"})
			end)
		end
	end

	local function shouldSell(category)
		local sellNames = SAE_ST.sellNames and #SAE_ST.sellNames > 0
		local sellRarities = SAE_ST.sellRarities and #SAE_ST.sellRarities > 0
		if not sellNames and not sellRarities then
			return false
		end
		if sellNames and not listContains(SAE_ST.sellNames, category) then
			return false
		end
		if sellRarities then
			local rarityInfo, value = getRarityInfo(category)
			if not listContains(SAE_ST.sellRarities, value) then
				return false
			end
		end
		return true
	end

	local function processInventorySales()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		if not save then
			return 0
		end
		local seen = {}
		for _, equippedAsset in ipairs(save.EquippedAssets or {}) do
			seen[equippedAsset] = true
		end
		local value = 0
		for key, value2 in pairs(save.Inventory or {}) do
			if not seen[key] then
				local info = deserializeAsset(value2)
				if info and not info.InFuse and not info.IsFavorite and shouldSell(info.Category) then
					local ok = pcall(function()
						Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.ActiveAssets.REQUEST_SELL, key)
					end)
					if ok then
						value = value + 1
					end
					if value % 10 == 0 then
						task.wait(0.05)
					end
					if value >= 60 then
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local stats = SAE_ST.stats
		stats.sells = stats.sells + value
		return value
	end

	local function shouldFavorite(category)
		if SAE_ST.favMode == "species" then
			local favSpecies = SAE_ST.favSpecies and #SAE_ST.favSpecies > 0
			if not favSpecies then
				return false
			end
			return listContains(SAE_ST.favSpecies, category)
		end
		local favRarities = SAE_ST.favRarities and #SAE_ST.favRarities > 0
		if not favRarities then
			return false
		end
		local rarityInfo, value = getRarityInfo(category)
		return listContains(SAE_ST.favRarities, value)
	end

	local function fire()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		if not save then
			return 0
		end
		local value = 0
		for key, value2 in pairs(save.Inventory or {}) do
			local info = deserializeAsset(value2)
			if info and not info.IsFavorite and shouldFavorite(info.Category) then
				pcall(function()
					Core.Network.Fire(Core.Network.NET_MAP.AssetInventory.SET_FAVORITE, key, true)
				end)
				value = value + 1
				if value % 10 == 0 then
					task.wait(0.05)
				end
			end
		end
		return value
	end

	local function sort2(arg, arg2)
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local seen = {}
		for _, equippedAsset in ipairs(save.EquippedAssets or {}) do
			seen[equippedAsset] = true
		end
		local set = arg and toSet(arg2) or nil
		local lookup = {}
		for uid, value in pairs(save.Inventory or {}) do
			if not seen[uid] then
				local info = deserializeAsset(value)
				if info and not info.InFuse and not info.IsFavorite then
					local category = info.Category
					if not set or set[category] == true then
						if not lookup[category] then
							lookup[category] = {}
						end
						table.insert(lookup[category], {uid = uid, d = info})
					end
				end
			end
		end
		for _, list in pairs(lookup) do
			if #list >= 3 then
				table.sort(list, function(info, info2)
					local rarityInfo = getRarityInfo(info.d.Category)
					local rarityInfo2 = getRarityInfo(info2.d.Category)
					return rarityInfo < rarityInfo2
				end)
				return {list[1].uid, list[2].uid, list[3].uid}
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function fusePets(fuseSelectedOnly, fuseNames)
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		if not save then
			return false
		end
		if save.FusionEggReward ~= false then
			local list = {
				pcall(function()
					return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.FuseMachine.COMPLETE_REVEAL)
				end),
			}
			if list[1] and list[2] then
				task.wait(0.05)
				return true
			end
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Fuse: reward claim failed - " .. tostring(list[3] or list[2] or "server error"))
			return false
		end
		if save.FusionLocked then
			return false
		end
		local value = 0
		for _ in pairs(save.EggInventory or {}) do
			value = value + 1
		end
		if value >= 115 then
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Fuse: egg inventory full")
			return false
		end
		local list = sort2(fuseSelectedOnly, fuseNames)
		if not list then
			return false
		end
		pcall(function()
			Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.FuseMachine.ACKNOWLEDGE_INFO)
		end)
		for _, item in ipairs(list) do
			local list2 = {
				pcall(function()
					return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.FuseMachine.INSERT_MOB, item)
				end),
			}
			if not (list2[1] and list2[2]) then
				SAE_ST.notify("Auto Fuse: insert failed - " .. tostring(list2[3] or list2[2] or "server error"))
				return false
			end
			task.wait(0.02)
		end
		local list2 = {
			pcall(function()
				return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.FuseMachine.START_FUSE)
			end),
		}
		if not (list2[1] and list2[2]) then
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Fuse: start failed - " .. tostring(list2[3] or list2[2] or "server error"))
			return false
		end
		local stats = SAE_ST.stats
		stats.fused = stats.fused + 1
		task.wait(0.06)
		local list3 = {
			pcall(function()
				return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.FuseMachine.COMPLETE_REVEAL)
			end),
		}
		if not (list3[1] and list3[2]) then
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Fuse: reward claim failed - " .. tostring(list3[3] or list3[2] or "server error"))
			return false
		end
		return true
	end

	local function unequipTreadmill()
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Treadmills.REQUEST_UNEQUIP)
		end)
		return ok and result == true
	end

	local function isOnTreadmill()
		if type(SAE_ST.onBelt) == "boolean" then
			return SAE_ST.onBelt
		end
		local ok, list = pcall(function()
			return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Treadmills.REQUEST_ACTIVE_RENDER_SNAPSHOT)
		end)
		if not ok or type(list) ~= "table" then
			return nil
		end
		return table.find(list, Core.LP.UserId) ~= nil
	end

	do
		local function getAutoSteal()
			return SAE_ST.autoSteal and not SAE_ST.autoTreadmill and not SAE_ST.wantTreadmill
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, signal = pcall(function()
				return Core.Network.Fired(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Treadmills.ACTIVE_TREADMILL_EVENT)
			end)
			if not ok or not signal then
				return
			end
			pcall(function()
				local list = Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Treadmills.REQUEST_ACTIVE_RENDER_SNAPSHOT)
				if type(list) == "table" then
					SAE_ST.onBelt = table.find(list, Core.LP.UserId) ~= nil
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				signal:Connect(function(arg)
					SAE_ST.onBelt = arg ~= nil
					if arg == nil then
						return
					end
					if not getAutoSteal() then
						return
					end
					task.spawn(function()
						task.wait(0.05)
						if getAutoSteal() then
							unequipTreadmill()
						end
					end)
				end)
			end)
		end)
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(3)
			while not SAE_ST.stale() do
				if getAutoSteal() and isOnTreadmill() == true then
					unequipTreadmill()
				end
				task.wait(15)
			end
		end)
	end

	local function getTreadmillPart()
		local plotData = Core.PlotCmds.GetPlotData()
		local child = Core.Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(plotData and plotData.Slot or ""))
		if child then
			local treadmillBottom = child:FindFirstChild("TreadmillBottom")
			if treadmillBottom then
				return treadmillBottom.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
			end
		end
		if plotData and plotData.CenterPoint then
			return plotData.CenterPoint.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		end
		return nil
	end

	do
		local lastHudPulseAt = 0
		local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

		local function pulseSpeedHeadline()
			local now = os.clock()
			if now - lastHudPulseAt < 0.05 then
				return
			end
			lastHudPulseAt = now
			pcall(function()
				local playerGui = Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
				local hud = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("HUD")
				if not hud then
					return
				end
				local list = {
					hud:FindFirstChild("GameHUD") and hud.GameHUD:FindFirstChild("BottomLeft") and hud.GameHUD.BottomLeft:FindFirstChild("Speed"),
					hud:FindFirstChild("TradmilHud") and hud.TradmilHud:FindFirstChild("BottomLeft") and hud.TradmilHud.BottomLeft:FindFirstChild("Speed"),
				}
				for _, parent in ipairs(list) do
					if parent and parent:IsA("GuiObject") then
						local UIScale = parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
						UIScale.Parent = parent
						UIScale.Scale = 0.7
						Core.TweenService:Create(UIScale, tweenInfo, {Scale = 1}):Play()
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		do
			local function clear(arg)
				pcall(function()
					local character = Core.LP.Character
					if character then
						for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("Trail") then
								descendant.Enabled = not arg
								if arg then
									pcall(function()
										descendant:Clear()
									end)
								end
							end
						end
					end
					local playerGui = Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
					if playerGui then
						for _, child in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == "PlusOne" or child.Name == "SpeedGainPopup" then
								if arg then
									pcall(function()
										child:Destroy()
									end)
								end
							end
						end
						local speedGainAnimation = playerGui:FindFirstChild("SpeedGainAnimation")
						if speedGainAnimation and speedGainAnimation:IsA("ScreenGui") then
							speedGainAnimation.Enabled = not arg
						end
						local hud = playerGui:FindFirstChild("HUD")
						if hud then
							local gameHUD = hud:FindFirstChild("GameHUD")
							local tradmilHud = hud:FindFirstChild("TradmilHud")
							if arg then
								if gameHUD and not gameHUD.Visible then
									gameHUD.Visible = true
								end
								if tradmilHud and tradmilHud.Visible then
									tradmilHud.Visible = false
								end
								local money = gameHUD and gameHUD:FindFirstChild("BottomLeft") and gameHUD.BottomLeft:FindFirstChild("Money")
								if money and not money.Visible then
									money.Visible = true
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			local function equipTreadmill()
				local list = {
					pcall(function()
						return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Treadmills.REQUEST_EQUIP)
					end),
				}
				if not list[1] then
					return false, tostring(list[2])
				end
				return list[2] == true
			end

			function SAE_ST.prepareTreadmillBoost(part, humanoid)
				if not part or not humanoid then
					return false
				end
				SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting = true
				local savedMyPlotSafeCFrame = myPlotSafeCFrame
				if savedMyPlotSafeCFrame then
					if (part.Position - savedMyPlotSafeCFrame.Position).Magnitude > 15 then
						down(part, humanoid, savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, nil, 600, false, false)
					end
					part.CFrame = savedMyPlotSafeCFrame
					part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				local treadmillPart = getTreadmillPart()
				if not treadmillPart then
					SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting = false
					return false
				end
				local currentCamera = Core.Workspace.CurrentCamera
				local cameraType = currentCamera and currentCamera.CameraType
				local CFrame2 = currentCamera and currentCamera.CFrame
				local character = getCharacter()
				local CFrame3 = part.CFrame
				local clone

				local function fn()
					pcall(function()
						if clone then
							clone:Destroy()
							clone = nil
						end
						if character then
							for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
								if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
									descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
								end
							end
						end
						if currentCamera then
							currentCamera.CameraType = cameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Scriptable and cameraType or Enum.CameraType.Custom
							local humanoid = getCharacter() and getCharacter():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							if humanoid then
								currentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
							end
							if CFrame2 then
								currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame2
							end
						end
						clear(false)
					end)
				end

				clear(true)
				pcall(function()
					if character and CFrame3 then
						character.Archivable = true
						clone = character:Clone()
						clone.Name = "SZ_VisualGhost"
						for _, descendant in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") or descendant:IsA("Sound") then
								descendant:Destroy()
							elseif descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
								descendant.CanCollide = false
								descendant.Anchored = true
								descendant.CastShadow = true
							end
						end
						clone.Parent = Core.Workspace
						for _, descendant in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
								descendant.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
							end
						end
					end
				end)
				if currentCamera and CFrame2 then
					currentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
					currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame2
				end
				local cframe = CFrame.new(treadmillPart)
				down(part, humanoid, cframe, nil, 600, false, false)
				part.CFrame = cframe
				part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				if isOnTreadmill() then
					pcall(unequipTreadmill)
					task.wait(0.05)
				end
				local flag = false
				local value = os.clock() + 2
				while os.clock() < value do
					local ok, result = pcall(equipTreadmill)
					if ok and result == true or isOnTreadmill() == true then
						flag = true
						break
					end
					part.CFrame = cframe
					part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					task.wait(0.08)
				end
				local now = os.clock()
				while os.clock() - now < 0.7 do
					part.CFrame = cframe
					part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					pulseSpeedHeadline()
					Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end
				pcall(unequipTreadmill)
				task.wait(0.05)
				if savedMyPlotSafeCFrame then
					down(part, humanoid, savedMyPlotSafeCFrame, nil, 600, false, false)
					part.CFrame = savedMyPlotSafeCFrame
					part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
				end
				fn()
				SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting = false
				return true
			end
		end
		SAE_ST.pulseSpeedHeadline = pulseSpeedHeadline
	end
	do
		local function requireIfPresent(arg)
			if not arg then
				return nil
			end
			local ok, result = pcall(require, arg)
			return ok and result or nil
		end

		local function makeWritable(arg)
			if not arg then
				return
			end
			if setreadonly then
				pcall(setreadonly, arg, false)
			end
			if makewritable then
				pcall(makewritable, arg)
			end
		end

		local function patchHud()
			pcall(function()
				local info = requireIfPresent(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Client") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client:FindFirstChild("Hud"))
				if info then
					makeWritable(info)
					if info.SetTreadmillActive then
						local setTreadmillActive = info.SetTreadmillActive

						function info.SetTreadmillActive(arg)
							if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting then
								local frame = info.Frame and info.Frame("Game")
								local frame2 = info.Frame and info.Frame("Treadmill")
								if frame then
									frame.Visible = true
								end
								if frame2 then
									frame2.Visible = false
								end
								return
							end
							return setTreadmillActive(arg)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local info = requireIfPresent(Core.LP.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("GUI") and Core.LP.PlayerScripts.GUI:FindFirstChild("TreadmillUI") and Core.LP.PlayerScripts.GUI.TreadmillUI:FindFirstChild("Visibility"))
				if info then
					makeWritable(info)
					if info.Apply then
						local apply = info.Apply

						function info.Apply(arg)
							if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting then
								local hud = Core.LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("HUD")
								if hud then
									local gameHUD = hud:FindFirstChild("GameHUD")
									local tradmilHud = hud:FindFirstChild("TradmilHud")
									if gameHUD then
										gameHUD.Visible = true
									end
									if tradmilHud then
										tradmilHud.Visible = false
									end
								end
								return
							end
							return apply(arg)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local showGainBadge, playWalk, play, setHeadlinePulsing
				local items = {}
				pcall(function()
					local list = requireIfPresent(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Client") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client:FindFirstChild("UI") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.UI:FindFirstChild("Effects") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.UI.Effects:FindFirstChild("SpeedGainPopup"))
					if list then
						table.insert(items, list)
					end
				end)
				pcall(function()
					local list = requireIfPresent(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Client") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client:FindFirstChild("UI") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.UI:FindFirstChild("VFX") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Client.UI.VFX:FindFirstChild("SpeedGainPopup"))
					if list then
						table.insert(items, list)
					end
				end)
				for _, info in ipairs(items) do
					makeWritable(info)
					pcall(function()
						if info.ShowGainBadge and debug and debug.getupvalues and hookfunction then
							local value = debug.getupvalues(info.ShowGainBadge)[2]
							if type(value) == "function" then
								local value2 = debug.getupvalues(value)[8]
								if type(value2) == "function" then
									local original
									original = hookfunction(value2, function(arg)
										SAE_ST.pulseSpeedHeadline()
										return original(arg)
									end)
								end
							end
						end
					end)
					if info.ShowGainBadge then
						showGainBadge = info.ShowGainBadge

						function info.ShowGainBadge(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							SAE_ST.pulseSpeedHeadline()
							if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting then
								if arg4 then
									pcall(arg4)
								end
								return
							end
							return showGainBadge(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
						end
					end
					if info.PlayWalk then
						playWalk = info.PlayWalk

						function info.PlayWalk(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							SAE_ST.pulseSpeedHeadline()
							if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting then
								if arg4 then
									pcall(arg4)
								end
								return
							end
							return playWalk(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
						end
					end
					if info.Play then
						play = info.Play

						function info.Play(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
							SAE_ST.pulseSpeedHeadline()
							if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting then
								if arg4 then
									pcall(arg4)
								end
								return
							end
							return play(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
						end
					end
					if info.SetHeadlinePulsing then
						setHeadlinePulsing = info.SetHeadlinePulsing

						function info.SetHeadlinePulsing(arg, arg2)
							if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting then
								return
							end
							return setHeadlinePulsing(arg, arg2)
						end
					end
				end
				pcall(function()
					local info = requireIfPresent(Core.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Shared") and Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared:FindFirstChild("Remotes"))
					if info and info.Treadmill and info.Treadmill.SpeedGained then
						info.Treadmill.SpeedGained.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
							SAE_ST.pulseSpeedHeadline()
						end)
					end
				end)
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local playerGui = Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
				if playerGui then
					playerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
						if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting and (instance.Name == "PlusOne" or instance.Name:find("SpeedGain")) then
							task.defer(function()
								if instance and instance.Parent then
									pcall(function()
										instance:Destroy()
									end)
								end
							end)
						end
					end)
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				local function fn(character)
					if not character then
						return
					end
					character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
						if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting and instance:IsA("Trail") then
							instance.Enabled = false
							pcall(function()
								instance:Clear()
							end)
						end
					end)
				end

				fn(Core.LP.Character)
				Core.LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(fn)
			end)
		end

		task.spawn(patchHud)
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		local treadmillPart
		while not SAE_ST.stale() do
			task.wait(1)
			if not (SAE_ST.treadmillWhenNoEggs and SAE_ST.autoSteal) then
				SAE_ST.wantTreadmill = false
				continue
			end
			if SAE_ST.busy or SAE_ST.repositioning or getMyCarriedEgg() then
				continue
			end
			if isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive() then
				continue
			end
			if #sort() > 0 then
				SAE_ST.wantTreadmill = false
				if isOnTreadmill() == true then
					pcall(unequipTreadmill)
				end
			else
				SAE_ST.wantTreadmill = true
				if isOnTreadmill() ~= true then
					treadmillPart = getTreadmillPart()
					if treadmillPart then
						SAE_ST.busy = true
						pcall(function()
							travelTo(treadmillPart, lookup.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed)
						end)
						SAE_ST.busy = false
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local function sort3()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local number = tonumber(save and save.TreadmillUpgradeLevel or 0) or 0
		local items = {}
		for id, info in pairs(Core.TreadmillsDir) do
			table.insert(items, {id = id, price = tonumber(info and info.Price or 0) or 0})
		end
		table.sort(items, function(info, info2)
			return info.price < info2.price
		end)
		local info = items[number + 1]
		if not info then
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Treadmill Upgrade: max level reached")
			return
		end
		local number2 = tonumber(save and save.Money or 0) or 0
		if number2 < info.price then
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Treadmill Upgrade: need $" .. string.format("%.0f", info.price) .. " (have $" .. string.format("%.0f", number2) .. ")")
			return
		end
		local list = {
			pcall(function()
				return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Treadmills.REQUEST_UPGRADE, info.id)
			end),
		}
		if not (list[1] and list[2]) then
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Treadmill Upgrade: server refused - " .. tostring(list[3] or list[2] or "unknown"))
		end
	end

	local function notify()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		if not save then
			return
		end
		local number = tonumber(save.BaseUpgradeLevel or 0) or 0
		local info
		pcall(function()
			local Bases = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Data.Bases)
			info = Bases.BASES[number + 1]
		end)
		if not info then
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Base Upgrade: max level reached")
			return
		end
		local number2 = tonumber(save.Money or 0) or 0
		if number2 < (info.Cost or 0) then
			SAE_ST.notify("Auto Base Upgrade: need $" .. string.format("%.0f", info.Cost or 0) .. " (have $" .. string.format("%.0f", number2) .. ")")
			return
		end
		pcall(function()
			Core.Network.Fire(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Plots.REQUEST_BASE_UPGRADE)
		end)
	end

	local function listBuyableTrails(arg, arg2)
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local trailInventory = save.TrailInventory or {}
		local set = arg and toSet(arg2) or nil
		local results = {}
		for id, player in pairs(Core.TrailsDir) do
			if not trailInventory[id] and player and player.Price then
				if not set or set[player.DisplayName or id] == true then
					table.insert(results, {id = id, price = tonumber(player.Price) or 0, mult = tonumber(player.SpeedMultiplier or 0) or 0})
				end
			end
		end
		table.sort(results, function(info, info2)
			return info.mult > info2.mult
		end)
		return results
	end

	local function buyTrails(buySelectedOnly, buyTrailNames)
		local value = 0
		for _, info in ipairs(listBuyableTrails(buySelectedOnly, buyTrailNames)) do
			local ok = pcall(function()
				return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Trails.REQUEST_PURCHASE, info.id)
			end)
			if ok then
				value = value + 1
			end
			task.wait(0.3)
		end
		return value
	end

	local function equipBestTrail()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local trailInventory = save.TrailInventory or {}
		local value
		local value2
		for key in pairs(trailInventory) do
			local info = Core.TrailsDir[key]
			local number = tonumber(info and info.SpeedMultiplier or 0) or 0
			if not value2 or number > value2 then
				value = key
				value2 = number
			end
		end
		if value then
			pcall(function()
				Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Trails.REQUEST_SELECT, value)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function equipBestGear()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local gearInventory = save.GearInventory or {}
		local value
		local value2
		for key, info in pairs(Core.GearsDir) do
			local number = tonumber(info and info.MoneyCost or 0) or 0
			if not value2 or number > value2 then
				value = key
				value2 = number
			end
		end
		if value and not gearInventory[value] then
			pcall(function()
				Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.GearShop.REQUEST_PURCHASE, value)
			end)
		end
		for key in pairs(gearInventory) do
			pcall(function()
				Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.ActiveAssets.REQUEST_EQUIP, key)
			end)
			task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end

	local function claimIndexRewards()
		pcall(function()
			Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Index.REQUEST_CLAIM_ALL)
		end)
	end

	local function claimGroupReward()
		pcall(function()
			Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.GroupReward.CLAIM_REWARD, true)
		end)
	end

	local function getAreaEggList()
		pcall(function()
			Core.EggCmds.RequestAreaEggSnapshot()
		end)
		local areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
		local set = SAE_ST.pursueSelectedOnly and toSet(SAE_ST.pursueNames) or nil
		local results = {}
		for _, record in ipairs(areaEggSnapshot.Records) do
			if record.State == "Carried" or record.State == "GuardCarried" then
				if not set or set[record.CarrierUserId and tostring(record.CarrierUserId) or "?"] == true then
					table.insert(results, record)
				end
			end
		end
		return results
	end

	local function requestEquipTool()
		local areaEggList = getAreaEggList()
		if #areaEggList == 0 then
			return false
		end
		local result = hrp()
		if not result then
			return false
		end
		local character = getCharacter()
		if not character then
			return false
		end
		local info = areaEggList[1]
		if not info.CarrierUserId then
			return false
		end
		local player = Core.Players:GetPlayerByUserId(info.CarrierUserId)
		local character2 = player and player.Character
		local humanoidRootPart = character2 and character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoidRootPart then
			local position = (info.BottomCFrame or info.BoundsCFrame).Position
			flyTo(raycastGround(position), SAE_ST.tweenSpeed)
			return true
		end
		local carrierGear = SAE_ST.carrierGear
		if carrierGear == "" or not carrierGear:find("Slap") then
			carrierGear = "Golden Slap"
		end
		pcall(function()
			Core.EggCmds.RequestEquipTool(carrierGear)
		end)
		result.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, -3))
		result.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		result.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		task.wait(0.3)
		local instance
		for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") then
				instance = child
				break
			end
		end
		if instance then
			pcall(function()
				instance:Activate() 
			end)
			SAE_ST.lastSlapAt = os.clock()
		end
		return true
	end

	local BAT_CONFIG = {reach = 17, swingGap = 0.6, stick = 3, timeout = 20}

	local function findEquippedTool()
		local character = getCharacter()
		if not character then
			return nil
		end
		for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") and (child:GetAttribute("IsBat") or child.Name:find("Bat")) then
				return child
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function getEquippedTool()
		local parent
		local equippedTool = findEquippedTool()
		if equippedTool then
			return equippedTool
		end
		local backpack = Core.LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		for _, child in ipairs(backpack and backpack:GetChildren() or {}) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") and (child:GetAttribute("IsBat") or child.Name:find("Bat")) then
				parent = getCharacter()
				if parent then
					pcall(function()
						child.Parent = parent
					end)
				end
				return findEquippedTool()
			end
		end
		pcall(function()
			Core.EggCmds.RequestEquipTool("Bat")
		end)
		return findEquippedTool()
	end

	local function listCarrierTargets()
		local set = toSet(SAE_ST.stealNames)
		local set2 = toSet(SAE_ST.minRarityNames)
		local results = {}
		for _, list in ipairs(getAreaEggList()) do
			if list.State == "Carried" and list.CarrierUserId and list.CarrierUserId ~= Core.LP.UserId then
				local rarityInfo, value = getRarityInfo(list.AssetCategory)
				if matchesSelection(list, value, set, set2) then
					table.insert(results, list)
				end
			end
		end
		return results
	end

	swingBatAtCarrier = function(uid, carrierUserId)
		if not uid or not carrierUserId then
			return false
		end
		local player = Core.Players:GetPlayerByUserId(carrierUserId)
		local humanoidRootPart = player and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoidRootPart then
			return false
		end
		local equippedTool = getEquippedTool()
		local flag = false
		local value = os.clock() + BAT_CONFIG.timeout
		local now = 0
		local now2 = 0
		pcall(function()
			tweenToCFrame(humanoidRootPart.CFrame, lookup.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed, false)
		end)
		while os.clock() < value do
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			local instance = hrp()
			if not instance or not player.Parent then
				break
			end
			local character = player.Character
			humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart then
				break
			end
			local areaEggRecord
			pcall(function()
				areaEggRecord = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggRecord(uid)
			end)
			if not areaEggRecord then
				break
			end
			if areaEggRecord.State == "Carried" and areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId == Core.LP.UserId then
				flag = true
				break
			end
			if areaEggRecord.State == "Claimed" then
				break
			end
			if areaEggRecord.State ~= "Carried" or areaEggRecord.CarrierUserId ~= carrierUserId then
				flag = recoverDroppedEgg(uid, 2)
				break
			end
			if (instance.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > BAT_CONFIG.stick then
				instance.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, BAT_CONFIG.stick)
				instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				instance.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end
			if not equippedTool then
				equippedTool = getEquippedTool()
			end
			if equippedTool and equippedTool.Parent and os.clock() - now >= BAT_CONFIG.swingGap then
				now = os.clock()
				pcall(function()
					equippedTool:Activate()
				end)
			end
			if os.clock() - now2 >= RECARRY_COOLDOWN then
				now2 = os.clock()
				pcall(function()
					Core.EggCmds.RequestCarryAreaEgg(uid)
				end)
			end
		end
		return flag
	end

	local function attackFirstCarrier()
		local carrierTargets = listCarrierTargets()
		if #carrierTargets == 0 then
			return false
		end
		local record = carrierTargets[1]
		SAE_ST.busy = true
		local flag = false
		pcall(function()
			flag = swingBatAtCarrier(record.Uid, record.CarrierUserId)
		end)
		SAE_ST.busy = false
		if flag and getMyCarriedEgg() == record.Uid then
			pcall(unequipTools)
		end
		return flag
	end

	local INCUBATOR_CONFIG = {incNames = {"IncubatorDead", "IncubatorBloomed"}, hitGap = 0.6, reach = 10}
	pcall(function()
		INCUBATOR_CONFIG.Dir = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Data.Sakura)
	end)

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.state()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		return save and save.Sakura or nil, save
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.prompt()
		for _, incName in ipairs(INCUBATOR_CONFIG.incNames) do
			local child = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild(incName)
			local root = child and child:FindFirstChild("Root")
			local promptAttachment = root and root:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
			local proximityPrompt = promptAttachment and promptAttachment:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
			if proximityPrompt and root:IsA("BasePart") then
				return proximityPrompt, root
			end
		end
		return nil, nil
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.hasCrane()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		if not save then
			return false
		end
		for _, value in pairs(save.Inventory or {}) do
			local ok, info = pcall(function()
				return deserializeAsset(value)
			end)
			if ok and info and tostring(info.Category) == "Crane" then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.open()
		if not INCUBATOR_CONFIG.hasCrane() then
			SAE_ST.notify("Incubator: no Crane in inventory")
			return false
		end
		local result, instance = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.prompt()
		if not result then
			SAE_ST.notify("Incubator: prompt not found")
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST.busy = true
		pcall(function()
			tweenToCFrame(CFrame.new(instance.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)), lookup.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed, false)
		end)
		pcall(function()
			result.Enabled = true
			result.HoldDuration = 0
			result.RequiresLineOfSight = false
			result.MaxActivationDistance = 9999
			if fireproximityprompt then
				fireproximityprompt(result)
			end
		end)
		SAE_ST.busy = false
		SAE_ST.notify("Incubator: prompt fired")
		return true
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.trees()
		local results = {}
		local seen = {}
		pcall(function()
			local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
			local treeTag = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.Dir and INCUBATOR_CONFIG.Dir.TreeTag or "SakuraBloomTree"
			for _, instance in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged(treeTag)) do
				if instance:IsDescendantOf(Core.Workspace) and not seen[instance] then
					seen[instance] = true
					table.insert(results, instance)
				end
			end
		end)
		local sakuraBloomTrees = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("SakuraBloomTrees")
		for _, child in ipairs(sakuraBloomTrees and sakuraBloomTrees:GetChildren() or {}) do
			if not seen[child] then
				seen[child] = true
				table.insert(results, child)
			end
		end
		return results
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.part(instance)
		if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
			return instance
		end
		return instance:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.breakOne()
		local info = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.state()
		if not info or not info.Unlocked then
			SAE_ST.notify("Break Tree: Sakura incubator not unlocked")
			return false
		end
		local list = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.trees()
		if #list == 0 then
			return false
		end
		local instance = hrp()
		if not instance then
			return false
		end
		table.sort(list, function(arg, arg2)
			local instance2 = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.part(arg)
			local instance3 = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.part(arg2)
			if not instance2 then
				return false
			end
			if not instance3 then
				return true
			end
			return (instance2.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude < (instance3.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude
		end)
		local instance2 = list[1]
		local instance3 = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.part(instance2)
		if not instance3 then
			return false
		end
		local character = getCharacter()
		if not character then
			return false
		end

		local function getTweenSpeed()
			return lookup.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed
		end

		local cframe = CFrame.new(instance3.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0))
		local equippedTool = getEquippedTool()
		SAE_ST.busy = true
		pcall(function()
			tweenToCFrame(cframe, getTweenSpeed(), false)
		end)
		local value = os.clock() + 30
		local now = 0
		local now2 = 0
		while os.clock() < value do
			Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			if not SAE_ST.autoBreakTree then
				break
			end
			if not instance2.Parent or not instance2:IsDescendantOf(Core.Workspace) then
				break
			end
			local instance4 = hrp()
			if not instance4 then
				break
			end
			local instance5 = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.part(instance2)
			if not instance5 then
				break
			end
			local vector2 = Vector3.new(instance4.Position.X - instance5.Position.X, 0, instance4.Position.Z - instance5.Position.Z)
			if vector2.Magnitude > INCUBATOR_CONFIG.reach * 0.5 then
				if os.clock() - now2 >= 0.5 then
					now2 = os.clock()
					pcall(function()
						tweenToCFrame(CFrame.new(instance5.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)), getTweenSpeed(), false)
					end)
				end
			end
			if not equippedTool or equippedTool.Parent ~= getCharacter() then
				equippedTool = getEquippedTool()
			end
			if equippedTool and equippedTool.Parent == getCharacter() and isAlive() and os.clock() - now >= INCUBATOR_CONFIG.hitGap then
				now = os.clock()
				pcall(function()
					Core.Network.Fire(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Sakura.REQUEST_HIT_TREE, instance2)
				end)
			end
		end
		SAE_ST.busy = false
		return true
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.deposit()
		if not INCUBATOR_CONFIG.Dir then
			return 0
		end
		local info, info2 = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.state()
		if not info or not info2 or info.Egg == false then
			return 0
		end
		local requiredCrystals = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.Dir.GetRequiredCrystals()
		if not requiredCrystals or requiredCrystals <= 0 then
			return 0
		end
		local value = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.Dir.GetFreeChargeCap(requiredCrystals) - (info.Deposited or 0)
		local floored = math.floor(math.max(0, math.min(info2.SakuraCrystals or 0, value)))
		if floored < 1 then
			return 0
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Sakura.REQUEST_DEPOSIT, floored)
		end)
		return ok and result and floored or 0
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.pickEgg()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		if not save then
			return nil
		end
		local incSelectBy = SAE_ST.incSelectBy
		local set = SAE_ST.incNames and #SAE_ST.incNames > 0 and toSet(SAE_ST.incNames) or nil
		local set2 = SAE_ST.incRarities and #SAE_ST.incRarities > 0 and toSet(SAE_ST.incRarities) or nil
		local value
		local value2
		for key, info in pairs(save.EggInventory or {}) do
			local assetCategory = info and info.AssetCategory
			if assetCategory and info.Placement == nil then
				local rarityInfo, value3 = getRarityInfo(assetCategory)
				local flag = true
				if incSelectBy == "Name" then
					flag = not set or set[assetCategory] == true
				elseif incSelectBy == "Rarity" then
					flag = not set2 or set2[value3] == true
				end
				if flag then
					local savedRarityInfo = rarityInfo
					if incSelectBy == "Value" then
						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							return Core.EggItemUtil.GetSellPrice(info)
						end)
						savedRarityInfo = ok and tonumber(result) or 0
					elseif incSelectBy == "Weight" then
						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							return Core.EggItemUtil.GetWeightKg(info)
						end)
						savedRarityInfo = ok and tonumber(result) or 0
					end
					if not value2 or savedRarityInfo > value2 then
						value, value2 = key, savedRarityInfo
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return value
	end

	function INCUBATOR_CONFIG.selectEgg()
		local info = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.state()
		if not info or not info.Unlocked then
			return false
		end
		if info.Egg ~= false then
			return false
		end
		local egg = INCUBATOR_CONFIG.pickEgg()
		if not egg then
			return false
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Sakura.REQUEST_INSERT_EGG, egg)
		end)
		return ok and result and true or false
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while not SAE_ST.stale() do
			local flag = false
			if SAE_ST.autoBreakTree and not SAE_ST.busy and not SAE_ST.repositioning and not SAE_ST.tpHasWork() then
				local ok, result = pcall(INCUBATOR_CONFIG.breakOne)
				if not ok then
					SAE_ST.busy = false
				end
				if ok and result then
					flag = true
				end
			end
			if SAE_ST.autoSelectEgg and not SAE_ST.busy then
				pcall(INCUBATOR_CONFIG.selectEgg)
			end
			if SAE_ST.autoDeposit and not SAE_ST.busy then
				local ok, text = pcall(INCUBATOR_CONFIG.deposit)
				if ok and (text or 0) > 0 then
					SAE_ST.notify("Sakura: deposited " .. tostring(text) .. " crystals")
				end
			end
			task.wait(flag and 0.1 or 1)
		end
	end)
	local Parasite = {reach = 12}
	pcall(function()
		Parasite.Dir = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Data.MonsterParasite)
	end)
	pcall(function()
		Parasite.Net = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Remotes).MonsterParasite
	end)

	function Parasite.state()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		return save and save.MonsterParasite or nil
	end

	function Parasite.snapshot()
		if not Parasite.Net then
			return nil
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return Parasite.Net.AskSnapshot:InvokeServer()
		end)
		return ok and type(result) == "table" and result or nil
	end

	function Parasite.monster()
		local child = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild(Parasite.Dir and Parasite.Dir.WorldFolderName or "MonsterParasiteMonsters")
		if not child then
			return nil
		end
		for _, child2 in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
			if child2:IsA("Model") and child2:GetAttribute("OwnerUserId") == Core.LP.UserId and child2:GetAttribute("MonsterRetiring") ~= true then
				return child2
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	function Parasite.inventoryEggs()
		local results = {}
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		for key, info in pairs(save and save.EggInventory or {}) do
			if type(info) == "table" and info.HasParasite == true then
				table.insert(results, key)
			end
		end
		return results
	end

	function Parasite.fieldEggs()
		local results = {}
		local ok, info = pcall(function()
			return Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
		end)
		if not ok or not info then
			return results
		end
		local instance = hrp()
		local zero = instance and instance.Position or Vector3.zero
		local collectCooldown = SAE_ST.collectCooldown or {}
		local now = os.clock()
		for key, value in pairs(collectCooldown) do
			if now - value > 10 then
				collectCooldown[key] = nil
			end
		end
		for _, rec in ipairs(info.Records or {}) do
			local flag = rec.State ~= "Claimed" and rec.State ~= "Carried" and not rec.CarrierUserId
			if rec.HasParasite == true and flag and rec.BottomCFrame and not collectCooldown[rec.Uid] and not tostring(rec.Uid):find("FirstAreaEgg") then
				table.insert(results, {rec = rec, dist = (rec.BottomCFrame.Position - zero).Magnitude})
			end
		end
		table.sort(results, function(info, info2)
			return info.dist < info2.dist
		end)
		return results
	end

	function Parasite.stealOne()
		local list = Parasite.fieldEggs()
		if #list == 0 then
			return false
		end
		SAE_ST.mpeSteal = true
		SAE_ST.busy = true
		local ok, result = pcall(runSteal, list[1].rec, lookup.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed)
		SAE_ST.busy = false
		SAE_ST.mpeSteal = false
		return ok and result and true or false
	end

	function Parasite.approach()
		local character = Parasite.monster()
		if not character then
			return false
		end
		local pivot = character:GetPivot()
		local instance = hrp()
		if instance and (instance.Position - pivot.Position).Magnitude <= Parasite.reach then
			return true
		end
		local cframe = CFrame.new((pivot * CFrame.new(0, 0, 8)).Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
		flyTo(cframe, lookup.GlideSpeed or SAE_ST.tweenSpeed)
		instance = hrp()
		return instance ~= nil and (instance.Position - pivot.Position).Magnitude <= Parasite.reach + 4
	end

	function Parasite.feed(arg)
		if not Parasite.Net then
			return false, "event remote missing"
		end
		if not Parasite.monster() then
			return false, "monster not spawned"
		end
		pcall(function()
			Core.EggCmds.RequestEquipTool(arg)
		end)
		local value = os.clock() + 3
		local flag = false
		repeat
			task.wait(0.1)
			local character = getCharacter()
			for _, child in ipairs(character and character:GetChildren() or {}) do
				if child:IsA("Tool") then
					flag = true
					break
				end
			end
		until flag or os.clock() >= value
		if not flag then
			return false, "egg tool did not equip"
		end
		if not Parasite.approach() then
			return false, "could not reach the monster"
		end
		task.wait(0.35)
		local ok, response = pcall(function()
			return Parasite.Net.AskFeed:InvokeServer()
		end)
		if ok and type(response) == "table" and response.Success then
			return true, response
		end
		return false, type(response) == "table" and response.Message or "feed rejected"
	end

	function Parasite.claimChest()
		if not Parasite.Net then
			return false, "event remote missing"
		end
		Parasite.approach()
		local ok, response = pcall(function()
			return Parasite.Net.AskChestTake:InvokeServer()
		end)
		if not ok or type(response) ~= "table" or not response.Success then
			return false, type(response) == "table" and response.Message or "chest not taken"
		end
		task.wait(0.5)
		local guid = Core.HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
		local response2
		for i = 1, 3 do
			local ok2, result = pcall(function()
				return Parasite.Net.AskChestClaim:InvokeServer(guid)
			end)
			response2 = ok2 and result or nil
			if type(response2) == "table" and response2.Success then
				break
			end
			task.wait(0.4)
		end
		if type(response2) ~= "table" or not response2.Success then
			return false, type(response2) == "table" and response2.Message or "chest not opened"
		end
		pcall(function()
			Parasite.Net.AskChestRevealComplete:InvokeServer(guid)
		end)
		return true, response2
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while not SAE_ST.stale() do
			local flag = false
			if SAE_ST.mpeAutoChest and not SAE_ST.busy and not SAE_ST.repositioning then
				local info = Parasite.state()
				if info and (info.PendingChests or 0) > 0 then
					SAE_ST.busy = true
					local ok, result, info2 = pcall(Parasite.claimChest)
					SAE_ST.busy = false
					if ok and result then
						local displayName = type(info2) == "table" and info2.Reward and info2.Reward.DisplayName or "reward"
						if SAE_ST.notify then
							SAE_ST.notify("Monster Chest: " .. tostring(displayName))
						end
						flag = true
					end
				end
			end
			if SAE_ST.mpeAutoFeed and not SAE_ST.busy and not SAE_ST.repositioning then
				local list = Parasite.inventoryEggs()
				if #list > 0 then
					SAE_ST.busy = true
					local ok, result, info = pcall(Parasite.feed, list[1])
					SAE_ST.busy = false
					if ok and result then
						if SAE_ST.notify then
							SAE_ST.notify(("Monster: fed 1 egg (charge %d/100)"):format(type(info) == "table" and info.Charge or 0))
						end
						flag = true
					end
				end
			end
			if SAE_ST.mpeAutoSteal and not SAE_ST.autoSteal and not SAE_ST.tpSteal and not SAE_ST.busy and not SAE_ST.repositioning then
				if #Parasite.inventoryEggs() < (SAE_ST.mpeEggBuffer or 5) then
					local ok, result = pcall(Parasite.stealOne)
					if ok and result then
						flag = true
					end
				end
			end
			task.wait(flag and 0.25 or 2)
		end
	end)

	local function serverHop()
		local placeId = game.PlaceId
		local id
		local playing
		local nextPageCursor = ""
		for i = 1, 3 do
			local ok, info = pcall(function()
				local text = ("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"):format(placeId)
				if nextPageCursor ~= "" then
					text = text .. "&cursor=" .. nextPageCursor
				end
				return Core.HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(text))
			end)
			if not ok or not info then
				break
			end
			for _, info2 in ipairs(info.data or {}) do
				if info2.id ~= game.JobId and info2.playing and info2.playing < info2.maxPlayers then
					if not playing or info2.playing < playing then
						playing = info2.playing
						id = info2.id
					end
				end
			end
			nextPageCursor = info.nextPageCursor or ""
			if nextPageCursor == "" then
				break
			end 
		end
		if id then
			pcall(function()
				Core.TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, id, Core.LP)
			end)
			return true
		end
		return false
	end

	SAE_ST.serverHop = serverHop
	local espCache = {}
	local ESP_COLORS = {
		egg = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 160),
		eggCarried = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60),
		pet = Color3.fromRGB(140, 180, 255),
		player = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		carrier = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 80),
	}

	local function createEspBillboard(text, arg)
		local billboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		billboardGui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 34)
		billboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
		billboardGui.Adornee = nil
		billboardGui.ClipsDescendants = false
		local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", billboardGui)
		textLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
		textLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
		textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		textLabel.Text = text
		textLabel.TextColor3 = arg or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		textLabel.TextSize = 14
		textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
		textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		return billboardGui
	end

	local function updateEspMarker(name, uid, position, arg, eggCarried)
		if not SAE_ST.esp[name] then
			if SAE_ST.markers[name] and SAE_ST.markers[name][uid] then
				SAE_ST.markers[name][uid]:Destroy()
				SAE_ST.markers[name][uid] = nil
			end
			return
		end
		if not SAE_ST.espFolder then
			SAE_ST.espFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
			SAE_ST.espFolder.Name = "__SAE_ESP"
			SAE_ST.espFolder.Parent = Core.Workspace
		end
		if not SAE_ST.markers[name] then
			SAE_ST.markers[name] = {}
		end
		if not SAE_ST.markers[name][uid] then
			local part = Instance.new("Part")
			part.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 2)
			part.Transparency = 1
			part.CanCollide = false
			part.Anchored = true
			part.Name = name .. "_" .. uid
			part.Parent = SAE_ST.espFolder
			local espBillboard = createEspBillboard(arg or uid, eggCarried)
			espBillboard.Parent = part
			SAE_ST.markers[name][uid] = part
		end
		local instance = SAE_ST.markers[name][uid]
		instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
		local billboardGui = instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("BillboardGui")
		if billboardGui then
			billboardGui:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel").Text = arg or uid
		end
	end

	local function clearEspMarkers(arg)
		if not SAE_ST.markers[arg] then
			return
		end
		for _, instance in pairs(SAE_ST.markers[arg]) do
			pcall(function()
				instance:Destroy()
			end)
		end
		SAE_ST.markers[arg] = nil
	end

	local function refreshEsp()
		local name, category
		if SAE_ST.esp.egg then
			pcall(function()
				Core.EggCmds.RequestAreaEggSnapshot()
			end)
			local areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
			local seen = {}
			for _, record in ipairs(areaEggSnapshot.Records) do
				if not tostring(record.Uid):find("FirstAreaEgg") then
					local position = (record.BottomCFrame or record.BoundsCFrame).Position
					local rarityInfo, text, value = getRarityInfo(record.AssetCategory)
					local text2 = text .. " x" .. #(record.Mutations or {})
					if record.State ~= "Slot" then
						text2 = text2 .. " (" .. record.State .. ")"
						seen["c_" .. record.Uid] = true
						updateEspMarker("eggCarried", "c_" .. record.Uid, position, text2, ESP_COLORS.eggCarried)
					else
						seen[record.Uid] = true
						updateEspMarker("egg", record.Uid, position, text2, value or ESP_COLORS.egg)
					end
				end
			end
			for key in pairs(SAE_ST.markers.egg or {}) do
				if not seen[key] then
					SAE_ST.markers.egg[key]:Destroy()
					SAE_ST.markers.egg[key] = nil
				end
			end
			for key in pairs(SAE_ST.markers.eggCarried or {}) do
				if not seen[key] then
					SAE_ST.markers.eggCarried[key]:Destroy()
					SAE_ST.markers.eggCarried[key] = nil
				end
			end
		else
			clearEspMarkers("egg")
			clearEspMarkers("eggCarried")
		end
		if SAE_ST.esp.player then
			local seen = {}
			local instance = hrp()
			local seen2 = {}
			pcall(function()
				Core.EggCmds.RequestAreaEggSnapshot()
			end)
			local areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
			for _, info in ipairs(areaEggSnapshot.Records) do
				if info.State ~= "Slot" and info.CarrierUserId then
					seen2[info.CarrierUserId] = true
				end
			end
			for _, player in ipairs(Core.Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if player ~= Core.LP then
					local character = player.Character
					local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if humanoidRootPart then
						seen[player.Name] = true
						local name2 = player.DisplayName or player.Name
						local player2 = ESP_COLORS.player
						if instance then
							local floored = math.floor((humanoidRootPart.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude)
							name2 = name2 .. "  " .. tostring(floored) .. "m"
						end
						if seen2[player.UserId] then
							name2 = name2 .. " (carrier)"
							player2 = ESP_COLORS.carrier
						end
						updateEspMarker("player", player.Name, humanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0), name2, player2)
					end
				end
			end
			for key in pairs(SAE_ST.markers.player or {}) do
				if not seen[key] then
					SAE_ST.markers.player[key]:Destroy()
					SAE_ST.markers.player[key] = nil
				end
			end
		else
			clearEspMarkers("player")
		end
		if SAE_ST.esp.pet then
			local clientRenderedAssets = Core.Workspace:FindFirstChild("ClientRenderedAssets")
			local seen = {}
			if clientRenderedAssets then
				for _, child in ipairs(clientRenderedAssets:GetChildren()) do
					local basePart = child.PrimaryPart or child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
					if basePart then
						name = child.Name:match("_(.+)$") or child.Name
						category = nil
						pcall(function()
							local info = Core.AssetSer.Deserialize(Core.Save.Get().Inventory[name])
							category = info and info.Category
						end)
						seen[name] = true
						local text = category or name:sub(1, 14)
						local pet = ESP_COLORS.pet
						if category then
							local rarityInfo, value, value2 = getRarityInfo(category)
							text = value or category
							pet = value2 or pet
						end
						updateEspMarker("pet", name, basePart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), text, pet)
					end
				end
			end
			for key in pairs(SAE_ST.markers.pet or {}) do
				if not seen[key] then
					SAE_ST.markers.pet[key]:Destroy()
					SAE_ST.markers.pet[key] = nil
				end
			end
		else
			clearEspMarkers("pet")
		end
	end

	local WEBHOOK_IDENTITY = {
		name = "Wishub Monitor",
		icon = "https://media.discordapp.net/attachments/1370206994744479876/1485452679311261900/W_logo_2.png?ex=6a81149d&is=6a7fc31d&hm=52b6e693f10f64c4fa3b699195eaaeb156939d193ec254ce0f199eb66e12afc5&=&format=webp&quality=lossless&width=1020&height=1020",
	}
	local httpRequest = http_request or request or http and http.request or syn and syn.request or fluxus and fluxus.request
	local webhookQueue = {}
	local webhookWorkerRunning = false

	local function sendJson(url, method, payload)
		local json = Core.HttpService:JSONEncode(payload)
		if httpRequest then
			local result = httpRequest({Url = url, Method = method, Headers = {["Content-Type"] = "application/json"}, Body = json})
			return result
		else
			if method == "POST" then
				local ok, body = pcall(function()
					return Core.HttpService:PostAsync(url, json, Enum.HttpContentType.ApplicationJson)
				end)
				return ok and {Body = body} or nil
			end
			return nil
		end
	end

	local function startWebhookWorker()
		if webhookWorkerRunning then
			return
		end
		webhookWorkerRunning = true
		while #webhookQueue > 0 do
			local removed = table.remove(webhookQueue, 1)
			pcall(function()
				sendJson(removed.url, "POST", removed.payload)
			end)
			task.wait(0.6)
		end
		webhookWorkerRunning = false
	end

	local function parseResponseBody(response)
		local body
		if type(response) == "table" then
			body = response.Body or response.body
		else
			body = response
		end
		if type(body) == "table" then
			return body
		end
		if type(body) == "string" and body ~= "" then
			local ok, result = pcall(Core.HttpService.JSONDecode, Core.HttpService, body)
			if ok and type(result) == "table" then
				return result
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function queueWebhook(arg, title, arg2, arg3, arg4)
		local info = SAE_ST.wb[arg]
		if not info or not info.on or info.url == "" then
			return
		end
		local payload = {
			embeds = {
				{
					title = title,
					description = arg2 or "",
					color = arg3 or 5814783,
					timestamp = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
					author = {name = WEBHOOK_IDENTITY.name, icon_url = WEBHOOK_IDENTITY.icon},
					footer = {text = "Wishub Monitor", icon_url = WEBHOOK_IDENTITY.icon},
					fields = arg4 or {},
				},
			},
		}
		table.insert(webhookQueue, {url = info.url, payload = payload})
		startWebhookWorker()
	end

	local function sendSummaryWebhook()
		local summary = SAE_ST.wb.summary
		if not summary or not summary.on or summary.url == "" then
			return
		end
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local areaEggSnapshot
		pcall(function()
			areaEggSnapshot = Core.EggCmds.GetAreaEggSnapshot()
		end)
		local text = 0
		for _, info in ipairs(areaEggSnapshot and areaEggSnapshot.Records or {}) do
			if info.State == "Slot" then
				text = text + 1
			end
		end
		local text2 = 0
		for _ in pairs(save.Inventory or {}) do
			text2 = text2 + 1
		end
		local fields = {
			{name = "Money", value = tostring(math.floor(save.Money or 0)), inline = true},
			{name = "Rebirth", value = tostring(save.Rebirth or 0), inline = true},
			{name = "Base Level", value = tostring(save.BaseUpgradeLevel or 0), inline = true},
			{name = "Inventory", value = tostring(text2), inline = true},
			{name = "Equipped", value = tostring(#(save.EquippedAssets or {})), inline = true},
			{name = "Slot Eggs", value = tostring(text), inline = true},
			{name = "Collected", value = tostring(SAE_ST.stats.eggsCollected), inline = true},
			{name = "Placed", value = tostring(SAE_ST.stats.eggsPlaced), inline = true},
			{name = "Hatched", value = tostring(SAE_ST.stats.eggsHatched), inline = true},
			{name = "Sells", value = tostring(SAE_ST.stats.sells), inline = true},
			{name = "Fuses", value = tostring(SAE_ST.stats.fused), inline = true},
			{name = "Carrying", value = tostring(save.SpeedPower or 0), inline = true},
		}
		local tbl = {
			embeds = {
				{
					title = "Progress Summary",
					description = "Uptime report for " .. Core.LP.Name,
					color = 5814783,
					timestamp = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
					author = {name = WEBHOOK_IDENTITY.name, icon_url = WEBHOOK_IDENTITY.icon},
					footer = {text = "Wishub Monitor", icon_url = WEBHOOK_IDENTITY.icon},
					fields = fields,
				},
			},
		}
		if SAE_ST.wb.summaryMsgId and SAE_ST.wb.summaryUrl == summary.url then
			local ok, response = pcall(function()
				return sendJson(summary.url .. "/messages/" .. tostring(SAE_ST.wb.summaryMsgId), "PATCH", tbl)
			end)
			if not ok or response and (response.StatusCode or response.status_code or 0) == 404 then
				SAE_ST.wb.summaryMsgId = nil
				SAE_ST.wb.summaryUrl = ""
			end
		end
		if not SAE_ST.wb.summaryMsgId then
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return sendJson(summary.url, "POST", tbl)
			end)
			local responseBody = ok and parseResponseBody(result)
			if responseBody and responseBody.id then
				SAE_ST.wb.summaryMsgId = tostring(responseBody.id)
				SAE_ST.wb.summaryUrl = summary.url
			end
		end
	end

	local function maybeSendSummary(arg)
		if arg or SAE_ST.wb.summary.on then
			sendSummaryWebhook()
		end
	end

	local function countInventory()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local text = 0
		for _ in pairs(save.Inventory or {}) do
			text = text + 1
		end
		local list = {
			{name = "Pet Count", value = tostring(text), inline = true},
			{name = "Favorites", value = "n/a", inline = true},
		}
		queueWebhook("summary", "Inventory Report", "Full inventory for " .. Core.LP.Name, 5814783, list)
	end

	local function listEquippedPets()
		local save = Core.Save.Get()
		local items = {}
		for _, equippedAsset in ipairs(save.EquippedAssets or {}) do
			local info = deserializeAsset(save.Inventory and save.Inventory[equippedAsset])
			if info then
				table.insert(items, info.Category or equippedAsset)
			else
				table.insert(items, equippedAsset:sub(1, 8))
			end
		end
		local list = {
			{name = "Trail", value = tostring(save.EquippedTrail or "None"), inline = true},
			{name = "Equipped Pets", value = #items > 0 and table.concat(items, ", ") or "None", inline = false},
		}
		queueWebhook("summary", "Equipped Report", "Currently equipped for " .. Core.LP.Name, 5814783, list)
	end

	do
		local function meetsWebhookRarity(assetCategory)
			local selected = select(1, getRarityInfo(assetCategory))
			if SAE_ST.wb.minRarity == "Any" then
				return true
			end
			return selected >= (rarityRankByName[SAE_ST.wb.minRarity] or 0)
		end

		local function meetsWebhookMutations(mutations)
			return #(mutations or {}) >= SAE_ST.wb.minMutations
		end

		SAE_ST._stealCheck = {at = 0, val = false}
		SAE_ST._fullCheck = {at = 0, full = false, n = 0}
		SAE_ST.eggCap = 115
		pcall(function()
			local MAX_INVENTORY = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Types.Eggs).MAX_INVENTORY
			if type(MAX_INVENTORY) == "number" and MAX_INVENTORY > 0 then
				SAE_ST.eggCap = MAX_INVENTORY
			end
		end)

		function SAE_ST.eggStorageFull()
			local now = os.clock()
			if now - SAE_ST._fullCheck.at < 2 then
				return SAE_ST._fullCheck.full, SAE_ST._fullCheck.n
			end
			SAE_ST._fullCheck.at = now
			local save = Core.Save.Get()
			local n = 0
			for _ in pairs(save and save.EggInventory or {}) do
				n = n + 1
			end
			SAE_ST._fullCheck.n = n
			SAE_ST._fullCheck.full = n >= SAE_ST.eggCap
			if SAE_ST._fullCheck.full and now - (SAE_ST._fullNotifyAt or 0) > 30 then
				SAE_ST._fullNotifyAt = now
				if SAE_ST.notify then
					SAE_ST.notify(("Egg storage full (%d/%d) - hatch or place eggs to keep stealing"):format(n, SAE_ST.eggCap))
				end
			end
			return SAE_ST._fullCheck.full, n
		end

		function stealWorkPending()
			if not SAE_ST.autoSteal then
				return false
			end
			if getMyCarriedEgg() then
				return true
			end
			if SAE_ST.eggStorageFull() then
				return false
			end
			local now = os.clock()
