			if now - SAE_ST._stealCheck.at < 0.3 then
				return SAE_ST._stealCheck.val
			end
			SAE_ST._stealCheck.at = now
			SAE_ST._stealCheck.val = #sort() > 0
			return SAE_ST._stealCheck.val
		end

		function treadmillMayRun()
			return SAE_ST.autoTreadmill and isAlive() and not SAE_ST.busy and not SAE_ST.repositioning and not SAE_ST.placing and not SAE_ST.hatching and not stealWorkPending() and not SAE_ST.tpHasWork()
		end

		function setupTreadmillMountWatch()
			pcall(function()
				Core.Network.Fired(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Treadmills.ACTIVE_TREADMILL_EVENT):Connect(function(arg)
					if arg == nil then
						return
					end
					if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting then
						return
					end
					if not stealWorkPending() then
						return
					end
					task.spawn(function()
						task.wait(0.05)
						if SAE_ST._treadmillBoosting then
							return
						end
						if not stealWorkPending() then
							return
						end
						pcall(unequipTreadmill)
					end)
				end)
			end)
		end

		task.spawn(setupTreadmillMountWatch)
		task.spawn(function()
			local fn, instance, fn2, instance2, character, myCarriedEgg2

			local function getAutoSteal()
				return SAE_ST.autoSteal and #sort() > 0 or SAE_ST.tpHasWork()
			end

			local function fn3()
				if not SAE_ST.autoBreakTree then
					return false
				end
				local ok, info = pcall(INCUBATOR_CONFIG.state)
				if not ok or not info or not info.Unlocked then
					return false
				end
				local ok2, list = pcall(INCUBATOR_CONFIG.trees)
				return ok2 and list and #list > 0 or false
			end

			while not SAE_ST.stale() do
				if SAE_ST.autoSteal and not SAE_ST.repositioning and (isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive()) then
					if isAlive() and getMyCarriedEgg() then
						SAE_ST.busy = true
						pcall(unequipTools)
						SAE_ST.busy = false
					end
					local value = 0

					fn = function()
						return isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive()
					end

					instance = nil
					local value2 = 0

					fn2 = function(list)
						local zero = Vector3.zero
						local value = 0
						for _, info in ipairs(list) do
							local bottomCFrame = info.rec and info.rec.BottomCFrame
							if bottomCFrame then
								zero = zero + bottomCFrame.Position
								value = value + 1
							end
						end
						if value == 0 then
							return nil
						end
						return CFrame.new(zero / value + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0))
					end

					local function fn4()
						if not isAlive() then
							return nil
						end
						local list = sort()
						if not list or #list == 0 then
							return nil
						end
						if not isWallSealed() then
							return fn2(list)
						end
						local rec = list[1].rec
						if rec and rec.BottomCFrame then
							return rec.BottomCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
						end
						return fn2(list)
					end

					repeat
						if SAE_ST.autoHatch and os.clock() >= value then
							value = os.clock() + 3
							local text = hatchEggs(function()
								return not fn()
							end)
							if text > 0 then
								local stats = SAE_ST.stats
								stats.eggsHatched = stats.eggsHatched + text
								if SAE_ST.wb.hatch.on then
									queueWebhook("hatch", "Eggs Hatched", text .. " egg(s) hatched", 5814783)
								end
							end
						end
						if os.clock() >= value2 then
							value2 = os.clock() + 2
							instance2 = fn4()
							if instance2 then
								local instance3 = hrp()
								if instance3 and (instance3.Position - instance2.Position).Magnitude > 25 then
									SAE_ST.busy, SAE_ST.staging = true, true
									pcall(function()
										tweenToCFrame(instance2, SAE_ST.tweenSpeed, false)
									end)
									SAE_ST.busy, SAE_ST.staging = false, false
								end
								instance = instance2
							end
						end
						if instance and isAlive() then
							local instance3 = hrp()
							if instance3 and (instance3.Position - instance.Position).Magnitude > 8 then
								character = getCharacter()
								if character then
									pcall(function()
										character:PivotTo(instance)
									end)
								end
							end
						end
						Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
					until not SAE_ST.autoSteal or not fn()
					continue
				end
				if SAE_ST.autoSteal and not SAE_ST.repositioning and isAlive() and getMyCarriedEgg() then
					SAE_ST.busy = true
					myCarriedEgg2 = getMyCarriedEgg()
					if SAE_ST.instantSteal and SAE_ST.ITS.heldIsJunk(myCarriedEgg2) then
						pcall(function()
							if not SAE_ST.ITS.dump(myCarriedEgg2, 2) then
								SAE_ST.ITS.release(myCarriedEgg2, 2)
							end
						end)
						SAE_ST.ITS.fuelUid = nil
						SAE_ST.busy = false
						task.wait(0.1)
						continue
					end
					local ok, result = pcall(unequipTools)
					SAE_ST.busy = false
					if ok and result then
						local stats = SAE_ST.stats
						stats.eggsCollected = stats.eggsCollected + 1
					end
					task.wait(0.1)
					continue
				end
				if SAE_ST.autoSteal and fn3() then
					task.wait(0.2)
					continue
				end
				if SAE_ST.autoSteal and not SAE_ST.repositioning and not SAE_ST.busy and isAlive() and not isWallSealed() and not isPhaseEnding() and not isWallActive() and not SAE_ST.eggStorageFull() then
					clearPlayerTraps()
					if Core.entryLost then
						Core.entryLost = false
						SAE_ST._entryDone = false
					end
					if not SAE_ST._entryDone then
						if os.clock() - (SAE_ST._entryTryAt or 0) > 20 then
							SAE_ST._entryTryAt = os.clock()
							pcall(SAE_ST.registerGameplayEntry)
						end
						task.wait(0.5)
						continue
					end
					local list = sort()
					if #list > 0 then
						if SAE_ST.wantTreadmill then
							SAE_ST.wantTreadmill = false
							pcall(unequipTreadmill)
						end
						SAE_ST.busy = true
						local flag = false
						local value = 0
						local minValue = math.min(#list, 5)
						while not flag and value < minValue do
							if not SAE_ST.autoSteal or not isAlive() or isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive() then
								break
							end
							if getMyCarriedEgg() then
								break
							end
							list = sort()
							if #list == 0 then
								break
							end
							local value2
							local value3
							local rec = list[1].rec
							SAE_ST.bait.lastArea = rec.AreaId
							if SAE_ST.fastSteal ~= false then
								value2, value3 = pcall(SAE_ST.fastStealOnce, rec)
								if value2 and value3 then
									flag = true
									value = value + 1
									break
								end
							end
							if SAE_ST.bait.wanted(rec.AreaId) then
								value2, value3 = pcall(SAE_ST.bait.run, rec)
							elseif SAE_ST.instantSteal then
								value2, value3 = pcall(SAE_ST.runInstantSteal, rec)
							else
								value2, value3 = pcall(runSteal, rec)
							end
							flag = value2 and value3 and true or false
							value = value + 1
							if not flag then
								task.wait(0.2)
							end
						end
						if flag then
							local stats = SAE_ST.stats
							stats.eggsCollected = stats.eggsCollected + 1
							logEggSighting(list[1].rec)
							SAE_ST.collectCooldown[list[1].rec.Uid] = os.clock()
							if SAE_ST.wb.claim.on and meetsWebhookRarity(list[1].rec.AssetCategory) then
								local rarityInfo, value2 = getRarityInfo(list[1].rec.AssetCategory)
								queueWebhook("claim", "Egg Collected", value2 .. " (" .. tostring(list[1].rec.AssetCategory) .. ")", 5814783, {
									{name = "Rarity", value = value2, inline = true},
									{name = "Mutations", value = tostring(#(list[1].rec.Mutations or {})), inline = true},
								})
							end
						end
						SAE_ST.busy = false
						if SAE_ST.randomTiming then
							task.wait(0.4 + math.random() * 1.5)
						else
							task.wait()
						end
						continue
					end
				end
				if SAE_ST.autoHop and not getAutoSteal() and os.clock() - SAE_ST.lastHopAt > SAE_ST.hopDelay * (1 + math.random()) then
					if serverHop() then
						SAE_ST.lastHopAt = os.clock()
						task.wait(SAE_ST.hopDelay + math.random() * 2)
					else
						task.wait(1)
					end
					continue
				end
				if (SAE_ST.autoPlace or SAE_ST.autoHatch) and not getAutoSteal() then
					local text = 0
					local text2 = 0
					if SAE_ST.autoHatch then
						text2 = hatchEggs(getAutoSteal)
						local stats = SAE_ST.stats
						stats.eggsHatched = stats.eggsHatched + text2
						if text2 > 0 and SAE_ST.wb.hatch.on then
							queueWebhook("hatch", "Eggs Hatched", text2 .. " egg(s) hatched", 5814783)
						end
					end
					if SAE_ST.autoPlace and not getAutoSteal() then
						local result, value, value2 = countOwnedEggs()
						if value > 0 and result ~= SAE_ST.plotFullAt then
							SAE_ST.plotFull = false
						end
						if value > 0 and value2 > 0 then
							local position = getCFrame().Position
							local instance3 = hrp()
							local humanoid = instance3 and instance3.Parent and instance3.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
							if not SAE_ST.plotFull and instance3 and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and (instance3.Position - position).Magnitude > 25 then
								flyTo(position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), SAE_ST.tweenSpeed)
								Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
							end
							if not getAutoSteal() then
								text = placeEggs(SAE_ST.placeSelectedOnly, SAE_ST.placeNames, SAE_ST.placeRarities, getAutoSteal)
								local stats = SAE_ST.stats
								stats.eggsPlaced = stats.eggsPlaced + text
								if text > 0 and SAE_ST.wb.claim.on then
									queueWebhook("claim", "Eggs Placed", text .. " egg(s) placed on plot", 5814783)
								end
							end
						elseif value <= 0 then
							SAE_ST.plotFull = true
							SAE_ST.plotFullAt = result
						end
					end
					task.wait(text + text2 > 0 and 0.05 or 2)
					continue
				end
				if isWallSealed() or isPhaseEnding() or isWallActive() then
					task.wait(0.1)
				else
					task.wait(SAE_ST.randomTiming and 0.4 + math.random() or nil)
				end
			end
		end)
		task.spawn(function()
			local cameraSubject

	task.spawn(function()

		local function fn()
			if not coreGui then
				return 0
			end
			local value
			if pcall(function()
				value = getthreadidentity()
			end) and value then
				pcall(function()
					setthreadidentity(2)
				end)
			end

			local function fn(parent)
				if not parent then
					return false
				end
				local parent2
				local ok = pcall(function()
					parent2 = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
					parent2.Name = "__ui_probe"
					parent2.Parent = parent
					local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
					frame.Parent = parent2
					frame.Visible = false
				end)
				pcall(function()
					if parent2 then
						parent2:Destroy()
					end
				end)
				return ok
			end

			local flag = not fn(coreGui)
			local parent
			if flag then
				local playerGui
				pcall(function()
					playerGui = Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
				end)
				if playerGui and fn(playerGui) then
					parent = playerGui
				end
			end
			if value then
				pcall(function()
					setthreadidentity(value)
				end)
			end
			if not parent then
				return 0
			end
			local value2 = 0
			pcall(function()
				for _, child in ipairs(coreGui:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("ScreenGui") and not seen[child] then
						local ok = pcall(function()
							child.ResetOnSpawn = false
							child.Parent = parent
						end)
						if ok then
							value2 = value2 + 1
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			return value2
		end

		function SAE_ST.notify(lastNotify)
			if lastNotify == SAE_ST._lastNotify and os.clock() - SAE_ST._lastNotifyAt < 30 then
				return
			end
			SAE_ST._lastNotify = lastNotify
			SAE_ST._lastNotifyAt = os.clock()

			end)
		end

		local function set_thread_identity()
			if pcall(function()
				setthreadidentity(8)
			end) then

		local function fn8()
			set_thread_identity()
			for key, instance in pairs(lookup3) do
				local flag = key == text4
				instance.BackgroundColor3 = flag and tbl.accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				instance.BackgroundTransparency = flag and 0.15 or 0.88
				instance.TextColor3 = flag and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or tbl.btnText
				local UIStroke = instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
				if UIStroke then
					UIStroke.Transparency = flag and 0 or 1
				end
			end
		end

		local function sort()
			set_thread_identity()
			if not parent then
				return
			end
			for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Frame") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end
			local items = {}
			local text = 0
			for _, list in pairs(SAE_ST.eggLog) do
				table.insert(items, list)
				text = text + list.count
			end
			if text4 == "count" then
				table.sort(items, function(info, info2)
					if info.count == info2.count then
						return info.rnum > info2.rnum
					end
					return info.count > info2.count
				end)
			elseif text4 == "name" then
				table.sort(items, function(info, info2)
					return tostring(info.name) < tostring(info2.name)
				end)
			else
				table.sort(items, function(info, info2)
					if info.rnum == info2.rnum then
						return info.count > info2.count
					end
					return info.rnum > info2.rnum
				end)
			end
			for layoutOrder, info in ipairs(items) do
				local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
				frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 34)
				frame.BackgroundColor3 = tbl.surface
				frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
				frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
				frame.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
				frame.Parent = parent
				Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
				local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
				UIStroke.Color = tbl.strokeCol
				UIStroke.Thickness = 1
				UIStroke.Transparency = 0.72
				local frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
				frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -10)
				frame2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 5)
				frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
				frame2.BackgroundColor3 = info.color or tbl.accent
				frame2.Parent = frame

		local tbl5 = {}
		local savedCycleCmds = CycleCmds
		local fn10
		local directory
		local directory2
		if not savedCycleCmds then
			pcall(function()
				local AreaEggCycle = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Shared.Util.AreaEggCycle)
				savedCycleCmds = {IsNight = AreaEggCycle.IsNightPhase, GetPhaseTimeLeft = AreaEggCycle.SecondsUntilPhaseEnd}
			end)
		end
		fn10 = nil
		pcall(function()
			directory = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Data.Areas).Directory
		end)
		pcall(function()
			directory2 = require(Core.ReplicatedStorage.Data.Assets).Directory
		end)
		tbl5.ok = savedCycleCmds ~= nil and fn10 ~= nil and directory ~= nil and directory2 ~= nil
		tbl5.AREAS = {"Forest", "Lake", "Desert", "Jungle", "Snow", "Volcano", "Abyss Ocean", "Prehistoric", "Cosmic"}
		tbl5.SLOTS = {"Slot_001", "Slot_002", "Slot_003", "Slot_004", "Slot_005"}
		local lookup4 = {}

		local function fn11(arg)
			local value = lookup4[arg]
			if value ~= nil then
				return value or nil
			end
			local ok, flag = pcall(function()
				return directory2[arg]
			end)
			flag = ok and flag or false
			lookup4[arg] = flag
			return flag or nil
		end

		local lookup5 = {}

		local function fn12(map)
			if type(map) ~= "table" then
				return nil
			end
			for _, name in ipairs({"Icon", "Image", "Thumbnail", "IconId", "ImageId", "DisplayImage"}) do
				local text = map[name]
				if type(text) == "string" and text ~= "" then
					if text:find("rbxassetid") or text:find("rbxthumb") then
						return text
					end
					if text:match("^%d+$") then
						return "rbxassetid://" .. text
					end
				elseif type(text) == "number" and text > 0 then
					return "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(text)
				end
			end
			return nil
		end

		function tbl5.iconOf(petName)
			local value = lookup5[petName]
			if value ~= nil then
				return value or nil
			end
			local flag = fn12(fn11(petName)) or false
			lookup5[petName] = flag
			return flag or nil
		end

		function tbl5.rarityOf(arg)
			local info = fn11(arg)
			local rarity = info and info.Rarity
			return rarity and (rarity._id or rarity.DisplayName) or "Common", rarity and Core.rarityRank(rarity) or 1, rarity and rarity.Color or Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
		end

		function tbl5.fmt(maxValue)
			maxValue = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(maxValue) or 0))
			local floored = math.floor(maxValue / 86400)
			local floored2 = math.floor(maxValue % 86400 / 3600)
			local floored3 = math.floor(maxValue % 3600 / 60)
			local value = maxValue % 60
			if floored > 0 then
				return string.format("%dd %02dh %02dm", floored, floored2, floored3)
			elseif floored2 > 0 then
				return string.format("%dh %02dm %02ds", floored2, floored3, value)
			elseif floored3 > 0 then
				return string.format("%dm %02ds", floored3, value)
			else
				return string.format("%ds", value)
			end
		end

		function tbl5.rollAt(arg, area, slot)
			local info = directory[area]
			if not info or not info.DropTable then
				return nil
			end
			return fn10(Random.new(savedCycleCmds.GetSlotSeed(arg, area, slot)), info.DropTable)
		end

		function tbl5.forecast(arg, amount)
			if not tbl5.ok then
				return {}
			end
			arg = arg or 12
			amount = amount or 6
			local serverTime = Core.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
			local periodIndex = savedCycleCmds.GetPeriodIndex(serverTime)
			local results = {}
			for resetNumber = 1, arg do
				local value = periodIndex + resetNumber
				local rares = {}
				local jackpot = false
				for _, area in ipairs(tbl5.AREAS) do
					for _, slot in ipairs(tbl5.SLOTS) do
						local result = tbl5.rollAt(value, area, slot)
						if result then
							local text, rarityNumber, color = tbl5.rarityOf(result)
							if rarityNumber >= amount or text == "Secret" or text == "Eternal" then
								table.insert(rares, {
									petName = result,
									area = area,
									slot = slot,
									rarity = text,
									rarityNumber = rarityNumber,
									color = color,
								})
								if text == "Secret" or text == "Eternal" or rarityNumber >= 8 then
									jackpot = true
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				table.sort(rares, function(info, info2)
					if info.rarityNumber == info2.rarityNumber then
						return info.area < info2.area
					end
					return info.rarityNumber > info2.rarityNumber
				end)
				table.insert(results, {
					resetNumber = resetNumber,
					period = value,
					releaseAt = savedCycleCmds.GetPeriodStartsAt(value),
					rares = rares,
					jackpot = jackpot,
				})
			end
			return results
		end

		function tbl5.search(arg, name, arg2, amount)
			if not tbl5.ok then
				return {}
			end
			arg2 = arg2 or 8640
			amount = amount or 60
			local lowered = string.lower(arg or "")
			local value = name and name ~= "Any" and name or nil
			if lowered == "" and not value then
				return {}
			end
			local periodIndex = savedCycleCmds.GetPeriodIndex(Core.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
			local results = {}
			for resetNumber = 1, arg2 do
				local value2 = periodIndex + resetNumber
				for _, area in ipairs(tbl5.AREAS) do
					for _, slot in ipairs(tbl5.SLOTS) do
						local text = tbl5.rollAt(value2, area, slot)
						if text and (lowered == "" or string.find(string.lower(tostring(text)), lowered, 1, true)) then
							local result, rarityNumber, color = tbl5.rarityOf(text)
							if not value or result == value then
								table.insert(results, {
									petName = text,
									area = area,
									slot = slot,
									rarity = result,
									rarityNumber = rarityNumber,
									color = color,
									resetNumber = resetNumber,
									period = value2,
									releaseAt = savedCycleCmds.GetPeriodStartsAt(value2),
								})
								if #results >= amount then
									return results
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				if resetNumber % 400 == 0 then
					task.wait()
				end
			end
			return results
		end

		function tbl5.status()
			if not tbl5.ok then
				return {period = 0, resetIn = 0, isNight = false, nextClock = "--"}
			end
			local serverTime = Core.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
			return {
				period = savedCycleCmds.GetPeriodIndex(serverTime),
				resetIn = savedCycleCmds.GetTimeLeft(serverTime),
				isNight = savedCycleCmds.IsNight(serverTime) and true or false,
				nextClock = os.date("!%H:%M:%S UTC", savedCycleCmds.GetNextResetAt(serverTime)),
			}
		end

		local text5 = ""
		local text6 = "Any"
		local flag2 = false
		local lookup6 = {}
		local textLabel4

		local function fn13(color)
			color = color or Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
			return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", math.clamp(math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), 0, 255), math.clamp(math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), 0, 255), math.clamp(math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5), 0, 255))
		end

		local function fn14()
			set_thread_identity()
			if not textLabel2 then
				return nil
			end
			if not tbl5.ok then
				textLabel2.Text = "<font color='#ff6a6a'>Predictor unavailable (module load failed)</font>"
				return nil
			end
			local info = tbl5.status()
			local isNight = info.isNight and "#ff5c5c" or (info.resetIn <= 20 and "#ffb020" or "#4dff9f")
			textLabel2.Text = string.format("<b>Phase:</b> <font color='%s'>%s</font>   <b>Next:</b> <font color='%s'>%s</font> <font color='#8f8da0'>(%s / #%d)</font>", isNight, info.isNight and "Night" or "Day", isNight, tbl5.fmt(info.resetIn), info.nextClock, info.period)
			return info
		end

				local UIStroke = instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
				if UIStroke then
					UIStroke.Transparency = flag and 0 or 1
				end
			end
		end

		local fn17

		fn17 = function()
			set_thread_identity()
			if not parent2 then
				return
			end
			for _, child in ipairs(parent2:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Frame") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end
			tbl4 = {}
			local info = fn14()
			if not tbl5.ok then
				return
			end
			period = info and info.period or nil
			local serverTime = Core.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
			if text5 ~= "" or text6 ~= "Any" then
				local text = text5 ~= "" and text5 or "any"
				local text2 = text6 ~= "Any" and " / " .. text6 or ""
				if textLabel4 then
					textLabel4.Text = string.format("<font color='#8f8da0'>Searching 30d for</font> <b>%s</b>%s...", text, text2)
				end
				flag2 = true
				local ok, tbl2 = pcall(tbl5.search, text5, text6, 8640, 60)
				flag2 = false
				if not ok then
					tbl2 = {}
				end
				if not parent2 then
					return
				end
				serverTime = Core.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
				for index, value in ipairs(tbl2) do
					table.insert(tbl4, fn16(parent2, index, value, serverTime))
				end
				if textLabel4 then
					if #tbl2 == 0 then
						textLabel4.Text = string.format("<font color='#ff9c6a'>No match in the next 30 days for</font> <b>%s</b>%s", text, text2)
					else
						textLabel4.Text = string.format("<font color='#4dff9f'><b>%d</b></font> <font color='#8f8da0'>hit(s), soonest in</font> <b>%s</b>", #tbl2, tbl5.fmt(tbl2[1].releaseAt - serverTime))
					end
				end
				return
			end
			if textLabel4 then
				textLabel4.Text = "<font color='#8f8da0'>Next 12 resets, Mythic+ / Secret / Eternal. Type a name or pick a rarity to search 30d.</font>"
			end
			local list = tbl5.forecast(12, 6)
			if #list == 0 then
				local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
				frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 30)
				frame.BackgroundColor3 = tbl.surface
				frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
				frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
				frame.LayoutOrder = 1
				frame.Parent = parent2
				Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
				local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

		end

		local function fn19()
			set_thread_identity()
			if not tbl3 then
				return
			end
			for key, instance in pairs(tbl3) do
				local flag = key == text
				instance.BackgroundColor3 = flag and tbl.accent or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				instance.BackgroundTransparency = flag and 0.15 or 0.88
				instance.TextColor3 = flag and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or tbl.btnText
				local UIStroke = instance:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
				if UIStroke then
					UIStroke.Transparency = flag and 0 or 1
				end
			end
		end

		local function fn20(name)
			set_thread_identity()
			text = name
			if tbl2 then
				for key, value in pairs(tbl2) do
					value.Visible = key == name
				end
			end
			fn19()
			if name == "logs" then
				sort()
			elseif name == "place" then
				fn2()
			elseif name == "pets" then
				fn3()
			elseif name == "predict" then
				fn7()
			end
		end

		local function getCoreGui()
			local parent
			local value
			if pcall(function()
				value = getthreadidentity()
			end) and value then
				pcall(function()
					setthreadidentity(2)
				end)
			end
			local items = {}
			pcall(function()
				if gethui then
					table.insert(items, gethui())
				end
			end)
			table.insert(items, Core.CoreGui)
			pcall(function()
				table.insert(items, Core.LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"))
			end)
			local value2
			for _, parent2 in ipairs(items) do
				if parent2 and not value2 then
					parent = nil
					local ok = pcall(function()
						parent = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
						parent.Name = "__wis_probe"
						parent.Parent = parent2
						local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
						frame.Parent = parent
						frame.Visible = false
					end)
					pcall(function()
						if parent then
							parent:Destroy()
						end
					end)
					if ok then
						value2 = parent2
					end
				end
			end
			if value then
				pcall(function()
					setthreadidentity(value)
				end)
			end
			return value2 or Core.CoreGui
		end

		local tbl6 = {moneys = "Money/s", kg = "Kg", rarity = "Rarity", name = "Name"}
		local lookup8 = {}

		local function fn24(moneys)
			if not moneys or moneys ~= moneys then
				return "0"
			end
			if moneys >= 1000000000000 then
				return ("%.2fT"):format(moneys / 1000000000000)
			elseif moneys >= 1000000000 then
				return ("%.2fB"):format(moneys / 1000000000)
			elseif moneys >= 1000000 then
				return ("%.2fM"):format(moneys / 1000000)
			elseif moneys >= 1000 then
				return ("%.2fK"):format(moneys / 1000)
			else
				return ("%d"):format(moneys)
			end
		end

		local function fn25()

				end
			end
		end

				local category = inventory.AssetCategory or inventory.Category

					end
				end
			else
				for _, info in pairs(save.Inventory or {}) do
					if type(info) == "table" and (info.AssetCategory or info.Category) then
						fn(info)
					end
				end
			end
			local value2 = text3 == "rarity" and "rnum" or text3

				UIStroke.Color = tbl.strokeCol
				UIStroke.Thickness = 1
				UIStroke.Transparency = 0.72
				local frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
				frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -10)
				frame2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 5)
				frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
				frame2.BackgroundColor3 = info.color or tbl.accent
				frame2.Parent = frame
				Instance.new("UICorner", frame2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

				imageLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 5)
				imageLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24)
				imageLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1

		local function sort2()
			local results = {}
			local seen = {}
			for _, child in ipairs(Core.LP.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Tool") and child:GetAttribute("ItemType") == "Asset" then
					local name = child:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or child.Name
					if not seen[name] then
						seen[name] = true
						table.insert(results, name)
					end
				end
			end
			table.sort(results)
			return results
		end

		local function sort4()
			local results = {}
			for _, player in ipairs(Core.Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if player ~= Core.LP and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and player.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("GiftPrompt") then
					table.insert(results, player.Name)
				end
			end
			table.sort(results)
			return results
		end

		local function fn27()
			if SAE_ST.giftToolMode == "heaviest" then
				local value
				local value2
				for _, child in ipairs(Core.LP.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") and child:GetAttribute("ItemType") == "Asset" then
						local weight = child:GetAttribute("Weight") or 0
						if not value2 or weight > value2 then
							value, value2 = child, weight
						end
					end
				end
				return value
			end
			if SAE_ST.giftToolMode == "selected" and SAE_ST.giftToolSelected ~= "" then
				for _, child in ipairs(Core.LP.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") and child:GetAttribute("ItemType") == "Asset" and (child:GetAttribute("DisplayName") or child.Name) == SAE_ST.giftToolSelected then
						return child
					end
				end
				return nil
			end
			for _, child in ipairs(Core.LP.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Tool") and child:GetAttribute("ItemType") == "Asset" then
					return child
				end
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function fn28()
			local results = {}
			for _, player in ipairs(Core.Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if player ~= Core.LP and player.Character then
					local humanoidRootPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					local giftPrompt = humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("GiftPrompt")
					if giftPrompt then
						if SAE_ST.giftTargetMode == "all" or player.Name == SAE_ST.giftTargetSelected then
							table.insert(results, player)
						end
					end
				end
			end
			return results
		end

		local function equipTool(item2, item)
			local instance = hrp()
			if not instance then
				return false, "no hrp"
			end
			local humanoidRootPart = item2.Character and item2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not humanoidRootPart then
				return false, "no target root"
			end
			local giftPrompt = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("GiftPrompt")
			if not giftPrompt then
				return false, "no gift prompt"
			end
			local humanoid = getCharacter():FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if not humanoid then
				return false, "no humanoid"
			end
			local name = item.Name
			local position = humanoidRootPart.Position
			local vector2 = (instance.Position - position) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
			if vector2.Magnitude < 0.1 then
				vector2 = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
			end
			vector2 = vector2.Unit
			local value = position + vector2 * 4
			flyTo(value + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), SAE_ST.tweenSpeed)
			instance = hrp()
			pcall(function()
				humanoid:EquipTool(item)
			end)
			local value2 = 0
			while not giftPrompt.Enabled and value2 < 50 do
				task.wait(0.1)
				value2 = value2 + 1
			end
			if not giftPrompt.Enabled then
				return false, "prompt not enabled"
			end
			local now = os.clock()
			local connection = Core.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if SAE_ST.stale() then
					return
				end
				local now2 = os.clock()
				local minValue = math.min(now2 - now, 0.1)
				now = now2
				local humanoidRootPart = item2.Character and item2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if humanoidRootPart and hrp() == instance then
					local savedVector2 = (instance.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position) * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
					if savedVector2.Magnitude < 0.1 then
						savedVector2 = vector2
					end
					savedVector2 = savedVector2.Unit
					local value = humanoidRootPart.Position + savedVector2 * 4 + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
					local distance = (instance.Position - value).Magnitude
					if distance > 0.5 then
						local value2 = math.min(SAE_ST.tweenSpeed, DEFAULT_FPS_CAP) * minValue
						local value3 = instance.Position + (value - instance.Position).Unit * math.min(value2, distance)
						local vector3 = value3 - instance.Position
						local magnitude = vector3.Magnitude
						if magnitude > 0.001 and minValue > 0 then
							instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vector3 / minValue
						else
							instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						end
						instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(value3)
					else
						instance.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end
				end
			end)
			task.wait(0.3)
			task.wait((giftPrompt.HoldDuration or 1) + 0.8)
			local message = Core.LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Message")
			for i = 1, 40 do
				local yes = message and message.Frame.Contents.Yes
				if yes and yes.Visible then
					break
				end
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
			task.wait(0.3)
			connection:Disconnect()
			local flag = false
			for i = 1, 25 do
				flag = true
				for _, child in ipairs(Core.LP.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
					if child == item then
						flag = false
						break
					end
				end
				if flag then
					local character = getCharacter()
					if character then
						for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
							if child == item then
								flag = false
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
				if flag then
					break
				end
				task.wait(0.2)
			end
			if flag then
				return true, name
			end
			return false, "still held"
		end

		local toggle
		local toggle2

		local function notify2()
			while SAE_ST.autoGift do
				local list = fn28()
				if #list == 0 then
					SAE_ST.notify("Auto Gift: no players with GiftPrompt nearby")
					pcall(function()
						toggle:Set(false)
					end)
					break
				end
				if SAE_ST.giftToolMode == "all" then
					local items = {}
					for _, child in ipairs(Core.LP.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Tool") and child:GetAttribute("ItemType") == "Asset" then
							table.insert(items, child)
						end
					end
					if #items == 0 then
						SAE_ST.notify("Auto Gift: no pets in backpack")
						pcall(function()
							toggle:Set(false)
						end)
						break
					end
					for _, item in ipairs(items) do
						if not SAE_ST.autoGift then
							break
						end
						for _, item2 in ipairs(list) do
							if not SAE_ST.autoGift then
								break
							end
							local result, text = equipTool(item2, item)
							if result then
								SAE_ST.notify("Gifted " .. text .. " to " .. item2.Name)
								queueWebhook("autoGift", "Auto Gift", "Gifted " .. text .. " to " .. item2.Name, 5814783)
							end
							task.wait(0.4)
						end
					end
				else
					local result = fn27()
					if not result then
						SAE_ST.notify("Auto Gift: selected pet already gifted or no pets in backpack")
						pcall(function()
							toggle:Set(false)
						end)
						break
					end
					for _, item in ipairs(list) do
						if not SAE_ST.autoGift then
							break
						end
						local result2, text = equipTool(item, result)
						if result2 then
							SAE_ST.notify("Gifted " .. text .. " to " .. item.Name)
							queueWebhook("autoGift", "Auto Gift", "Gifted " .. text .. " to " .. item.Name, 5814783)
						end
						task.wait(0.4)
					end
				end
				task.wait(0.3)
			end
		end

		local connection

		local function notify3()
			if connection then
				pcall(function()
					connection:Disconnect()
				end)
				connection = nil
			end
			local ok, signal = pcall(function()
				return Core.Network.Fired(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Gifting.GIFT_REQUEST)
			end)
			if not ok then
				SAE_ST.notify("Auto Claim: cannot hook gift requests")
				return
			end
			connection = signal:Connect(function(...)
				local list = {...}
				task.spawn(function()
					local value = list[2]
					local value2 = list[4]
					task.wait(0.1)
					local giftNotification = Core.LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("GiftNotification")
					local frame = giftNotification and giftNotification:FindFirstChild("Frame")
					local flag = false
					if frame then
						for _, child in ipairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
							local holder = child:FindFirstChild("Holder")
							local accept = holder and holder:FindFirstChild("Frame") and holder.Frame:FindFirstChild("Accept")
							if accept then
								flag = true
							end
						end
					end
					if not flag and value and value2 then
						pcall(function()
							Core.Network.Invoke(Core.Network.NET_MAP.Gifting.RESPONSE, value, value2, true)
						end)
						if frame then
							for _, child in ipairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
								if child:FindFirstChild("Holder") then
									pcall(function()
										child:Destroy()
									end)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end)
		end

			end,
		})
		section12:AddButton({
			Title = "Feed One Egg Now",
			Content = "Feed a single infested egg to the monster right away.",
			Callback = function()
				task.spawn(function()
					if SAE_ST.busy then
						SAE_ST.notify("Monster: busy")
						return
					end
					local list = Parasite.inventoryEggs()
					if #list == 0 then
						SAE_ST.notify("Monster: no infested egg in stock")
						return
					end
					SAE_ST.busy = true
					local ok, result, info = pcall(Parasite.feed, list[1])
					SAE_ST.busy = false
					if ok and result then
						SAE_ST.notify(("Monster: fed 1 egg (charge %d/100)"):format(type(info) == "table" and info.Charge or 0))
					else
						SAE_ST.notify("Monster: " .. tostring(info or "feed failed"))
					end
				end)
			end,
		})
		section12:AddButton({
			Title = "Claim Chest Now",
			Content = "Take and open a pending Monster Chest.",
			Callback = function()
				task.spawn(function()
					if SAE_ST.busy then
						SAE_ST.notify("Monster: busy")
						return
					end
					SAE_ST.busy = true
					local ok, result, info = pcall(Parasite.claimChest)
					SAE_ST.busy = false
					if ok and result then
						local displayName = type(info) == "table" and info.Reward and info.Reward.DisplayName or "reward"
						SAE_ST.notify("Monster Chest: " .. tostring(displayName))
					else
						SAE_ST.notify("Monster: " .. tostring(info or "chest failed"))
					end
				end)
			end,
		})
		task.spawn(function()
			while not SAE_ST.stale() do
				local ok = pcall(function()
					local tbl = Parasite.state() or {}
					local charge = tbl.Charge or 0
					local pendingChests = tbl.PendingChests or 0
					local totalFeeds = tbl.TotalFeeds or 0
					local count = #Parasite.inventoryEggs()
					local count2 = #Parasite.fieldEggs()
					local endsAt = Parasite.Dir and Parasite.Dir.EndsAt or 0
					local maxValue = math.max(endsAt - Core.Workspace:GetServerTimeNow(), 0)
					local floored = math.floor(maxValue / 86400)
					local floored2 = math.floor(maxValue % 86400 / 3600)
					paragraph:Set({
						Title = "Event Status",
						Content = ("Charge %d/100  |  Chests ready %d  |  Total feeds %d\n"):format(charge, pendingChests, totalFeeds) .. ("Infested eggs: %d banked, %d on the map\n"):format(count, count2) .. (maxValue > 0 and ("Event ends in %dd %dh"):format(floored, floored2) or "Event has ended"),
					})
				end)
				if not ok then
					break
				end
				task.wait(3)
			end
		end)

if not game:IsLoaded() then
	game.Loaded:Wait()
end
if _G.StandaloneAntiAFK and type(_G.StandaloneAntiAFK.Stop) == "function" then
	pcall(_G.StandaloneAntiAFK.Stop)
end
do
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
	local antiAfkPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer or Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
	_G.__SAE_AAF_GEN = _G.__SAE_GEN
	local antiAfkState = {antiAfk = true, connections = {}, loops = {}}

	local function notify(arg, arg2)
		pcall(function()
			StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = arg or "Anti-AFK", Text = arg2 or "Active", Duration = 3})
		end)
	end

	local function disableIdleConnections()
		pcall(function()
			local raw = rawget(_G, "getconnections") or rawget(_G, "get_signal_cons") or type(getconnections) == "function" and getconnections
			if raw then
				for _, connection in pairs(raw(antiAfkPlayer.Idled)) do 
					if connection.Disable then
						pcall(function()
							connection:Disable()
						end)
					elseif connection.Disconnect then
						pcall(function()
							connection:Disconnect()
						end)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function setAntiAfk(antiAfk)
		antiAfkState.antiAfk = antiAfk
		if antiAfkState.antiAfk then
			pcall(function()
				antiAfkPlayer:SetAttribute("AntiAfkIdleOverride", 99999999)
			end)
			disableIdleConnections()
		else
			pcall(function()
				antiAfkPlayer:SetAttribute("AntiAfkIdleOverride", nil)
			end)
		end
	end

	setAntiAfk(true)
	disableIdleConnections()
	table.insert(antiAfkState.connections, antiAfkPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
		if antiAfkState.antiAfk then
			__AAF_simulateActivity()
		end
	end))
	table.insert(antiAfkState.connections, antiAfkPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(1)
		if antiAfkState.antiAfk then
			pcall(function()
				antiAfkPlayer:SetAttribute("AntiAfkIdleOverride", 99999999)
			end)
			disableIdleConnections()
		end
	end))
	table.insert(antiAfkState.loops, task.spawn(function()
		while _G.__SAE_GEN == _G.__SAE_AAF_GEN do
			task.wait(60)
			if antiAfkState.antiAfk then
				__AAF_simulateActivity()
				disableIdleConnections()
			end
		end
	end))
	_G.StandaloneAntiAFK = {
		State = antiAfkState,
		Enable = function()
			setAntiAfk(true)
			notify("Anti-AFK", "Enabled")
		end,
		Disable = function()
			setAntiAfk(false)
			notify("Anti-AFK", "Disabled")
		end,
		Trigger = function()
			__AAF_simulateActivity()
		end,
		Stop = function()
			setAntiAfk(false)
			for _, connection in ipairs(antiAfkState.connections) do
				pcall(function()
					if connection and connection.Connected then
						connection:Disconnect()
					end
				end)
			end
			antiAfkState.connections = {}
			for _, loop in ipairs(antiAfkState.loops) do
				pcall(function()
					if loop and coroutine.status(loop) ~= "dead" then
						task.cancel(loop)
					end
				end)
			end
			antiAfkState.loops = {}
		end,
	}
end
if type(getgenv) == "function" then
	getgenv().StandaloneAntiAFK = _G.StandaloneAntiAFK 
end
